<div class="col-sm order-1">
      <h2 class="chapter_title">CHAPTER 356. RETIREMENT SYSTEMS, GENERALLY</h2>
      
      <div class="section" id="stat.356.001">
        <h1 class="shn">356.001 PURPOSE OF PUBLIC PLANS.</h1>
        <div class="subd" id="stat.356.001.1">
          <a title="Link to Subdivision 1." class="permalink" href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/statutes/2024/cite/356/full#stat.356.001.1">§</a>
          <h2 class="subd_no">Subdivision 1.<span class="headnote">Exclusive benefit of members and beneficiaries.</span></h2>
          <p>(a) The public plans and funds specified in subdivision 4 are established to provide for the retirement of their members and to provide funds for the beneficiaries of members in the event of death of a member.</p>
          <p>(b) The public plans and funds are established and must be maintained for the exclusive benefit of the members and the beneficiaries of the members. Except as provided in subdivisions 2 and 3, no part of the moneys of the plans and funds may revert to the plan or fund or be used for or diverted to purposes other than the exclusive benefit of the members or their beneficiaries.</p>
        </div>
        <div class="subd" id="stat.356.001.2">
          <a title="Link to Subd. 2." class="permalink" href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/statutes/2024/cite/356/full#stat.356.001.2">§</a>
          <h2 class="subd_no">Subd. 2.<span class="headnote">Allowable expenses.</span></h2>
          <p>The necessary, reasonable, and direct expenses of maintaining, protecting, and administering the public plan or fund, as authorized in the laws governing the plan or fund, must be considered as expenditures for the exclusive benefit of the members or their beneficiaries.</p>
        </div>
        <div class="subd" id="stat.356.001.3">
          <a title="Link to Subd. 3." class="permalink" href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/statutes/2024/cite/356/full#stat.356.001.3">§</a>
          <h2 class="subd_no">Subd. 3.<span class="headnote">Effect of amendments or termination.</span></h2>
          <p>(a) If a public plan or fund defined in subdivision 4 is terminated or the plan or fund provisions are amended, no part of the moneys held in the plan or fund may be used for or diverted to any purpose other than the exclusive benefit of the members or their beneficiaries, except as provided in this subdivision.</p>
          <p>(b) If a plan or fund is terminated, all affected members have a nonforfeitable interest in their benefits that were accrued and funded to date. The value of the accrued benefits to be credited to the account of each affected member must be calculated as of the date of termination and the funding ratio of the plan or fund must be applied to the accrued benefit of each affected member.</p>
          <p>(c) The board of trustees of the plan or fund shall, as soon as administratively feasible following the termination, pay each eligible member or beneficiary on behalf of a member the amount in the member's account in a lump sum. In the case of a member whose whereabouts is unknown, the board shall notify the member at the last known address by certified mail with return receipt requested advising the member of the member's right to a pending distribution. If the member cannot be located in this manner, the board shall establish a custodial account for the member's benefit in a federally insured bank, savings association, or credit union in which the member's account balance must be deposited. If the board receives proof of death of a member that is satisfactory to the board, the account balance must be paid to the beneficiary of the member.</p>
        </div>
        <div class="subd" id="stat.356.001.4">
          <a title="Link to Subd. 4." class="permalink" href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/statutes/2024/cite/356/full#stat.356.001.4">§</a>
          <h2 class="subd_no">Subd. 4.<span class="headnote">Covered plans and funds.</span></h2>
          <p>This section applies to all public pension and retirement plans and funds established under the laws of the state of Minnesota that receive contributions from moneys derived from taxation.</p>
        </div>
        <div class="subd" id="stat.356.001.5">
          <a title="Link to Subd. 5." class="permalink" href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/statutes/2024/cite/356/full#stat.356.001.5">§</a>
          <h2 class="subd_no">Subd. 5.<span class="headnote">Construction.</span></h2>
          <p>Nothing contained in this section may be construed to authorize, or otherwise imply, a legislative policy or intent favoring the termination of any plan or fund to which this section applies.</p>
        </div>
      </div>
      <div class="history" id="stat.356.001.history
			">
        <h2>History: </h2>
        <p class="first"><a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/laws/1983/0/286/#laws.0.23.0">1983 c 286 s 23</a>; <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/laws/1995/0/202/#laws.1.25.0">1995 c 202 art 1 s 25</a>; <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/laws/2002/0/392/#laws.11.1.0">2002 c 392 art 11 s 1</a></p>
      </div>

      <div class="section" id="stat.356.195">
        <h1 class="shn">356.195 SERVICE CREDIT PURCHASE PROCEDURES FOR STRIKE PERIODS.</h1>
        <div class="subd" id="stat.356.195.1">
          <a title="Link to Subdivision 1." class="permalink" href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/statutes/2024/cite/356/full#stat.356.195.1">§</a>
          <h2 class="subd_no">Subdivision 1.<span class="headnote">Covered plans.</span></h2>
          <p class="in">This section applies to all defined benefit plans specified in section <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/statutes/cite/356.30#stat.356.30.3">356.30, subdivision 3</a>, except clause (9).</p>
        </div>
        <div class="subd" id="stat.356.195.2">
          <a title="Link to Subd. 2." class="permalink" href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/statutes/2024/cite/356/full#stat.356.195.2">§</a>
          <h2 class="subd_no">Subd. 2.<span class="headnote">Purchase procedure for strike periods.</span></h2>
          <p>(a) An employee covered by a plan specified in subdivision 1 may purchase allowable service credit in the applicable plan for any period of time during which the employee was on a public employee strike without pay, not to exceed a period of one year, if the employee makes a payment in lieu of salary deductions as specified in paragraph (b) or (c), whichever applies. The employing unit, at its option, may pay the employer portion of the amount specified in paragraph (b) on behalf of its employees.</p>
          <p>(b) If payment is received by the applicable pension plan executive director within one year from the end of the strike, the payment amount is equal to the applicable employee and employer contribution rates specified in law for the applicable plan during the strike period, applied to the employee's rate of salary in effect at the conclusion of the strike for the period of the strike without pay, plus compound interest at the applicable monthly rate or rates specified in section <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/statutes/cite/356.59">356.59</a>, subdivision 2, 3, 4, or 5, whichever applies, from the last day of the strike period until the date payment is received.</p>
          <p>(c) If payment is received by the applicable pension fund director after one year and before five years from the end of the strike, the payment amount is the amount determined under section <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/statutes/cite/356.551">356.551</a>.</p>
          <p>(d) Payments may not be made more than five years after the end of the strike.</p>
        </div>
      </div>
      <div class="history" id="stat.356.195.history
			">
        <h2>History: </h2>
        <p class="first"><a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/laws/2005/1/8/#laws.2.1.0">1Sp2005 c 8 art 2 s 1</a>; <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/laws/2007/0/134/#laws.2.42.0">2007 c 134 art 2 s 42</a>; <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/laws/2014/0/275/#laws.2.25.0">2014 c 275 art 2 s 25</a>; <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/laws/2015/0/68/#laws.3.13.0">2015 c 68 art 3 s 13</a>; <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/laws/2018/0/211/#laws.6.35.0">2018 c 211 art 6 s 35</a></p>
      </div>
      <h1 class="fgh">PUBLIC PENSION PLAN ACTUARIAL, FINANCIAL,<br>AND INVESTMENT REPORTING</h1>
      <div class="section" id="stat.356.20">
        <h1 class="shn">356.20 PUBLIC PENSION FUND FINANCIAL REPORTING REQUIREMENT.</h1>
        <div class="subd" id="stat.356.20.1">
          <a title="Link to Subdivision 1." class="permalink" href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/statutes/2024/cite/356/full#stat.356.20.1">§</a>
          <h2 class="subd_no">Subdivision 1.<span class="headnote">Report required.</span></h2>
          <p class="in">(a) The governing or managing board or the chief administrative officer of each public pension and retirement plan enumerated in subdivision 2 shall annually prepare and file a financial report following the close of each fiscal year.</p>
          <p class="in">(b) This requirement also applies to any plan or fund which may be a successor to any organization so enumerated or to any newly formed retirement plan, fund or association operating under the control or supervision of any public employee group, governmental unit, or institution receiving a portion of its support through legislative appropriations.</p>
          <p class="in">(c) The report must be prepared under the supervision and at the direction of the management of each plan and must be signed by the presiding officer of the managing board of the plan and the chief administrative official of the plan.</p>
        </div>
        <div class="subd" id="stat.356.20.2">
          <a title="Link to Subd. 2." class="permalink" href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/statutes/2024/cite/356/full#stat.356.20.2">§</a>
          <h2 class="subd_no">Subd. 2.<span class="headnote">Covered public pension plans and funds.</span></h2>
          <p class="in">This section applies to the following public pension plans:</p>
          <p class="in">(1) the general state employees retirement plan of the Minnesota State Retirement System;</p>
          <p class="in">(2) the general employees retirement plan of the Public Employees Retirement Association;</p>
          <p class="in">(3) the Teachers Retirement Association;</p>
          <p class="in">(4) the State Patrol retirement plan;</p>
          <p class="in">(5) the St. Paul Teachers Retirement Fund Association;</p>
          <p class="in">(6) the University of Minnesota faculty retirement plan;</p>
          <p class="in">(7) the University of Minnesota faculty supplemental retirement plan;</p>
          <p class="in">(8) the judges retirement fund;</p>
          <p class="in">(9) the Bloomington Fire Department Relief Association;</p>
          <p class="in">(10) a firefighters relief association governed by section <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/statutes/cite/424A.091">424A.091</a>;</p>
          <p class="in">(11) the public employees police and fire plan of the Public Employees Retirement Association;</p>
          <p class="in">(12) the correctional state employees retirement plan of the Minnesota State Retirement System;</p>
          <p class="in">(13) the local government correctional service retirement plan of the Public Employees Retirement Association; and</p>
          <p>(14) the statewide volunteer firefighter plan.</p>
        </div>
        <div class="subd" id="stat.356.20.3">
          <a title="Link to Subd. 3." class="permalink" href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/statutes/2024/cite/356/full#stat.356.20.3">§</a>
          <h2 class="subd_no">Subd. 3.<span class="headnote">Filing requirement.</span></h2>
          <p class="in">The financial report is a public record. A copy of the report or a synopsis of the report containing the information required by this section must be made available annually to each member of the fund and to the governing body of each governmental subdivision of the state which makes employers contributions thereto or in whose behalf taxes are levied for the employers' contribution. A signed copy of the report must be delivered to the executive director of the Legislative Commission on Pensions and Retirement and to the Legislative Reference Library not later than six months after the close of each fiscal year or one month following the completion and delivery to the retirement fund of the actuarial valuation report of the fund by the actuary retained under section <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/statutes/cite/356.214">356.214</a>, if applicable, whichever is later.</p>
        </div>
        <div class="subd" id="stat.356.20.4">
          <a title="Link to Subd. 4." class="permalink" href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/statutes/2024/cite/356/full#stat.356.20.4">§</a>
          <h2 class="subd_no">Subd. 4.<span class="headnote">Contents of financial report.</span></h2>
          <p class="in">(a) The financial report required by this section must contain financial statements and disclosures that indicate the financial operations and position of the retirement plan and fund. The report must conform with generally accepted governmental accounting principles, applied on a consistent basis. The report must be audited.</p>
          <p class="in">(b) The report must include a statement that the actuarial valuation calculations prepared by the actuary retained under section <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/statutes/cite/356.214">356.214</a> or by the actuary retained by the retirement fund or plan, whichever applies, comply with applicable actuarial requirements enumerated in section <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/statutes/cite/356.215">356.215</a>, and specified in the most recent standards for actuarial work adopted by the Legislative Commission on Pensions and Retirement. The actuarial value of assets, the actuarial accrued liabilities, and the unfunded actuarial accrued liability of the fund or plan must be disclosed. The report must contain a certification by the actuary retained under section <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/statutes/cite/356.214">356.214</a> or the actuary retained by the fund or plan, whichever applies, specifying that normal cost and the actuarial accrued liabilities for all benefits are computed in accordance with the entry age actuarial cost method and in accordance with the most recent applicable standards for actuarial work adopted by the Legislative Commission on Pensions and Retirement.</p>
          <p class="in">(c) The report must contain an itemized exhibit describing the administrative expenses of the plan, including, but not limited to, the following items, classified on a consistent basis from year to year, and with any further meaningful detail:</p>
          <p class="in">(1) personnel expenses;</p>
          <p class="in">(2) communication-related expenses;</p>
          <p class="in">(3) office building and maintenance expenses;</p>
          <p class="in">(4) professional services fees; and</p>
          <p class="in">(5) other expenses.</p>
          <p class="in">(d) The report must contain an itemized exhibit describing the investment expenses of the plan, including, but not limited to, the following items, classified on a consistent basis from year to year, and with any further meaningful detail:</p>
          <p class="in">(1) internal investment-related expenses; and</p>
          <p class="in">(2) external investment-related expenses.</p>
          <p class="in">(e) Any additional statements or exhibits or more detailed or subdivided itemization of a disclosure item that will enable the management of the plan to portray a true interpretation of the plan's financial condition must be included in the additional statements or exhibits.</p>
        </div>
        <div class="subd" id="stat.356.20.4a">
          <a title="Link to Subd. 4a." class="permalink" href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/statutes/2024/cite/356/full#stat.356.20.4a">§</a>
          <h2 class="subd_no">Subd. 4a.<span class="headnote">Financial report for police or firefighters relief association.</span></h2>
          <p class="in">For any police or firefighters relief association referred to in subdivision 2, clause (10) or (11), a financial report that is duly filed and that meets the requirements of section <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/statutes/cite/424A.014">424A.014 </a>is deemed to have met the requirements of subdivision 4.</p>
        </div>
        <div class="subd" id="stat.356.20.5">
          <a title="Link to Subd. 5." class="permalink" href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/statutes/2024/cite/356/full#stat.356.20.5">§</a>
          <h2 class="subd_no">Subd. 5.</h2>
          <p>[Repealed, <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/laws/1984/0/383/#laws.0.5.0">1984 c 383 s 5</a>]</p>
        </div>
      </div>
      <div class="history" id="stat.356.20.history
			">

      <div class="sr" id="stat.356.211"><b>356.211</b> [Repealed, <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/laws/1975/0/192/#laws.0.7.0">1975 c 192 s 7</a>]</div>
      <div class="sr" id="stat.356.212"><b>356.212</b> [Repealed, <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/laws/1975/0/192/#laws.0.7.0">1975 c 192 s 7</a>]</div>
      <div class="section" id="stat.356.214">
        <h1 class="shn">356.214 ACTUARIAL VALUATION PREPARATION.</h1>
        <div class="subd" id="stat.356.214.1">
          <a title="Link to Subdivision 1." class="permalink" href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/statutes/2024/cite/356/full#stat.356.214.1">§</a>
          <h2 class="subd_no">Subdivision 1.<span class="headnote">Actuary retention.</span></h2>
          <p class="in">(a) The governing board or managing or administrative official of each public pension plan and retirement fund or plan enumerated in paragraph (b) shall contract with an established actuarial consulting firm to conduct annual actuarial valuations and related services. The principal from the actuarial consulting firm on the contract must be an approved actuary under section <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/statutes/cite/356.215#stat.356.215.1">356.215, subdivision 1</a>, paragraph (c).</p>
          <p class="in">(b) Actuarial services must include the preparation of actuarial valuations and related actuarial work for the following retirement plans:</p>
          <p class="in">(1) the teachers retirement plan, Teachers Retirement Association;</p>
          <p class="in">(2) the general state employees retirement plan, Minnesota State Retirement System;</p>
          <p class="in">(3) the correctional employees retirement plan, Minnesota State Retirement System;</p>
          <p class="in">(4) the State Patrol retirement plan, Minnesota State Retirement System;</p>
          <p class="in">(5) the judges retirement plan, Minnesota State Retirement System;</p>
          <p class="in">(6) the general employees retirement plan, Public Employees Retirement Association;</p>
          <p class="in">(7) the public employees police and fire plan, Public Employees Retirement Association;</p>
          <p class="in">(8) the St. Paul teachers retirement plan, St. Paul Teachers Retirement Fund Association;</p>
          <p class="in">(9) the legislators retirement plan, Minnesota State Retirement System; and</p>
          <p class="in">(10) the local government correctional service retirement plan, Public Employees Retirement Association.</p>
          <p>(c) The actuarial valuation for the legislators retirement plan must include a separate calculation of total plan actuarial accrued liabilities due to constitutional officer coverage under section <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/statutes/cite/3A.17">3A.17</a>.</p>
          <p class="in">(d) The contracts must require completion of the annual actuarial valuation calculations on a fiscal year basis, with the contents of the actuarial valuation calculations as specified in section <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/statutes/cite/356.215">356.215</a>, and in conformity with the standards for actuarial work adopted by the Legislative Commission on Pensions and Retirement.</p>
          <p class="in">The contracts must require completion of annual experience data collection and processing and a quadrennial published experience study for the plans listed in paragraph (b), clauses (1), (2), and (6), as provided for in the standards for actuarial work adopted by the commission. The experience data collection, processing, and analysis must evaluate the following:</p>
          <p class="in">(1) individual salary progression;</p>
          <p class="in">(2) the rate of return on investments based on the current asset value;</p>
          <p class="in">(3) payroll growth;</p>
          <p class="in">(4) mortality;</p>
          <p class="in">(5) retirement age;</p>
          <p class="in">(6) withdrawal; and</p>
          <p class="in">(7) disablement.</p>
          <p class="in">(e) The actuary shall annually prepare a report to the governing or managing board or administrative official and the legislature, summarizing the results of the actuarial valuation calculations. The actuary shall include with the report any recommendations concerning the appropriateness of the support rates to achieve proper funding of the retirement plans by the required funding dates. The actuary shall, as part of the quadrennial experience study, include recommendations on the appropriateness of the actuarial valuation assumptions required for evaluation in the study.</p>
          <p class="in">(f) If the actuarial gain and loss analysis in the actuarial valuation calculations indicates a persistent pattern of sizable gains or losses, the governing or managing board or administrative official shall direct the actuary to prepare a special experience study for a plan listed in paragraph (b), clause (3), (4), (5), (7), (8), (9), or (10), in the manner provided for in the standards for actuarial work adopted by the commission.</p>
        </div>
        <div class="subd" id="stat.356.214.2">
          <a title="Link to Subd. 2." class="permalink" href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/statutes/2024/cite/356/full#stat.356.214.2">§</a>
          <h2 class="subd_no">Subd. 2.</h2>
          <p>[Repealed, <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/laws/2008/0/349/#laws.10.18.0">2008 c 349 art 10 s 18</a>]</p>
        </div>
        <div class="subd" id="stat.356.214.3">
          <a title="Link to Subd. 3." class="permalink" href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/statutes/2024/cite/356/full#stat.356.214.3">§</a>
          <h2 class="subd_no">Subd. 3.<span class="headnote">Reporting to commission.</span></h2>
          <p class="in">A copy of the actuarial valuations and experience studies prepared by the actuary retained under a contract provided for in this section must be filed with the executive director of the Legislative Commission on Pensions and Retirement.</p>
        </div>
        <div class="subd" id="stat.356.214.4">
          <a title="Link to Subd. 4." class="permalink" href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/statutes/2024/cite/356/full#stat.356.214.4">§</a>
          <h2 class="subd_no">Subd. 4.<span class="headnote">Commission to contract with auditing actuary.</span></h2>
          <p class="in">(a) The Legislative Commission on Pensions and Retirement may contract with an established actuarial consulting firm to audit or review the actuarial valuations, experience studies, and actuarial cost analyses prepared by the actuary retained by the governing or managing boards, or administrative officials of each of the plans or funds listed in subdivision 1, paragraph (b). The principal representative from the actuarial consulting firm so engaged must be an approved actuary under section <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/statutes/cite/356.215#stat.356.215.1">356.215, subdivision 1</a>, paragraph (c).</p>
          <p class="in">(b) Any actuarial consulting firm retained under paragraph (a) will, according to a schedule determined under an agreement with the Legislative Commission on Pensions and Retirement, audit the valuation reports submitted by the actuary retained by each governing or managing board or administrative official, and provide an assessment of the reasonableness, reliability, and areas of concern or potential improvement in the specific reports reviewed, the procedures utilized by any particular reporting actuary, or general modifications to standards, procedures, or assumptions that the commission may wish to consider. Actuarial firms retained by the retirement funds must cooperate fully and make available any data or other materials necessary for the commission-retained actuary to conduct an adequate review and to render advice to the commission.</p>
        </div>
      </div>
      <div class="history" id="stat.356.214.history
			">
        <h2>History: </h2>
        <p class="first"><a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/laws/2004/0/223/#laws.0.6.0">2004 c 223 s 6</a>; <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/laws/2006/0/277/#laws.3.32.0">2006 c 277 art 3 s 32</a>; <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/laws/2008/0/349/#laws.10.7.0">2008 c 349 art 10 s 7</a>-9; <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/laws/2010/0/359/#laws.12.24.0">2010 c 359 art 12 s 24</a>; <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/laws/2013/0/111/#laws.2.25.0">2013 c 111 art 2 s 25</a>; <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/laws/2014/0/296/#laws.6.33.0">2014 c 296 art 6 s 33</a>,49; <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/laws/2015/0/68/#laws.14.17.0">2015 c 68 art 14 s 17</a></p>
      </div>
      <div class="section" id="stat.356.215">
        <h1 class="shn">356.215 ACTUARIAL VALUATIONS AND EXPERIENCE STUDIES.</h1>
        <div class="subd" id="stat.356.215.1">
          <a title="Link to Subdivision 1." class="permalink" href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/statutes/2024/cite/356/full#stat.356.215.1">§</a>
          <h2 class="subd_no">Subdivision 1.<span class="headnote">Definitions.</span></h2>
          <p class="in">(a) For the purposes of sections <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/statutes/cite/3.85">3.85</a> and <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/statutes/cite/356.20">356.20</a> to <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/statutes/cite/356.23">356.23</a>, each of the terms in the following paragraphs has the meaning given.</p>
          <p class="in">(b) "Actuarial valuation" means a set of calculations prepared by an actuary retained under section <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/statutes/cite/356.214">356.214 </a>if so required under section <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/statutes/cite/3.85">3.85</a>, or otherwise, by an approved actuary, to determine the normal cost and the accrued actuarial liabilities of a benefit plan, according to the entry age actuarial cost method and based upon stated assumptions including, but not limited to rates of interest, mortality, salary increase, disability, withdrawal, and retirement and to determine the payment necessary to amortize over a stated period any unfunded accrued actuarial liability disclosed as a result of the actuarial valuation of the benefit plan.</p>
          <p class="in">(c) "Approved actuary" means:</p>
          <p class="in">(1) a person who is regularly engaged in the business of providing actuarial services and who is a fellow in the Society of Actuaries; or</p>
          <p>(2) a firm that retains a person described in clause (1) on its staff.</p>
          <p class="in">(d) "Entry age actuarial cost method" means an actuarial cost method under which the actuarial present value of the projected benefits of each individual currently covered by the benefit plan and included in the actuarial valuation is allocated on a level basis over the service of the individual, if the benefit plan is governed by section <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/statutes/cite/424A.093">424A.093</a>, or over the earnings of the individual, if the benefit plan is governed by any other law, between the entry age and the assumed exit age, with the portion of the actuarial present value which is allocated to the valuation year to be the normal cost and the portion of the actuarial present value not provided for at the valuation date by the actuarial present value of future normal costs to be the actuarial accrued liability, with aggregation in the calculation process to be the sum of the calculated result for each covered individual and with recognition given to any different benefit formulas which may apply to various periods of service.</p>
          <p class="in">(e) "Experience study" means a report providing experience data and an actuarial analysis of the adequacy of the actuarial assumptions on which actuarial valuations are based.</p>
          <p class="in">(f) "Actuarial value of assets" means the market value of all assets as of the preceding June 30, reduced by:</p>
          <p class="in">(1) 20 percent of the difference between the actual net change in the market value of total assets between the June 30 that occurred three years earlier and the June 30 that occurred four years earlier and the computed increase in the market value of total assets over that fiscal year period if the assets had earned a rate of return on assets equal to the annual percentage investment return assumption used in the actuarial valuation for the July 1 that occurred four years earlier;</p>
          <p class="in">(2) 40 percent of the difference between the actual net change in the market value of total assets between the June 30 that occurred two years earlier and the June 30 that occurred three years earlier and the computed increase in the market value of total assets over that fiscal year period if the assets had earned a rate of return on assets equal to the annual percentage investment return assumption used in the actuarial valuation for the July 1 that occurred three years earlier;</p>
          <p class="in">(3) 60 percent of the difference between the actual net change in the market value of total assets between the June 30 that occurred one year earlier and the June 30 that occurred two years earlier and the computed increase in the market value of total assets over that fiscal year period if the assets had earned a rate of return on assets equal to the annual percentage investment return assumption used in the actuarial valuation for the July 1 that occurred two years earlier; and</p>
          <p class="in">(4) 80 percent of the difference between the actual net change in the market value of total assets between the most recent June 30 and the June 30 that occurred one year earlier and the computed increase in the market value of total assets over that fiscal year period if the assets had earned a rate of return on assets equal to the annual percentage investment return assumption used in the actuarial valuation for the July 1 that occurred one year earlier.</p>
          <p class="in">(g) "Unfunded actuarial accrued liability" means the total current and expected future benefit obligations, reduced by the sum of the actuarial value of assets and the present value of future normal costs.</p>
          <p class="in">(h) "Pension benefit obligation" means the actuarial present value of credited projected benefits, determined as the actuarial present value of benefits estimated to be payable in the future as a result of employee service attributing an equal benefit amount, including the effect of projected salary increases and any step rate benefit accrual rate differences, to each year of credited and expected future employee service.</p>
        </div>
        <div class="subd" id="stat.356.215.2">
          <a title="Link to Subd. 2." class="permalink" href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/statutes/2024/cite/356/full#stat.356.215.2">§</a>
          <h2 class="subd_no">Subd. 2.<span class="headnote">Requirements.</span></h2>
          <p class="in">(a) It is the policy of the legislature that it is necessary and appropriate to determine annually the financial status of tax-supported retirement and pension plans for public employees. To achieve this goal, the actuary retained under section <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/statutes/cite/356.214">356.214 </a>shall prepare annual actuarial valuations, as of the beginning of each fiscal year, of the retirement plans enumerated in section <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/statutes/cite/356.214#stat.356.214.1">356.214, subdivision 1</a>, paragraph (b), and quadrennial experience studies of the retirement plans enumerated in section <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/statutes/cite/356.214#stat.356.214.1">356.214, subdivision 1</a>, paragraph (b), clauses (1), (2), and (6).</p>
          <p class="in">(b) The governing board or executive director of each public pension and retirement plan enumerated in section <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/statutes/cite/356.20#stat.356.20.2">356.20, subdivision 2</a>, clauses (7), (9), and (10), shall have prepared by an approved actuary annual actuarial valuations of their respective funds as provided in this section. This requirement also applies to any plan that is the successor to any organization enumerated in section <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/statutes/cite/356.20#stat.356.20.2">356.20, subdivision 2</a>, or to the governing board or chief administrative officer of any newly formed retirement fund, plan, or association operating under the control or supervision of any public employee group, governmental unit, or institution receiving a portion of its support through legislative appropriations, and any local police or fire relief association to which section <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/statutes/cite/356.216">356.216</a> applies.</p>
        </div>
        <div class="subd" id="stat.356.215.2a">
          <a title="Link to Subd. 2a." class="permalink" href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/statutes/2024/cite/356/full#stat.356.215.2a">§</a>
          <h2 class="subd_no">Subd. 2a.</h2>
          <p>[Repealed, <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/laws/2008/0/349/#laws.10.18.0">2008 c 349 art 10 s 18</a>]</p>
        </div>
        <div class="subd" id="stat.356.215.3">
          <a title="Link to Subd. 3." class="permalink" href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/statutes/2024/cite/356/full#stat.356.215.3">§</a>
          <h2 class="subd_no">Subd. 3.<span class="headnote">Reports.</span></h2>
          <p class="in">(a) The governing board or executive director of each public pension plan required to prepare an annual valuation under subdivision 2 must deliver the annual valuation to the executive director of the Legislative Commission on Pensions and Retirement, the commissioner of management and budget, and the Legislative Reference Library no later than the last day of the sixth month occurring after the end of the previous fiscal year. The annual valuation may be delivered by email.</p>
          <p class="in">(b) The governing board or executive director of each public pension plan required to prepare a quadrennial experience study under subdivision 2 must deliver the quadrennial experience study to the executive director of the Legislative Commission on Pensions and Retirement, the commissioner of management and budget, and the Legislative Reference Library, no later than the last day of the 12th month occurring after the end of the last fiscal year of the four-year period covered by the experience study. The quadrennial experience study may be delivered by email.</p>
        </div>
        <div class="subd" id="stat.356.215.4">
          <a title="Link to Subd. 4." class="permalink" href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/statutes/2024/cite/356/full#stat.356.215.4">§</a>
          <h2 class="subd_no">Subd. 4.<span class="headnote">Actuarial valuation; contents.</span></h2>
          <p>(a) The actuarial valuation must be made in conformity with the requirements of the definition contained in subdivision 1 and the most recent standards for actuarial work adopted by the Legislative Commission on Pensions and Retirement.</p>
          <p>(b) The actuarial valuation must measure all aspects of the benefit plan of the fund in accordance with changes in benefit plans, if any, and salaries reasonably anticipated to be in force during the ensuing fiscal year. The actuarial valuation must be prepared in accordance with the entry age actuarial cost method. The actuarial valuation required under this section must include the information required in subdivisions 5 to 15.</p>
        </div>
        <div class="subd" id="stat.356.215.4a">
          <a title="Link to Subd. 4a." class="permalink" href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/statutes/2024/cite/356/full#stat.356.215.4a">§</a>
          <h2 class="subd_no">Subd. 4a.</h2>
          <p>[Renumbered subd 5]</p>

          <h2 class="subd_no">Subd. 4f.</h2>

        <div class="subd" id="stat.356.215.4g">
          <a title="Link to Subd. 4g." class="permalink" href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/statutes/2024/cite/356/full#stat.356.215.4g">§</a>
          <h2 class="subd_no">Subd. 4g.</h2>
          <p>[Renumbered subd 11]</p>
        </div>
        <div class="subd" id="stat.356.215.4h">
          <a title="Link to Subd. 4h." class="permalink" href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/statutes/2024/cite/356/full#stat.356.215.4h">§</a>
          <h2 class="subd_no">Subd. 4h.</h2>
          <p>[Renumbered subd 12]</p>
        </div>
        <div class="subd" id="stat.356.215.4i">
          <a title="Link to Subd. 4i." class="permalink" href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/statutes/2024/cite/356/full#stat.356.215.4i">§</a>
          <h2 class="subd_no">Subd. 4i.</h2>
          <p>[Renumbered subd 13]</p>
        </div>
        <div class="subd" id="stat.356.215.4j">
          <a title="Link to Subd. 4j." class="permalink" href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/statutes/2024/cite/356/full#stat.356.215.4j">§</a>
          <h2 class="subd_no">Subd. 4j.</h2>
          <p>[Renumbered subd 14]</p>
        </div>
        <div class="subd" id="stat.356.215.4k">
          <a title="Link to Subd. 4k." class="permalink" href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/statutes/2024/cite/356/full#stat.356.215.4k">§</a>
          <h2 class="subd_no">Subd. 4k.</h2>
          <p>[Renumbered subd 15]</p>
        </div>
        <div class="subd" id="stat.356.215.5">
          <a title="Link to Subd. 5." class="permalink" href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/statutes/2024/cite/356/full#stat.356.215.5">§</a>
          <h2 class="subd_no">Subd. 5.</h2>
          <p>MS 2000 [Renumbered subd 16]</p>
        </div>
        <div class="subd" id="stat.356.215.5">
          <a title="Link to Subd. 5." class="permalink" href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/statutes/2024/cite/356/full#stat.356.215.5">§</a>
          <h2 class="subd_no">Subd. 5.<span class="headnote">Normal cost.</span></h2>
          <p>For a fund providing benefits in whole or in part under a defined benefit plan, the actuarial valuation must indicate the level normal cost of the benefits provided under the laws governing the fund as of the date of the valuation, calculated in accordance with the entry age actuarial cost method. The normal cost must be expressed as a level percentage of the present value of future payrolls of the active participants of the fund as of the date of the valuation.</p>
        </div>
        <div class="subd" id="stat.356.215.6">
          <a title="Link to Subd. 6." class="permalink" href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/statutes/2024/cite/356/full#stat.356.215.6">§</a>
          <h2 class="subd_no">Subd. 6.</h2>
          <p>MS 2000 [Renumbered subd 17]</p>
        </div>
        <div class="subd" id="stat.356.215.6">
          <a title="Link to Subd. 6." class="permalink" href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/statutes/2024/cite/356/full#stat.356.215.6">§</a>
          <h2 class="subd_no">Subd. 6.<span class="headnote">Accrued liability.</span></h2>
          <p>For a fund providing benefits under a defined benefit plan, the actuarial valuation must contain an exhibit indicating the actuarial accrued liabilities of the fund. This figure is the present value of future benefits reduced by the present value of future normal costs, calculated in accordance with the entry age actuarial cost method.</p>
        </div>
        <div class="subd" id="stat.356.215.7">
          <a title="Link to Subd. 7." class="permalink" href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/statutes/2024/cite/356/full#stat.356.215.7">§</a>
          <h2 class="subd_no">Subd. 7.</h2>
          <p>MS 2000 [Renumbered subd 18]</p>
        </div>
        <div class="subd" id="stat.356.215.7">
          <a title="Link to Subd. 7." class="permalink" href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/statutes/2024/cite/356/full#stat.356.215.7">§</a>
          <h2 class="subd_no">Subd. 7.<span class="headnote">Defined contribution plan accumulations.</span></h2>
          <p>For each fund providing benefits under a money purchase or defined contribution plan, the actuarial valuation must contain an exhibit indicating the member contributions accumulated at interest, as apportioned to members accounts, to the date of the valuation. These accumulations must be separately tabulated in a manner which properly reflects any differences in money purchase or defined contribution annuity rates which may apply.</p>
        </div>
        <div class="subd" id="stat.356.215.8">
          <a title="Link to Subd. 8." class="permalink" href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/statutes/2024/cite/356/full#stat.356.215.8">§</a>
          <h2 class="subd_no">Subd. 8.<span class="headnote">Actuarial assumptions.</span></h2>
          <p class="in">(a) The actuarial valuation must use the applicable following investment return assumption:</p>
          <table>
            <col style="width:55.5555555555556%">
            <col style="width:22.5589225589226%">
            <col style="width:21.8855218855219%">
            <tr>
              <td style="text-align: center;">plan</td>
              <td style="text-align: center;"> investment return assumption</td>
              <td></td>
            </tr>
            <tr>
              <td class="valign_top">general state employees retirement plan</td>
              <td class="valign_top" style="text-align: center;">7%</td>
              <td class="valign_top" style="text-align: center;"></td>
            </tr>
            <tr>
              <td class="valign_top">correctional state employees retirement plan</td>
              <td class="valign_top" style="text-align: center;">7</td>
              <td class="valign_top" style="text-align: center;"></td>
            </tr>
            <tr>
              <td class="valign_top">State Patrol retirement plan</td>
              <td class="valign_top" style="text-align: center;">7</td>
              <td class="valign_top" style="text-align: center;"></td>
            </tr>
            <tr>
              <td class="valign_top">legislators retirement plan, and for the constitutional officers calculation of total plan liabilities</td>
              <td class="valign_top" style="text-align: center;">0</td>
              <td class="valign_top" style="text-align: center;"></td>
            </tr>
            <tr>
              <td class="valign_top">judges retirement plan</td>
              <td class="valign_top" style="text-align: center;">7</td>
              <td class="valign_top" style="text-align: center;"></td>
            </tr>
            <tr>
              <td class="valign_top">general public employees retirement plan</td>
              <td class="valign_top" style="text-align: center;">7</td>
              <td class="valign_top" style="text-align: center;"></td>
            </tr>
            <tr>
              <td class="valign_top">public employees police and fire retirement plan</td>
              <td class="valign_top" style="text-align: center;">7</td>
              <td class="valign_top" style="text-align: center;"></td>
            </tr>
            <tr>
              <td class="valign_top">local government correctional service retirement plan</td>
              <td class="valign_top" style="text-align: center;">7</td>
              <td class="valign_top" style="text-align: center;"></td>
            </tr>
            <tr>
              <td class="valign_top">teachers retirement plan</td>
              <td class="valign_top" style="text-align: center;">7</td>
              <td class="valign_top" style="text-align: center;"></td>
            </tr>
            <tr>
              <td class="valign_top">St. Paul teachers retirement plan</td>
              <td class="valign_top" style="text-align: center;">7</td>
              <td class="valign_top" style="text-align: center;"></td>
            </tr>
            <tr>
              <td>Bloomington Fire Department Relief Association</td>
              <td style="text-align: center;">6</td>
              <td style="text-align: center;"></td>
            </tr>
            <tr>
              <td>local monthly benefit volunteer firefighter relief associations</td>
              <td style="text-align: center;">5</td>
              <td style="text-align: center;"></td>
            </tr>
            <tr>
              <td class="valign_top">monthly benefit retirement plans in the statewide volunteer firefighter retirement plan</td>
              <td class="valign_top" style="text-align: center;">6</td>
              <td class="valign_top" style="text-align: center;"></td>
            </tr>
          </table>
          <p>(b) The actuarial valuation for each of the covered retirement plans listed in section <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/statutes/cite/356.415#stat.356.415.2">356.415, subdivision 2</a>, and the St. Paul Teachers Retirement Fund Association must take into account the postretirement adjustment rate or rates applicable to the plan as specified in section <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/statutes/cite/354A.29#stat.354A.29.7">354A.29, subdivision 7</a>, or <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/statutes/cite/356.415">356.415</a>, whichever applies.</p>
          <p class="in">(c) The actuarial valuation must use the applicable salary increase and payroll growth assumptions found in the appendix to the standards for actuarial work adopted by the Legislative Commission on Pensions and Retirement pursuant to section <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/statutes/cite/3.85#stat.3.85.10">3.85, subdivision 10</a>. The appendix must be updated whenever new assumptions have been approved or deemed approved under subdivision 18.</p>
          <p class="in">(d) The assumptions set forth in the appendix to the standards for actuarial work continue to apply, unless a different salary assumption or a different payroll increase assumption:</p>
          <p class="in">(1) has been proposed by the governing board of the applicable retirement plan;</p>
          <p class="in">(2) is accompanied by the concurring recommendation of the actuary retained under section <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/statutes/cite/356.214#stat.356.214.1">356.214, subdivision 1</a>, if applicable, or by the approved actuary preparing the most recent actuarial valuation report if section <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/statutes/cite/356.214">356.214</a> does not apply; and</p>
          <p class="in">(3) has been approved or deemed approved under subdivision 18.</p>
        </div>
        <div class="subd" id="stat.356.215.9">
          <a title="Link to Subd. 9." class="permalink" href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/statutes/2024/cite/356/full#stat.356.215.9">§</a>
          <h2 class="subd_no">Subd. 9.<span class="headnote">Other assumptions.</span></h2>
          <p>(a) Each plan's actuarial valuation must use assumptions concerning base mortality rates, disability, retirement, withdrawal, retirement age, and any other relevant demographic or economic factor. These assumptions must be set at levels consistent with those determined in the most recent quadrennial experience study completed under subdivision 16, if required, or as recommended by the plan's approved actuary, if a quadrennial experience study is not required.</p>
          <p>(b) The actuarial valuation may use an assumption concerning future mortality improvement. This assumption may be set at levels consistent with those determined in the most recent mortality improvement scale published by the Society of Actuaries or as otherwise recommended by the plan's approved actuary.</p>
          <p>(c) The actuarial valuation must contain an exhibit indicating the actuarial assumptions used in preparing the valuation report.</p>
        </div>
        <div class="subd" id="stat.356.215.10">
          <a title="Link to Subd. 10." class="permalink" href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/statutes/2024/cite/356/full#stat.356.215.10">§</a>
          <h2 class="subd_no">Subd. 10.<span class="headnote">Public sector accounting disclosure information.</span></h2>
          <p>The actuarial valuation must contain those actuarial calculations that are necessary to allow the retirement plan administration or participating employing units to prepare the pension-related portions of annual financial reporting that meet generally accepted accounting principles for the public sector.</p>
        </div>
        <div class="subd" id="stat.356.215.11">
          <a title="Link to Subd. 11." class="permalink" href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/statutes/2024/cite/356/full#stat.356.215.11">§</a>
          <h2 class="subd_no">Subd. 11.<span class="headnote">Amortization contributions.</span></h2>
          <p class="in">(a) In addition to the exhibit indicating the level normal cost, the actuarial valuation of the retirement plan must contain an exhibit for financial reporting purposes indicating the additional annual contribution sufficient to amortize the unfunded actuarial accrued liability and must contain an exhibit indicating the additional contribution sufficient to amortize the unfunded actuarial accrued liability. For the retirement plans listed in subdivision 8, paragraph (a), but excluding the legislators retirement plan, the Bloomington Fire Department Relief Association, and the local monthly benefit volunteer firefighter relief associations, the additional contribution must be calculated on a level percentage of covered payroll basis by the established date for full funding in effect when the valuation is prepared, assuming annual payroll growth at the applicable percentage rate set forth in the appendix described in subdivision 8, paragraph (c). For the legislators retirement plan, the additional annual contribution must be calculated on a level annual dollar amount basis.</p>
          <p class="in">(b) This paragraph applies only if the calculation under this paragraph for a retirement plan results in an established date for full funding that is earlier than the established date for full funding applicable to the retirement plan under paragraph (c). For any retirement plan, if there has been a change in any or all of the actuarial assumptions used for calculating the actuarial accrued liability of the fund, a change in the benefit plan governing annuities and benefits payable from the fund, a change in the actuarial cost method used in calculating the actuarial accrued liability of all or a portion of the fund, or a combination of the three, and the change or changes, by itself or by themselves and without inclusion of any other items of increase or decrease, produce a net increase in the unfunded actuarial accrued liability in the fund, the established date for full funding must be determined using the following procedure:</p>
          <p class="in">(i) the unfunded actuarial accrued liability of the fund must be determined in accordance with the plan provisions governing annuities and retirement benefits and the actuarial assumptions in effect before an applicable change;</p>
          <p class="in">(ii) the level annual dollar contribution or level percentage, whichever is applicable, needed to amortize the unfunded actuarial accrued liability amount determined under item (i) by the established date for full funding in effect before the change must be calculated using the investment return assumption specified in subdivision 8 in effect before the change;</p>
          <p class="in">(iii) the unfunded actuarial accrued liability of the fund must be determined in accordance with any new plan provisions governing annuities and benefits payable from the fund and any new actuarial assumptions and the remaining plan provisions governing annuities and benefits payable from the fund and actuarial assumptions in effect before the change;</p>
          <p class="in">(iv) the level annual dollar contribution or level percentage, whichever is applicable, needed to amortize the difference between the unfunded actuarial accrued liability amount calculated under item (i) and the unfunded actuarial accrued liability amount calculated under item (iii) over a period of 30 years from the end of the plan year in which the applicable change is effective must be calculated using the applicable investment return assumption specified in subdivision 8 in effect after any applicable change;</p>
          <p class="in">(v) the level annual dollar or level percentage amortization contribution under item (iv) must be added to the level annual dollar amortization contribution or level percentage calculated under item (ii);</p>
          <p class="in">(vi) the period in which the unfunded actuarial accrued liability amount determined in item (iii) is amortized by the total level annual dollar or level percentage amortization contribution computed under item (v) must be calculated using the investment return assumption specified in subdivision 8 in effect after any applicable change, rounded to the nearest integral number of years, but not to exceed 30 years from the end of the plan year in which the determination of the established date for full funding using the procedure set forth in this clause is made and not to be less than the period of years beginning in the plan year in which the determination of the established date for full funding using the procedure set forth in this clause is made and ending by the date for full funding in effect before the change; and</p>
          <p class="in">(vii) the period determined under item (vi) must be added to the date as of which the actuarial valuation was prepared and the date obtained is the new established date for full funding.</p>
          <p>(c) The established date for full funding is the date provided for each of the following plans:</p>
          <p class="in">(i) for the general employees retirement plan of the Public Employees Retirement Association, the established date for full funding is June 30, 2048;</p>
          <p class="in">(ii) for the Teachers Retirement Association, the established date for full funding is June 30, 2048;</p>
          <p class="in">(iii) for the correctional state employees retirement plan and the State Patrol retirement plan of the Minnesota State Retirement System, the established date for full funding is June 30, 2048;</p>
          <p class="in">(iv) for the judges retirement plan, the established date for full funding is June 30, 2048;</p>
          <p class="in">(v) for the local government correctional service retirement plan and the public employees police and fire retirement plan, the established date for full funding is June 30, 2048;</p>
          <p class="in">(vi) for the St. Paul Teachers Retirement Fund Association, the established date for full funding is June 30, 2048; and</p>
          <p>(vii) for the general state employees retirement plan of the Minnesota State Retirement System, the established date for full funding is June 30, 2048.</p>
          <p class="in">(d) For the retirement plans for which the annual actuarial valuation indicates an excess of valuation assets over the actuarial accrued liability, the valuation assets in excess of the actuarial accrued liability must be recognized as a reduction in the current contribution requirements by an amount equal to the amortization of the excess expressed as a level percentage of pay over a 30-year period beginning anew with each annual actuarial valuation of the plan.</p>
        </div>
        <div class="subd" id="stat.356.215.12">
          <a title="Link to Subd. 12." class="permalink" href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/statutes/2024/cite/356/full#stat.356.215.12">§</a>
          <h2 class="subd_no">Subd. 12.<span class="headnote">Actuarial gains and losses.</span></h2>
          <p>The actuarial valuation must contain an exhibit consisting of an analysis by the actuary explaining the net increase or decrease in the unfunded actuarial accrued liability since the last valuation. The explanation must subdivide the net increase or decrease in the unfunded actuarial accrued liability into at least the following parts:</p>
          <p>(1) increases or decreases in the unfunded actuarial accrued liability because of changes in benefits;</p>
          <p>(2) increases and decreases in the unfunded actuarial accrued liability because of changes in actuarial assumptions;</p>
          <p>(3) increases or decreases in the unfunded actuarial accrued liability attributable to actuarial gains or losses resulting from any experience deviations from the assumptions on which the valuation is based, as follows:</p>
          <p>(i) actual investment earnings;</p>
          <p>(ii) actual postretirement mortality rates;</p>
          <p>(iii) actual salary increase rates; and</p>
          <p>(iv) the remainder of the increase or decrease not attributable to any separate source;</p>
          <p>(4) increases or decreases in unfunded actuarial accrued liability because of other reasons, including the effect of any amortization contribution paid or additional amortization contribution previously calculated but unpaid; and</p>
          <p>(5) increases or decreases in unfunded actuarial accrued liability because of changes in eligibility requirements or groups included in the membership of the fund.</p>
        </div>
        <div class="subd" id="stat.356.215.13">
          <a title="Link to Subd. 13." class="permalink" href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/statutes/2024/cite/356/full#stat.356.215.13">§</a>
          <h2 class="subd_no">Subd. 13.<span class="headnote">Membership tabulation.</span></h2>
          <p>(a) The actuarial valuation must contain a tabulation of active membership and annuitants in the fund. If the membership of a fund is under more than one general benefit program, a separate tabulation must be made for each general benefit program.</p>
          <p>(b) The tabulations must be prepared by the administration of the pension fund and must contain the following information:</p>
          <table>
            <col style="width:7.692307692307693%">
            <col style="width:69.2307692307692%">

              <td></td>
            </tr>
          </table>
          <p>(c) The tabulation required under paragraph (b), clause (2), must be made separately for each of the following classes of benefit recipients:</p>
          <p>(1) service retirement annuitants;</p>
          <p>(2) disability benefit recipients;</p>
          <p>(3) survivor benefit recipients; and</p>
          <p>(4) deferred annuitants.</p>
        </div>
        <div class="subd" id="stat.356.215.14">
          <a title="Link to Subd. 14." class="permalink" href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/statutes/2024/cite/356/full#stat.356.215.14">§</a>
          <h2 class="subd_no">Subd. 14.<span class="headnote">Administrative expenses.</span></h2>
          <p>(a) The actuarial valuation must indicate the administrative expenses of the fund, expressed both in dollars and as a percentage of covered payroll.</p>
          <p>(b) Administrative expenses are the costs incurred by the retirement plans in the course of operating the plan, excluding investment expenses. Investment expenses include all expenses incurred for the retention of professional external investment managers and professional investment consultants, custodian bank fees, investment transaction costs, and the costs incurred by the retirement plans to manage investment portfolios or assets internally. Investment expenses must be deducted from the investment return used in the actuarial valuation, and must not be included in administrative expenses when calculating the allowance for expenses.</p>
        </div>
        <div class="subd" id="stat.356.215.15">
          <a title="Link to Subd. 15." class="permalink" href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/statutes/2024/cite/356/full#stat.356.215.15">§</a>
          <h2 class="subd_no">Subd. 15.<span class="headnote">Benefit plan summary.</span></h2>
          <p>The actuarial valuation must contain a summary of the principal provisions of the benefit plan upon which the valuation is based.</p>
        </div>
        <div class="subd" id="stat.356.215.16">
          <a title="Link to Subd. 16." class="permalink" href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/statutes/2024/cite/356/full#stat.356.215.16">§</a>
          <h2 class="subd_no">Subd. 16.<span class="headnote">Quadrennial experience study; contents.</span></h2>
          <p>A quadrennial experience study, if required, must contain an analysis by the approved actuary of the experience of the fund and a comparison of the experience with the actuarial assumptions on which the most recent actuarial valuation of the retirement fund was based.</p>
        </div>
        <div class="subd" id="stat.356.215.17">
          <a title="Link to Subd. 17." class="permalink" href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/statutes/2024/cite/356/full#stat.356.215.17">§</a>
          <h2 class="subd_no">Subd. 17.<span class="headnote">Actuarial services by approved actuaries.</span></h2>
          <p>(a) The actuarial valuation or quadrennial experience study must be made and any actuarial consulting services for a retirement fund or plan must be provided by an approved actuary. The actuarial valuation or quadrennial experience study must include a signed written declaration that it has been prepared according to sections <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/statutes/cite/356.20">356.20</a> to <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/statutes/cite/356.23">356.23</a> and according to the most recent standards for actuarial work adopted by the Legislative Commission on Pensions and Retirement.</p>
          <p>(b) Actuarial valuations or experience studies prepared by an approved actuary retained by a retirement fund or plan must be submitted to the Legislative Commission on Pensions and Retirement within ten days of the submission of the document to the retirement fund or plan.</p>
        </div>
        <div class="subd" id="stat.356.215.18">
          <a title="Link to Subd. 18." class="permalink" href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/statutes/2024/cite/356/full#stat.356.215.18">§</a>
          <h2 class="subd_no">Subd. 18.<span class="headnote">Establishment of actuarial assumptions.</span></h2>
          <p class="in">(a) The actuarial assumptions used for the preparation of actuarial valuations under this section that are other than the interest rate may be changed only with the approval of the Legislative Commission on Pensions and Retirement or after a period of one year has elapsed since the date on which the proposed assumption change or changes were received by the Legislative Commission on Pensions and Retirement without commission action.</p>
          <p class="in">(b) A change in the applicable actuarial assumptions may be proposed by the governing board of the applicable pension fund or relief association, by an actuary retained under section <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/statutes/cite/356.214">356.214</a> or by the actuary retained by a local relief association governed by sections <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/statutes/cite/424A.091">424A.091</a> to <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/statutes/cite/424A.096">424A.096</a> or by Laws 2013, chapter 111, article 5, sections 31 to 42, if one is retained.</p>
        </div>
      </div>
