      <div class="section" id="stat.356.216">
        <h1 class="shn">356.216 CONTENTS OF ACTUARIAL VALUATIONS FOR LOCAL MONTHLY VOLUNTEER FIREFIGHTER RELIEF ASSOCIATIONS.</h1>
        <p>The provisions of section <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/statutes/cite/356.215">356.215</a> that govern the contents of actuarial valuations apply to the Bloomington Fire Department Relief Association and to any local monthly firefighters relief association required to make an actuarial report under this section, except as follows:</p>
        <p>(1) in lieu of the amortization date specified in section <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/statutes/cite/356.215#stat.356.215.11">356.215, subdivision 11</a>, the appropriate amortization target date specified in clause (2) or section <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/statutes/cite/424A.093#stat.424A.093.4">424A.093, subdivision 4</a>, paragraph (c), must be used in calculating any required amortization contribution;</p>
        <p>(2) for the Bloomington Fire Department Relief Association, any unfunded actuarial accrued liability must be amortized on a level dollar basis by December 31 of the year occurring 20 years after the year in which the unfunded actuarial accrued liability initially occurred, and, if subsequent actuarial valuations for the Bloomington Fire Department Relief Association indicate a net actuarial experience loss incurred during the year which ended as of the day before the most recent actuarial valuation date, any unfunded actuarial accrued liability due to that loss is to be amortized on a level dollar basis by December 31 of the year occurring 20 years after the year in which the net actuarial experience loss occurred;</p>
        <p>(3) in addition to the tabulation of active members and annuitants provided for in section <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/statutes/cite/356.215#stat.356.215.13">356.215, subdivision 13</a>, the prospective annual service pensions under the benefit plan for active members must be reported;</p>
        <p>(4) actuarial valuations required under Laws 2013, chapter 111, article 5, section 39, must be made annually and actuarial valuations required under section <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/statutes/cite/424A.093#stat.424A.093.2">424A.093, subdivision 2</a>, must be made every four years or as frequently as required by generally accepted accounting principles in the government sector, whichever frequency requirement is shorter;</p>
        <p>(5) the actuarial balance sheet showing accrued assets valued at market value, actuarial accrued liabilities, and the unfunded actuarial accrued liability must include the following required reserves:</p>
        <p>(i) for active members:</p>
        <p>(A) retirement benefits or service pensions;</p>
        <p>(B) disability benefits; and</p>
        <p>(C) survivors' benefits;</p>
        <p>(ii) for deferred annuitants' benefits;</p>
        <p>(iii) for former members without vested rights;</p>
        <p>(iv) for annuitants:</p>
        <p>(A) retirement annuities or service pensions;</p>
        <p>(B) disability annuities; and</p>
        <p>(C) survivor benefits.</p>
        <p>In addition to those required reserves, separate items must be shown for additional benefits, if any, which may not be appropriately included in the reserves listed above; and</p>
        <p>(6) actuarial valuations are due to be filed with the state auditor by the first day of the seventh month after the end of the fiscal year which the actuarial valuation covers.</p>
      </div>
      <div class="history" id="stat.356.216.history
			">
        <h2>History: </h2>
        <p class="first"><a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/laws/1978/0/563/#laws.0.11.0">1978 c 563 s 11</a>; <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/laws/1981/0/224/#laws.0.170.0">1981 c 224 s 170</a>; <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/laws/1983/0/71/#laws.0.2.0">1983 c 71 s 2</a>; <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/laws/1985/1/7/#laws.0.28.0">1Sp1985 c 7 s 28</a>; <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/laws/1986/0/359/#laws.0.14.0">1986 c 359 s 14</a>; <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/laws/1986/1/3/#laws.1.46.0">1Sp1986 c 3 art 1 s 46</a>; <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/laws/1987/0/259/#laws.0.56.0">1987 c 259 s 56</a>; <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/laws/1989/0/319/#laws.19.4.0">1989 c 319 art 19 s 4</a>; <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/laws/1991/0/199/#laws.1.91.0">1991 c 199 art 1 s 91</a>; <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/laws/2002/0/392/#laws.1.8.0">2002 c 392 art 1 s 8</a>; art 11 s 8; <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/laws/2005/1/8/#laws.11.3.0">1Sp2005 c 8 art 11 s 3</a>; <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/laws/2010/0/359/#laws.15.3.0">2010 c 359 art 15 s 3</a>; <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/laws/2011/1/8/#laws.6.19.0">1Sp2011 c 8 art 6 s 19; art 7 s 19; art 8 s 7,14</a>; <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/laws/2013/0/111/#laws.5.62.0">2013 c 111 art 5 s 62</a>,80; <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/laws/2024/0/102/#laws.2.31.0">2024 c 102 art 2 s 31</a></p>
      </div>
      <div class="sr" id="stat.356.2165"><b>356.2165</b> [Repealed, <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/laws/2009/0/169/#laws.11.7.0">2009 c 169 art 11 s 7</a>]</div>
      <div class="sr" id="stat.356.217"><b>356.217</b> [Repealed, <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/laws/2004/0/223/#laws.0.11.0">2004 c 223 s 11</a>]</div>
      <div class="sr" id="stat.356.218"><b>356.218</b> [Repealed, <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/laws/1997/0/241/#laws.10.7.0">1997 c 241 art 10 s 7</a>]</div>
      <div class="section" id="stat.356.219">
        <h1 class="shn">356.219 DISCLOSURE OF PUBLIC PENSION PLAN INVESTMENT PORTFOLIO AND PERFORMANCE INFORMATION.</h1>
        <div class="subd" id="stat.356.219.1">
          <a title="Link to Subdivision 1." class="permalink" href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/statutes/2024/cite/356/full#stat.356.219.1">§</a>
          <h2 class="subd_no">Subdivision 1.<span class="headnote">Report required.</span></h2>
          <p>(a) The Bloomington Fire Department Relief Association, volunteer firefighters relief associations governed by sections <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/statutes/cite/424A.091">424A.091</a> to <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/statutes/cite/424A.095">424A.095</a>, the St. Paul Teachers Retirement Fund Association, and any Minnesota public pension plan that is not fully invested through the State Board of Investment, must report the information specified in subdivision 3 to the state auditor. The state auditor may prescribe a form or forms for the purposes of the reporting requirements contained in this section.</p>
          <p>(b) For purposes of this section, a pension plan is fully invested through the State Board of Investment during a given calendar year if all assets of the pension plan beyond sufficient cash equivalent investments to cover six months of expected expenses are invested under section <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/statutes/cite/11A.17">11A.17</a>.</p>
          <p>(c) A public pension plan to which subdivision 3, paragraph (b) or (c), applies is not required to file the report required by this subdivision for a given calendar year if the pension plan's most recent annual financial audit was conducted by the state auditor.</p>
          <p>(d) This section does not apply to the following plans:</p>
          <p>(1) the Minnesota unclassified employees retirement program under chapter 352D;</p>
          <p>(2) the public employees defined contribution plan under chapter 353D;</p>
          <p>(3) the individual retirement account plans under chapters 354B and 354D;</p>
          <p>(4) the higher education supplemental retirement plan under chapter 354C;</p>
          <p>(5) any alternative retirement benefit plan established under section <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/statutes/cite/383B.914">383B.914</a>;</p>
          <p>(6) the University of Minnesota faculty retirement plan and supplemental plan; and</p>
          <p>(7) any other statewide plan required to be invested by the State Board of Investment under section <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/statutes/cite/11A.23">11A.23</a>.</p>
        </div>
        <div class="subd" id="stat.356.219.2">
          <a title="Link to Subd. 2." class="permalink" href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/statutes/2024/cite/356/full#stat.356.219.2">§</a>
          <h2 class="subd_no">Subd. 2.<span class="headnote">Asset class definition.</span></h2>
          <p>(a) For purposes of this section, "asset class" means any of the following asset groupings as authorized in applicable law, bylaws, or articles of incorporation:</p>
          <p>(1) cash and any cash equivalent investments with maturities of one year or less when issued;</p>
          <p>(2) debt securities with maturities greater than one year when issued, including but not limited to mortgage participation certificates and pools, asset backed securities, guaranteed investment contracts, and authorized government and corporate obligations of corporations organized under laws of the United States or any state, or the Dominion of Canada or its provinces;</p>
          <p>(3) stocks or convertible issues of any corporation organized under laws of the United States or any state, or the Dominion of Canada or its provinces, or any corporation listed on the New York Stock Exchange or the American Stock Exchange;</p>
          <p>(4) international stocks or convertible issues;</p>
          <p>(5) international debt securities; and</p>
          <p>(6) real estate and venture capital.</p>
          <p>(b) If the pension plan is investing in open-end investment companies registered under the federal Investment Company Act of 1940, or in the Minnesota supplemental investment fund under section <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/statutes/cite/11A.17">11A.17</a>, this investment must be included under an asset class indicated in paragraph (a), clauses (1) through (6), as appropriate. If the investment vehicle includes underlying securities from more than one asset class as indicated by paragraph (a), clauses (1) through (6), the investment may be treated as a separate asset class.</p>
        </div>
        <div class="subd" id="stat.356.219.3">
          <a title="Link to Subd. 3." class="permalink" href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/statutes/2024/cite/356/full#stat.356.219.3">§</a>
          <h2 class="subd_no">Subd. 3.<span class="headnote">Content of reports.</span></h2>
          <p>(a) The report required by subdivision 1 must include a written statement of the investment policy. Following that initial report, subsequent reports must include investment policy changes and the effective date of each policy change rather than a complete statement of investment policy, unless the state auditor requests submission of a complete current statement. The report must also include the information required by the following paragraphs, as applicable.</p>
          <p>(b) For public pension plans not fully invested through the State Board of Investment with assets having a market value of $50,000,000 or more as of the beginning of the calendar year, or that had a market value of $50,000,000 or more in a prior calendar year, the report required by subdivision 1 must include the following:</p>
          <p>(1) the market value of the total portfolio and the market value of each investment account, investment portfolio, or asset class included in the pension fund for each month;</p>
          <p>(2) the amount and date of each injection and withdrawal to the total portfolio and to each investment account, investment portfolio, or asset class;</p>
          <p>(3) a calculation of the total time-weighted rate of return available from index-matching investments, assuming the asset class performance targets and target asset mix indicated in the written statement of investment policy;</p>
          <p>(4) a description of indices used in the analyses and an explanation of why those indices are appropriate;</p>
          <p>(5) computed time-weighted rates of return; and</p>
          <p>(6) any other information required by the state auditor.</p>
          <p>(c) For public pension plans fully invested through the State Board of Investment with assets having a market value of $50,000,000 or more as of the beginning of the calendar year, or that had a market value of $50,000,000 or more in a prior calendar year, the report required by subdivision 1 must be in the form required by the state auditor and include the information needed by the state auditor to supplement the reporting available from the State Board of Investment.</p>
          <p>(d) If a public pension plan has assets with a total market value of less than $50,000,000 as of the beginning of the calendar year and was never required to file under paragraph (b) or (c), the report required by subdivision 1 must include the following:</p>
          <p>(1) unless paragraph (f) applies, the amount and date of each total portfolio injection and withdrawal; and</p>
          <p>(2) the market value of the total portfolio as of the beginning of the calendar year and for each quarter.</p>
          <p>(e) For public pension plans reporting under paragraph (d), the public pension plan must retain supporting information specifying the date and amount of each injection and withdrawal to each investment account and investment portfolio. The public pension plan must also retain the market value of each investment account and investment portfolio at the beginning of the calendar year and for each quarter. Information that is required to be collected and retained for any given year or years under this paragraph must be submitted to the state auditor if the state auditor requests in writing that the information be submitted by the public pension plan. If the state auditor requests information under this subdivision, and the pension plan fails to comply, the pension plan is subject to penalties under subdivision 5, unless penalties are waived by the state auditor under that subdivision.</p>
          <p>(f) A public pension plan reporting under paragraph (d) that is fully invested through the State Board of Investment for the given calendar year is required to report the market value of the total portfolio as of the beginning of the calendar year and for each quarter, but need not report the amount and date of each total portfolio injection and withdrawal.</p>
        </div>
        <div class="subd" id="stat.356.219.4">
          <a title="Link to Subd. 4." class="permalink" href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/statutes/2024/cite/356/full#stat.356.219.4">§</a>
          <h2 class="subd_no">Subd. 4.</h2>
          <p>[Repealed, <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/laws/2012/0/286/#laws.10.13.0">2012 c 286 art 10 s 13</a>]</p>
        </div>
        <div class="subd" id="stat.356.219.5">
          <a title="Link to Subd. 5." class="permalink" href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/statutes/2024/cite/356/full#stat.356.219.5">§</a>
          <h2 class="subd_no">Subd. 5.<span class="headnote">Penalty for noncompliance.</span></h2>
          <p>Failure to comply with the reporting requirements of this section must result in a withholding of all state aid or state appropriation to which the pension plan may otherwise be directly or indirectly entitled until the pension plan has complied with the reporting requirements. The state auditor shall instruct the commissioners of revenue and management and budget to withhold any state aid or state appropriation from any pension plan that fails to comply with the reporting requirements contained in this section, until the pension plan has complied with the reporting requirements. The state auditor may waive the withholding of state aid or state appropriations if the state auditor determines in writing that compliance would create an excessive hardship for the pension plan.</p>
        </div>
        <div class="subd" id="stat.356.219.6">
          <a title="Link to Subd. 6." class="permalink" href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/statutes/2024/cite/356/full#stat.356.219.6">§</a>
          <h2 class="subd_no">Subd. 6.<span class="headnote">Investment disclosure report.</span></h2>
          <p>(a) The state auditor shall prepare an annual report to the legislature on the investment performance of the public pension plans subject to this section. The content of the report is specified in paragraphs (b) to (d).</p>
          <p>(b) For each public pension plan reporting under subdivision 3, paragraph (b), the state auditor shall compute and report total portfolio and asset class time-weighted rates of return, net of all investment-related costs and fees. The report by the state auditor must also include the information submitted by the pension plans under subdivision 3 or a summary of that information.</p>
          <p>(c) For each public pension plan reporting under subdivision 3, paragraph (d), the state auditor shall compute and report total portfolio time-weighted rates of return, net of all costs and fees.</p>
          <p>(d) The report by the state auditor may also include a presentation of multiyear performance and any other information or analysis deemed appropriate by the state auditor.</p>
        </div>
        <div class="subd" id="stat.356.219.7">
          <a title="Link to Subd. 7." class="permalink" href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/statutes/2024/cite/356/full#stat.356.219.7">§</a>
          <h2 class="subd_no">Subd. 7.<span class="headnote">Expense of report.</span></h2>
          <p>All administrative expenses incurred relating to the investment report by the state auditor described in subdivision 6 must be borne by the state auditor and may not be charged back to the entities described in subdivisions 1 or 4.</p>
        </div>
        <div class="subd" id="stat.356.219.8">
          <a title="Link to Subd. 8." class="permalink" href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/statutes/2024/cite/356/full#stat.356.219.8">§</a>
          <h2 class="subd_no">Subd. 8.<span class="headnote">Timing of reports.</span></h2>
          <p>(a) For the Bloomington Fire Department Relief Association and the volunteer firefighters relief associations, the information required under this section must be submitted by the due date for reports required under section <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/statutes/cite/424A.014#stat.424A.014.1">424A.014, subdivision 1</a> or 2, as applicable.</p>
          <p>(b) For the St. Paul Teachers Retirement Fund Association, the information required under this section must be submitted to the state auditor by June 1 of each year.</p>
          <p>(c) Any public pension plan required to submit information under this section that is not identified in paragraph (a) or (b) must submit the information to the state auditor by June 1 of each year.</p>
        </div>
        <div class="subd" id="stat.356.219.9">
          <a title="Link to Subd. 9." class="permalink" href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/statutes/2024/cite/356/full#stat.356.219.9">§</a>
          <h2 class="subd_no">Subd. 9.<span class="headnote">Data availability.</span></h2>
          <p>Any information received by the state auditor under this section, if the data are public, must be made available to individuals or organizations which request that information. The state auditor is authorized to charge fees sufficient to cover the cost of providing the requested information in usable formats.</p>
        </div>
        <div class="subd" id="stat.356.219.10">
          <a title="Link to Subd. 10." class="permalink" href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/statutes/2024/cite/356/full#stat.356.219.10">§</a>
          <h2 class="subd_no">Subd. 10.<span class="headnote">Pension performance reporting.</span></h2>
          <p>In addition to report presentations that the state auditor is required to provide elsewhere in this section, the state auditor shall provide an analysis comparing the one-year and the five-year rate of return for each pension fund and the benchmark rate of return for each fund. The state auditor shall select the benchmark rate of return based on the best practice in the industry.</p>
        </div>
      </div>
      <div class="history" id="stat.356.219.history
			">
        <h2>History: </h2>
        <p class="first"><a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/laws/1994/0/565/#laws.2.1.0">1994 c 565 art 2 s 1</a>; <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/laws/1995/0/262/#laws.9.1.0">1995 c 262 art 9 s 1</a>; <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/laws/1996/0/438/#laws.10.1.0">1996 c 438 art 10 s 1</a>; <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/laws/1997/0/241/#laws.10.4.0">1997 c 241 art 10 s 4</a>; <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/laws/2002/0/392/#laws.1.8.0">2002 c 392 art 1 s 8</a>; art 11 s 10; <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/laws/2006/0/271/#laws.8.4.0">2006 c 271 art 8 s 4</a>,5; <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/laws/2006/0/277/#laws.6.1.0">2006 c 277 art 6 s 1</a>,2; <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/laws/2009/0/101/#laws.2.109.0">2009 c 101 art 2 s 109</a>; <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/laws/2009/0/169/#laws.10.6.0">2009 c 169 art 10 s 6</a>; <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/laws/2012/0/286/#laws.10.7.0">2012 c 286 art 10 s 7</a>,8; <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/laws/2013/0/111/#laws.5.63.0">2013 c 111 art 5 s 63</a>-65,80; <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/laws/2014/0/296/#laws.6.35.0">2014 c 296 art 6 s 35</a>,49; <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/laws/2019/1/6/#laws.22.10.0">1Sp2019 c 6 art 22 s 10</a>; <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/laws/2019/1/8/#laws.6.1.0">1Sp2019 c 8 art 6 s 1</a>; <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/laws/2020/0/108/#laws.4.1.0">2020 c 108 art 4 s 1</a>-5</p>
      </div>
      <div class="section" id="stat.356.22">
        <h1 class="shn">356.22 INTERPRETATION.</h1>
        <div class="subd" id="stat.356.22.1">
          <a title="Link to Subdivision 1." class="permalink" href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/statutes/2024/cite/356/full#stat.356.22.1">§</a>
          <h2 class="subd_no">Subdivision 1.<span class="headnote">Provision of additional valuations.</span></h2>
          <p>No provision in sections <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/statutes/cite/356.20">356.20</a> to <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/statutes/cite/356.23">356.23</a> may be construed in any way to limit any of the enumerated pension and retirement funds from furnishing additional actuarial valuations or experience studies, or additional data and actuarial calculations, as may be requested by the legislature or any standing committee or by the Legislative Commission on Pensions and Retirement.</p>
        </div>
        <div class="subd" id="stat.356.22.2">
          <a title="Link to Subd. 2." class="permalink" href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/statutes/2024/cite/356/full#stat.356.22.2">§</a>
          <h2 class="subd_no">Subd. 2.<span class="headnote">Accelerated amortization.</span></h2>
          <p>No provision in sections <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/statutes/cite/356.20">356.20</a> to <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/statutes/cite/356.23">356.23</a> may be construed to preclude any public pension and retirement fund enumerated in section <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/statutes/cite/356.20#stat.356.20.2">356.20, subdivision 2</a>, from requesting, or the legislature from providing for, the amortization of any unfunded actuarial accrued liability in a shorter period of time than by the established date for full funding as determined under section <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/statutes/cite/356.215#stat.356.215.11">356.215, subdivision 11</a>.</p>
        </div>
        <div class="subd" id="stat.356.22.3">
          <a title="Link to Subd. 3." class="permalink" href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/statutes/2024/cite/356/full#stat.356.22.3">§</a>
          <h2 class="subd_no">Subd. 3.<span class="headnote">Additional required valuations.</span></h2>
          <p>The legislature or any committee or commission which has assigned to it the subject of public pensions or public retirement plans may require actuarial valuations and experience studies in conformity with the provisions of sections <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/statutes/cite/356.20">356.20</a> to <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/statutes/cite/356.23">356.23</a> from any public pension and retirement plan or fund, whether enumerated in sections <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/statutes/cite/356.20">356.20</a> to <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/statutes/cite/356.23">356.23</a> or otherwise.</p>
        </div>
      </div>
      <div class="history" id="stat.356.22.history
			">
        <h2>History: </h2>
        <p class="first"><a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/laws/1965/0/359/#laws.0.3.0">1965 c 359 s 3</a>; <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/laws/1975/0/192/#laws.0.4.0">1975 c 192 s 4</a>,5; <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/laws/1979/0/184/#laws.0.2.0">1979 c 184 s 2</a>; <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/laws/1981/0/224/#laws.0.171.0">1981 c 224 s 171</a>; <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/laws/1985/1/7/#laws.0.35.0">1Sp1985 c 7 s 35</a>; <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/laws/1987/0/259/#laws.0.57.0">1987 c 259 s 57</a>; <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/laws/2002/0/392/#laws.11.11.0">2002 c 392 art 11 s 11</a></p>
      </div>
      <div class="section" id="stat.356.23">
        <h1 class="shn">356.23 SUPPLEMENTAL VALUATIONS; ALTERNATIVE REPORTS AND VALUATIONS.</h1>
        <div class="subd" id="stat.356.23.1">
          <a title="Link to Subdivision 1." class="permalink" href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/statutes/2024/cite/356/full#stat.356.23.1">§</a>
          <h2 class="subd_no">Subdivision 1.<span class="headnote">Supplemental actuarial valuations.</span></h2>
          <p>Any supplemental actuarial valuations prepared on behalf of any governing or managing board of any pension and retirement fund enumerated in section <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/statutes/cite/356.20#stat.356.20.2">356.20, subdivision 2</a>, by an approved actuary, must be prepared in accordance with the applicable provisions of sections <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/statutes/cite/356.20">356.20</a> to <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/statutes/cite/356.23">356.23</a> and with the standards adopted by the Legislative Commission on Pensions and Retirement. Any pension and retirement fund which prepares an alternative actuarial valuation under subdivision 2 also must have a supplemental actuarial valuation prepared.</p>
        </div>
        <div class="subd" id="stat.356.23.2">
          <a title="Link to Subd. 2." class="permalink" href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/statutes/2024/cite/356/full#stat.356.23.2">§</a>
          <h2 class="subd_no">Subd. 2.<span class="headnote">Alternative reports and valuations.</span></h2>
          <p>In addition to the financial reports and actuarial valuations required by sections <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/statutes/cite/356.20">356.20</a> to <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/statutes/cite/356.23">356.23</a>, the governing or managing board of any fund concerned may submit alternative reports and actuarial valuations for distribution to the legislature, any of its committees, or the Legislative Commission on Pensions and Retirement on a different basis or on different assumptions than are specified in sections <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/statutes/cite/356.20">356.20</a> to <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/statutes/cite/356.23">356.23</a>. The assumptions and basis of any alternative reports and valuations must be clearly stated in the document.</p>
        </div>
      </div>
      <div class="history" id="stat.356.23.history
			">
        <h2>History: </h2>
        <p class="first"><a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/laws/1965/0/359/#laws.0.4.0">1965 c 359 s 4</a>; <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/laws/1971/0/7/#laws.0.7.0">1971 c 7 s 7</a>; <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/laws/1975/0/192/#laws.0.6.0">1975 c 192 s 6</a>; <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/laws/1984/0/655/#laws.1.59.0">1984 c 655 art 1 s 59</a>; <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/laws/1987/0/259/#laws.0.58.0">1987 c 259 s 58</a>; <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/laws/2002/0/392/#laws.11.12.0">2002 c 392 art 11 s 12</a></p>
      </div>
      <h1 class="fgh">LIMITATIONS ON SUPPLEMENTAL AND LOCAL<br>RETIREMENT PLANS</h1>
      <div class="section" id="stat.356.24">
        <h1 class="shn">356.24 SUPPLEMENTAL PENSION OR DEFERRED COMPENSATION PLANS, RESTRICTIONS UPON GOVERNMENT UNITS.</h1>
        <div class="subd" id="stat.356.24.1">
          <a title="Link to Subdivision 1." class="permalink" href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/statutes/2024/cite/356/full#stat.356.24.1">§</a>
          <h2 class="subd_no">Subdivision 1.<span class="headnote">Restriction; exceptions.</span></h2>
          <p class="in">It is unlawful for a school district or other governmental subdivision or state agency to levy taxes for or to contribute public funds to a supplemental pension or deferred compensation plan that is established, maintained, and operated in addition to a primary pension program for the benefit of the governmental subdivision employees other than:</p>
          <p class="in">(1) to a supplemental pension plan that was established, maintained, and operated before May 6, 1971;</p>
          <p class="in">(2) to a plan that provides solely for group health, hospital, disability, or death benefits;</p>
          <p class="in">(3) to the individual retirement account plan established by chapter 354B;</p>
          <p class="in">(4) to a plan that provides solely for severance pay under section <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/statutes/cite/465.72">465.72</a> to a retiring or terminating employee;</p>
          <p class="in">(5) to a deferred compensation plan defined in subdivision 3;</p>
          <p class="in">(6) for personnel employed by the Board of Trustees of the Minnesota State Colleges and Universities and not covered by clause (5), to the supplemental retirement plan under chapter 354C, if the supplemental plan coverage is provided for in a personnel policy or in the collective bargaining agreement of the public employer with the exclusive representative of the covered employees in an appropriate unit, in an amount matching employee contributions on a dollar for dollar basis, but not to exceed an employer contribution of $2,700 a year for each employee;</p>
          <p class="in">(7) to a supplemental plan or to a governmental trust to save for postretirement health care expenses qualified for tax-preferred treatment under the Internal Revenue Code, if the supplemental plan coverage is provided for in a personnel policy or in the collective bargaining agreement of a public employer with the exclusive representative of the covered employees in an appropriate unit;</p>
          <p class="in">(8) to the laborers national industrial pension fund or to a laborers local pension fund for the employees of a governmental subdivision who are covered by a collective bargaining agreement that provides for coverage by that fund and that sets forth a fund contribution rate, but not to exceed an employer contribution of $10,000 per year per employee;</p>
          <p class="in">(9) to the plumbers and pipefitters national pension fund or to a plumbers and pipefitters local pension fund for the employees of a governmental subdivision who are covered by a collective bargaining agreement that provides for coverage by that fund and that sets forth a fund contribution rate, but not to exceed an employer contribution of $5,000 per year per employee;</p>
          <p class="in">(10) to the international union of operating engineers pension fund for the employees of a governmental subdivision who are covered by a collective bargaining agreement that provides for coverage by that fund and that sets forth a fund contribution rate, but not to exceed an employer contribution of $10,000 per year per employee;</p>
          <p class="in">(11) to the International Association of Machinists national pension fund for the employees of a governmental subdivision who are covered by a collective bargaining agreement that provides for coverage by that fund and that sets forth a fund contribution rate, but not to exceed an employer contribution of $5,000 per year per employee;</p>
          <p class="in">(12) for employees of United Hospital District, Blue Earth, to the state of Minnesota deferred compensation program, if the employee makes a contribution, in an amount that does not exceed the total percentage of covered salary under section <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/statutes/cite/353.27">353.27</a>, subdivisions 3 and 3a;</p>
          <p>(13) to the alternative retirement plans established by the Hennepin County Medical Center under section <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/statutes/cite/383B.914#stat.383B.914.5">383B.914, subdivision 5</a>;</p>
          <p>(14) to the International Brotherhood of Teamsters Central States pension plan for fixed-route bus drivers employed by the St. Cloud Metropolitan Transit Commission who are members of the International Brotherhood of Teamsters Local 638 by virtue of that employment; or</p>
          <p>(15) to a supplemental plan organized and operated under the Internal Revenue Code, as amended, that is wholly and solely funded by the employee's accumulated sick leave, accumulated vacation leave, and accumulated severance pay.</p>
        </div>
        <div class="subd" id="stat.356.24.1a">
          <a title="Link to Subd. 1a." class="permalink" href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/statutes/2024/cite/356/full#stat.356.24.1a">§</a>
          <h2 class="subd_no">Subd. 1a.</h2>
          <p>[Repealed, <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/laws/2000/0/461/#laws.13.4.0">2000 c 461 art 13 s 4</a>]</p>
        </div>
        <div class="subd" id="stat.356.24.1b">
          <a title="Link to Subd. 1b." class="permalink" href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/statutes/2024/cite/356/full#stat.356.24.1b">§</a>
          <h2 class="subd_no">Subd. 1b.<span class="headnote">Vendor restrictions.</span></h2>
          <p>A personnel policy for unrepresented employees, a collective bargaining agreement for represented employees, or a school board for school district employees may establish limits on the number of vendors of plans covered by the exceptions set forth in subdivision 1 that it will utilize and conditions under which those vendors may contact employees both during working hours and after working hours.</p>
        </div>
        <div class="subd" id="stat.356.24.1c">
          <a title="Link to Subd. 1c." class="permalink" href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/statutes/2024/cite/356/full#stat.356.24.1c">§</a>
          <h2 class="subd_no">Subd. 1c.<span class="headnote">State Board of Investment review.</span></h2>
          <p>(a) Any insurance company, mutual fund company, or similar company providing investments eligible under section 403(b) of the Internal Revenue Code and eligible to receive employer contributions under this section may request the State Board of Investment, in conjunction with the Department of Commerce, to review the financial standing of the company, the competitiveness of its investment options and returns, and the level of all charges and fees impacting those returns.</p>
          <p>(b) The State Board of Investment may establish a fee for each review. The State Board of Investment must maintain and have available a list of all reviewed companies.</p>
          <p>(c) In reviewing companies under this section, the State Board of Investment must not be considered to be acting as a fiduciary or to be engaged in a fiduciary activity under chapter 356A or common law.</p>
        </div>
        <div class="subd" id="stat.356.24.2">
          <a title="Link to Subd. 2." class="permalink" href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/statutes/2024/cite/356/full#stat.356.24.2">§</a>
          <h2 class="subd_no">Subd. 2.</h2>
          <p>MS 2018 [Repealed, <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/laws/2020/0/108/#laws.2.3.0">2020 c 108 art 2 s 3</a>]</p>
        </div>
        <div class="subd" id="stat.356.24.3">
          <a title="Link to Subd. 3." class="permalink" href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/statutes/2024/cite/356/full#stat.356.24.3">§</a>
          <h2 class="subd_no">Subd. 3.<span class="headnote">Deferred compensation plan.</span></h2>
          <p>(a) As used in this section:</p>
          <p>(1) "deferred compensation plan" means a plan that satisfies the requirements of this subdivision;</p>
          <p>(2) "plan administrator" means the individual or entity defined as the plan administrator in the plan document for the Minnesota deferred compensation plan under section <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/statutes/cite/352.965">352.965 </a>or a deferred compensation plan under section 457(b) of the Internal Revenue Code; and</p>
          <p>(3) "vendor" means the provider of an annuity contract, custodial account, or retirement income account under a tax-sheltered annuity plan under section 403(b) of the Internal Revenue Code.</p>
          <p>(b) The plan is:</p>
          <p>(1) the Minnesota deferred compensation plan under section <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/statutes/cite/352.965">352.965</a>;</p>
          <p>(2) a tax-sheltered annuity plan under section 403(b) of the Internal Revenue Code; or</p>
          <p>(3) a deferred compensation plan under section 457(b) of the Internal Revenue Code.</p>
          <p>(c) For each investment fund available to participants under the plan, other than in a self-directed brokerage account or fixed annuity contract, the plan administrator or vendor discloses at least annually to participants a statement that sets forth (1) all fees, including administrative, maintenance, and investment fees, that impact the rate of return on each investment fund available under the plan, and (2) the rates of return for the prior one-, five-, and ten-year periods or for the life of the fund, if shorter, in an easily understandable document. The plan administrator or vendor must file a copy of this statement annually with the executive director of the Legislative Commission on Pensions and Retirement.</p>
          <p>(d) Enrollment in the plan is provided for in:</p>
          <p>(1) a personnel policy of the public employer;</p>
          <p>(2) a collective bargaining agreement between the public employer and the exclusive representative of public employees in an appropriate unit; or</p>
          <p>(3) an individual employment contract (i) between a city and a city manager or other management employee, or (ii) between a school district and a superintendent or other management employee.</p>
          <p>(e) The plan covers employees of a school district, state agency, or other governmental subdivision. The plan may cover city managers covered by an alternative retirement arrangement under section <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/statutes/cite/353.028#stat.353.028.3">353.028, subdivision 3</a>, paragraph (a) or (b), but must not cover employees of the Board of Trustees of Minnesota State Colleges and Universities who are covered by the Higher Education Supplemental Retirement Plan under chapter 354C.</p>
          <p>(f) If the public employer makes matching contributions to the plan, the matching contributions must match, on a dollar for dollar basis, employee elective deferral contributions not to exceed the lesser of (1) the maximum authorized under the policy described in paragraph (d) that provides for enrollment in the plan or program, or (2) one-half of the annual limit on elective deferrals under section 402(g) of the Internal Revenue Code. In lieu of or in addition to matching an employee's elective deferral contributions, the public employer may make employer matching contributions on behalf of an employee on account of qualified student loan payments, as defined in the Secure 2.0 Act of 2022, Public Law 117-328 (December 29, 2022), Division T, section 110, paragraph (b), and any regulations adopted thereunder. The employer matching contributions on account of an employee's qualified student loan payments plus any employer matching contributions that match an employee's elective deferral contributions must not exceed, for the year, the lesser of (1) the maximum authorized under the policy described in paragraph (d) that provides for enrollment in the plan or program, (2) one-half of the annual limit on elective deferrals under section 402(g) of the Internal Revenue Code, or (3) the employee's compensation for the year.</p>
          <p>(g) Contributions to the plan may include contributions deducted from an employee's sick leave, accumulated vacation leave, or accumulated severance pay, whether characterized as employee contributions or nonelective employer contributions, up to applicable limits under the Internal Revenue Code. Such contributions are not subject to the match requirement and limit in paragraph (f).</p>
        </div>
      </div>

			">

      <div class="section" id="stat.356.245">
        <h1 class="shn">356.245 LOCAL ELECTED OFFICIALS.</h1>
        <p>An elected official who is covered by section <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/statutes/cite/353.01#stat.353.01.2a">353.01, subdivision 2a</a>, or <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/statutes/cite/353D.01">353D.01</a>, subdivision 2, whichever applies, is eligible to participate in a deferred compensation plan under section <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/statutes/cite/356.24">356.24</a>. The applicable local governmental unit may make the matching employer contributions authorized by that section on the part of a participating elected official.</p>
      </div>
      <div class="history" id="stat.356.245.history
			">
        <h2>History: </h2>
        <p class="first"><a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/laws/1988/0/709/#laws.9.3.0">1988 c 709 art 9 s 3</a>; <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/laws/2002/0/392/#laws.11.17.0">2002 c 392 art 11 s 17</a>; <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/laws/2015/0/68/#laws.13.46.0">2015 c 68 art 13 s 46</a></p>
      </div>
      <div class="section" id="stat.356.25">
        <h1 class="shn">356.25 LOCAL GOVERNMENTAL PENSION FUND PROHIBITIONS; EXCLUSIONS.</h1>
        <p>Notwithstanding any other provision of law or charter to the contrary, no city, county, public agency or instrumentality, or other political subdivision is required or permitted to establish for any of its employees a local pension plan or fund financed in whole or in part from public funds, other than:</p>
        <p>(1) a supplemental pension or deferred compensation plan authorized under section <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/statutes/cite/356.24">356.24</a>; or</p>
        <p>(2) a firefighters relief association that is established under chapter 424A and is governed by sections <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/statutes/cite/424A.091">424A.091</a> to <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/statutes/cite/424A.096">424A.096</a>.</p>
      </div>
      <div class="history" id="stat.356.25.history
			">
        <h2>History: </h2>
        <p class="first"><a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/laws/1975/0/405/#laws.0.1.0">1975 c 405 s 1</a>; <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/laws/1977/0/429/#laws.0.63.0">1977 c 429 s 63</a>; <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/laws/1981/0/224/#laws.0.173.0">1981 c 224 s 173</a>; <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/laws/1984/0/655/#laws.1.60.0">1984 c 655 art 1 s 60</a>; <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/laws/2002/0/392/#laws.10.2.0">2002 c 392 art 10 s 2</a>; art 11 s 18; <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/laws/2013/0/111/#laws.5.80.0">2013 c 111 art 5 s 80</a>;<a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/laws/2024/0/102/#laws.2.31.0">2024 c 102 art 2 s 31</a> </p>
      </div>
      <div class="sr" id="stat.356.26"><b>356.26</b> [Repealed, <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/laws/1976/0/129/#laws.0.1.0">1976 c 129 s 1</a>]</div>
      <div class="section" id="stat.356.27">
        <h1 class="shn">356.27 CITY OF ST. PAUL AND INDEPENDENT SCHOOL DISTRICT NO. 625, ST. PAUL; CONTRIBUTIONS TO MULTIEMPLOYER PLANS.</h1>
        <div class="subd" id="stat.356.27.1">
          <a title="Link to Subdivision 1." class="permalink" href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/statutes/2024/cite/356/full#stat.356.27.1">§</a>
          <h2 class="subd_no">Subdivision 1.<span class="headnote">Definitions.</span></h2>
          <p>(a) For purposes of this section, the terms defined in this subdivision have the meanings given them.</p>
          <p>(b) "Building and construction trades" means categories of employees who perform building construction, maintenance, or inspection services, including:</p>
          <p>(1) bricklayers;</p>
          <p>(2) carpenters;</p>
          <p>(3) cement masons;</p>
          <p>(4) electricians;</p>
          <p>(5) elevator constructors;</p>
          <p>(6) glaziers;</p>
          <p>(7) laborers;</p>
          <p>(8) operating engineers;</p>
          <p>(9) painters;</p>
          <p>(10) pipefitters;</p>
          <p>(11) plasterers;</p>
          <p>(12) plumbers;</p>
          <p>(13) roofers;</p>
          <p>(14) sheet metal workers; and</p>
          <p>(15) sprinkler fitters.</p>
          <p class="bl">Building and construction trades does not include machinists or teamsters.</p>
          <p>(c) "Employers" means the city of St. Paul and Independent School District No. 625, St. Paul.</p>
          <p>(d) "Grandfathered trades employees" means trades employees on whose behalf an employer made contributions on or before June 30, 2020, to PERA and to one or more multiemployer plans other than as provided in section <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/statutes/cite/356.24#stat.356.24.1">356.24, subdivision 1</a>, clause (8), (9), or (10).</p>
          <p>(e) "Multiemployer plan" means a plan or fund subject to the federal Employee Retirement Income Security Act of 1974, as amended, to which more than one employer is required to contribute and that is maintained pursuant to one or more collective bargaining agreements between one or more labor organizations and more than one employer. For purposes of this section, a multiemployer plan may be: (1) either a defined benefit pension plan or a defined contribution retirement plan; and (2) either a plan that covers employees in one or more local units in the state of Minnesota or a plan that covers union employees nationwide.</p>
          <p>(f) "PERA" means the Public Employees Retirement Association general plan established under chapter 353.</p>
          <p>(g) "Trades employees" means employees principally employed in one of the building and construction trades.</p>
        </div>
        <div class="subd" id="stat.356.27.2">
          <a title="Link to Subd. 2." class="permalink" href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/statutes/2024/cite/356/full#stat.356.27.2">§</a>
          <h2 class="subd_no">Subd. 2.<span class="headnote">Negotiating over contributions to multiemployer plans authorized.</span></h2>
          <p>The employers are authorized to negotiate, with labor organizations representing trades employees, collective bargaining agreements that provide for contributions to multiemployer plans on the basis of hours worked or paid. Any provision must identify each multiemployer plan to which contributions are to be made and, beginning with any such collective bargaining agreement or renewal thereof entered into after June 30, 2020, must include the employer identification number and plan number unique to the plan.</p>
        </div>
        <div class="subd" id="stat.356.27.3">
          <a title="Link to Subd. 3." class="permalink" href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/statutes/2024/cite/356/full#stat.356.27.3">§</a>
          <h2 class="subd_no">Subd. 3.<span class="headnote">Participation in PERA.</span></h2>
          <p>(a) In connection with services performed for an employer under a collective bargaining agreement authorized by subdivision 2, a trades employee first hired by the employer on or after July 1, 2020, shall not participate in PERA, except for a trades employee whose employer makes contributions on behalf of the trades employee to PERA and to one or more multiemployer plans as provided in section <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/statutes/cite/356.24">356.24</a>, subdivision 1, clause (8), (9), or (10).</p>
          <p>(b) Grandfathered trades employees shall continue to participate in PERA according to chapter 353 and in one or more multiemployer plans pursuant to a collective bargaining agreement authorized by subdivision 2. Participation shall not be subject to section <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/statutes/cite/356.24">356.24</a>.</p>
        </div>
        <div class="subd" id="stat.356.27.4">
          <a title="Link to Subd. 4." class="permalink" href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/statutes/2024/cite/356/full#stat.356.27.4">§</a>
          <h2 class="subd_no">Subd. 4.<span class="headnote">Employer's reporting obligation.</span></h2>
          <p>(a) If an employer negotiates a collective bargaining agreement authorized by subdivision 2 that covers grandfathered trades employees, the employer shall annually submit a report that satisfies the requirements of paragraph (b) to the executive director of the Legislative Commission on Pensions and Retirement annually, no later than 60 days after the end of the employer's fiscal year.</p>
          <p>(b) The report shall provide for each labor organization the number of grandfathered trades employees for whom the employer made contributions during the prior fiscal year.</p>
          <p>(c) After receiving a report from an employer, the executive director of the Legislative Commission on Pensions and Retirement may request additional information that the employer shall promptly provide.</p>
          <p>(d) The reporting obligation expires upon submission of a report for the last fiscal year in which the employer makes a contribution to PERA with respect to a grandfathered trades employee.</p>
        </div>
      </div>
      <div class="history" id="stat.356.27.history
			">
        <h2>History: </h2>
        <p class="first">
          <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/laws/2020/0/108/#laws.2.2.0">2020 c 108 art 2 s 2</a>
        </p>
      </div>
      <h1 class="fgh">PUBLIC RETIREMENT PLAN PORTABILITY</h1>
      <div class="section" id="stat.356.30">
        <h1 class="shn">356.30 COMBINED SERVICE ANNUITY.</h1>
        <div class="subd" id="stat.356.30.1">
          <a title="Link to Subdivision 1." class="permalink" href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/statutes/2024/cite/356/full#stat.356.30.1">§</a>
          <h2 class="subd_no">Subdivision 1.<span class="headnote">Eligibility; computation of annuity.</span></h2>
          <p>(a) Notwithstanding any provisions of the laws governing the covered retirement plans listed in subdivision 3, a person may elect to receive, upon retirement, a retirement annuity from each covered retirement plan, subject to the provisions of paragraph (b), if the person has:</p>
          <p>(1) allowable service in any two or more of the covered plans;</p>
          <p>(2) at least one-half year of allowable service in each covered plan, based on the allowable service in each plan;</p>
          <p>(3) total allowable service that equals or exceeds the longest service credit vesting requirement of the applicable retirement plan; and</p>
          <p>(4) not begun to receive an annuity from any covered plan or made application for benefits from each applicable plan and the retirement annuity effective dates of each plan are within a one-year period.</p>
          <p>(b) If all requirements in paragraph (a) have been satisfied, the retirement annuity from each plan must be based upon the allowable service, accrual rates, and average salary in the applicable plan except as further specified or modified in the following clauses:</p>
          <p>(1) the laws governing annuities must be the law in effect on the date of termination from the last period of public service under a covered retirement plan with which the person earned a minimum of one-half year of allowable service credit during that employment;</p>
          <p>(2) the average salary used to calculate the annuity for each formula plan must be based on the employee's highest five successive years of covered salary during the entire service in covered plans;</p>
          <p>(3) the accrual rates under each plan must be the percentages prescribed by each plan's formula in effect for the respective years of allowable service from one plan to the next, recognizing all previous allowable service with the other covered plans;</p>
          <p>(4) the allowable service in all the covered plans must be combined in determining eligibility for and the application of each plan's provisions with respect to reduction in the annuity amount for retirement prior to normal retirement age; and</p>
          <p>(5) the annuity amount payable for any allowable service under a nonformula plan that is a covered plan must not be affected, but such service and covered salary must be used in the above calculation.</p>
          <p>(c) If a person eligible for an annuity under paragraph (a) from each covered plan terminates all public service, the deferred annuity must be augmented from the date of termination until the earlier of:</p>
          <p>(1) the effective date of retirement; or</p>
          <p>(2) December 31, 2018, for the Minnesota State Retirement System and the Public Employees Retirement Association or June 30, 2019, for the Teachers Retirement Association and the St. Paul Teachers Retirement Association.</p>
          <p>A deferred annuity must not be augmented after the applicable dates under clause (2). The appropriate rate of augmentation is the rate in effect on the date on which the person entered into public employment and subsequently adjusted according to the laws governing each covered plan, as applicable.</p>
          <p>(d) This section does not apply to any person whose final termination from the last public service under a covered plan was before May 1, 1975.</p>
          <p>(e) For the purpose of computing annuities under this section:</p>
          <p>(1) the judges retirement fund accrual rate must not exceed 3.2 percent per year of service for any year of service or fraction thereof;</p>
          <p>(2) the public employees police and fire plan and the State Patrol retirement plan accrual rate must not exceed 3.0 percent per year of service for any year of service or fraction thereof;</p>
          <p>(3) the legislators retirement plan accrual rate must not exceed 2.5 percent, but this limit does not apply to the adjustment provided under section <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/statutes/cite/3A.02#stat.3A.02.1">3A.02, subdivision 1</a>, paragraph (c); and</p>
          <p>(4) any other covered plan's accrual rate must not exceed 2.7 percent per year of service for any year of service or fraction thereof.</p>
          <p>(f) Any period of time for which a person has credit in more than one of the covered plans must be used only once for the purpose of determining total allowable service.</p>
          <p>(g) If the period of duplicated service credit is more than one-half year, or the person has credit for more than one-half year, with each of the plans, each plan must apply its formula to a prorated service credit for the period of duplicated service based on a fraction of the salary on which deductions were paid to that fund for the period divided by the total salary on which deductions were paid to all plans for the period.</p>
          <p>(h) If the period of duplicated service credit is less than one-half year, or when added to other service credit with that plan is less than one-half year, the service credit must be ignored and a refund of contributions made to the person in accord with that plan's refund provisions.</p>
        </div>
        <div class="subd" id="stat.356.30.2">
          <a title="Link to Subd. 2." class="permalink" href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/statutes/2024/cite/356/full#stat.356.30.2">§</a>
          <h2 class="subd_no">Subd. 2.<span class="headnote">Repayment of refunds.</span></h2>
          <p>A person who has service credit in one of the retirement plans enumerated in subdivision 3 and who is employed or was formerly employed in a position covered by one of these covered plans but also has received a refund from any other of these covered plans, may repay the refund to the respective plan under terms and conditions that are consistent with the laws governing the other plan, except that the person need not be a currently contributing member of the plan to which the refund is repaid at the time the repayment is made. Unless otherwise provided by statute, the repayment of a refund under this subdivision may only be made within six months following termination of employment from a position covered by one of the covered plans enumerated in subdivision 3 or before the date of retirement from the plan to which the refund is repaid, whichever is earlier.</p>
        </div>
        <div class="subd" id="stat.356.30.2a">
          <a title="Link to Subd. 2a." class="permalink" href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/statutes/2024/cite/356/full#stat.356.30.2a">§</a>
          <h2 class="subd_no">Subd. 2a.<span class="headnote">Purchases of prior service.</span></h2>
          <p>If a purchase of prior service is made under the provisions of Laws 1988, chapter 709, article 3, or any similar special or general law provision which allows a purchase of service credit in any of the retirement plans enumerated in subdivision 3, the amount of required reserves calculated as prescribed in Laws 1988, chapter 709, article 3, must be paid to each plan based on the amount of benefit increase payable from that plan as a result of the purchase of prior service.</p>
        </div>
        <div class="subd" id="stat.356.30.3">
          <a title="Link to Subd. 3." class="permalink" href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/statutes/2024/cite/356/full#stat.356.30.3">§</a>
          <h2 class="subd_no">Subd. 3.<span class="headnote">Covered plans.</span></h2>
          <p>This section applies to the following retirement plans:</p>
          <p>(1) the general state employees retirement plan of the Minnesota State Retirement System, established under chapter 352;</p>
          <p>(2) the correctional state employees retirement plan of the Minnesota State Retirement System, established under chapter 352;</p>
          <p>(3) the unclassified employees retirement program, established under chapter 352D;</p>
          <p>(4) the State Patrol retirement plan, established under chapter 352B;</p>
          <p>(5) the legislators retirement plan, established under chapter 3A, including constitutional officers as specified in that chapter;</p>
          <p>(6) the general employees retirement plan of the Public Employees Retirement Association, established under chapter 353;</p>
          <p>(7) the public employees police and fire retirement plan of the Public Employees Retirement Association, established under chapter 353;</p>
          <p>(8) the local government correctional service retirement plan of the Public Employees Retirement Association, established under chapter 353E;</p>
          <p>(9) the Teachers Retirement Association, established under chapter 354;</p>
          <p>(10) the St. Paul Teachers Retirement Fund Association, established under chapter 354A; and</p>
          <p>(11) the judges retirement fund, established by chapter 490.</p>
        </div>
      </div>
      <div class="history" id="stat.356.30.history
			">
