      <div class="sr" id="stat.356.301"><b>356.301</b> [Repealed, <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/laws/1987/0/284/#laws.8.3.0">1987 c 284 art 8 s 3</a>]</div>
      <div class="section" id="stat.356.302">
        <h1 class="shn">356.302 DISABILITY BENEFIT WITH COMBINED SERVICE.</h1>
        <div class="subd" id="stat.356.302.1">
          <a title="Link to Subdivision 1." class="permalink" href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/statutes/2024/cite/356/full#stat.356.302.1">§</a>
          <h2 class="subd_no">Subdivision 1.<span class="headnote">Definitions.</span></h2>
          <p>(a) The terms used in this section are defined in this subdivision.</p>
          <p>(b) "Average salary" means the highest average of covered salary for the appropriate period of credited service that is required for the calculation of a disability benefit by the covered retirement plan and that is drawn from any period of credited service and successive years of covered salary in a covered retirement plan.</p>
          <p>(c) "Covered retirement plan" or "plan" means a retirement plan listed in subdivision 7.</p>
          <p>(d) "Duty-related" means a disabling illness or injury that occurred while the person was actively engaged in employment duties or that arose out of the person's active employment duties.</p>
          <p>(e) "General employee retirement plan" means a covered retirement plan listed in subdivision 7, clauses (1) to (6) and (11).</p>
          <p>(f) "Occupationally disabled" means the condition of having a medically determinable physical or mental impairment that makes a person unable to satisfactorily perform the minimum requirements of the person's employment position or a substantially similar employment position.</p>
          <p>(g) "Public safety employee retirement plan" means a covered retirement plan listed in subdivision 7, clauses (7) to (10).</p>
          <p>(h) "Totally and permanently disabled" means the condition of having a medically determinable physical or mental impairment that makes a person unable to engage in any substantial gainful activity and that is expected to continue or has continued for a period of at least one year or that is expected to result directly in the person's death.</p>
        </div>
        <div class="subd" id="stat.356.302.2">
          <a title="Link to Subd. 2." class="permalink" href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/statutes/2024/cite/356/full#stat.356.302.2">§</a>
          <h2 class="subd_no">Subd. 2.<span class="headnote">Entitlement.</span></h2>
          <p>Notwithstanding any provision of law to the contrary governing any covered retirement plan, a member of a covered retirement plan may receive a combined service disability benefit from each covered retirement plan in which the person has credit for at least one-half year of allowable service if that person meets the applicable qualifying conditions. Subdivision 3 applies to a member of a general employee retirement plan. Subdivision 4 applies to a member of a public safety employee retirement plan. Subdivision 5 applies to a member of a covered retirement plan with both general employee and public safety employee retirement plan service.</p>
        </div>
        <div class="subd" id="stat.356.302.3">
          <a title="Link to Subd. 3." class="permalink" href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/statutes/2024/cite/356/full#stat.356.302.3">§</a>
          <h2 class="subd_no">Subd. 3.<span class="headnote">General employee plan eligibility requirements.</span></h2>
          <p>A disabled member of a covered retirement plan who has credit for allowable service in a combination of general employee retirement plans is entitled to a combined service disability benefit if the member:</p>
          <p>(1) is less than the normal retirement age on the date of the application for the disability benefit;</p>
          <p>(2) has become totally and permanently disabled;</p>
          <p>(3) has credit for allowable service in any combination of general employee retirement plans totaling at least the number of years required by the applicable retirement plan with the longest service credit requirement for disability benefit receipt;</p>
          <p>(4) has credit for at least one-half year of allowable service with the current general employee retirement plan before the commencement of the disability;</p>
          <p>(5) has at least three continuous years of allowable service credit by the general employee retirement plan or has at least a total of three years of allowable service credit by a combination of general employee retirement plans in a 72-month period during which no interruption of allowable service credit from a termination of employment exceeded 29 days; and</p>
          <p>(6) was not receiving a retirement annuity or disability benefit from any covered general employee retirement plan at the time of the commencement of the disability.</p>
        </div>
        <div class="subd" id="stat.356.302.4">
          <a title="Link to Subd. 4." class="permalink" href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/statutes/2024/cite/356/full#stat.356.302.4">§</a>
          <h2 class="subd_no">Subd. 4.<span class="headnote">Public safety plan eligibility requirements.</span></h2>
          <p>A disabled member of a covered retirement plan who has credit for allowable service in a combination of public safety employee retirement plans is entitled to a combined service disability benefit if the member:</p>
          <p>(1) has become occupationally disabled;</p>
          <p>(2) has credit for allowable service in any combination of public safety employee retirement plans totaling at least the minimum period of service credit required by the applicable retirement plan with the longest service credit eligibility requirement for the receipt of a duty-related disability benefit if the disability is duty-related or totaling at least the minimum period of service credit required by the applicable retirement plan with the longest service credit eligibility requirement for a disability benefit that is not duty-related if the disability is not duty-related;</p>
          <p>(3) has credit for at least one-half year of allowable service with the current public safety employee retirement plan before the commencement of the disability; and</p>
          <p>(4) was not receiving a retirement annuity or disability benefit from any covered public safety employee retirement plan at the time of the commencement of the disability.</p>
        </div>
        <div class="subd" id="stat.356.302.5">
          <a title="Link to Subd. 5." class="permalink" href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/statutes/2024/cite/356/full#stat.356.302.5">§</a>
          <h2 class="subd_no">Subd. 5.<span class="headnote">General and public safety plan eligibility requirements.</span></h2>
          <p>A disabled member of a covered retirement plan who has credit for allowable service in a combination of both a public safety employee retirement plan and general employee retirement plan must meet the qualifying requirements in subdivisions 3 and 4 to receive a combined service disability benefit from the applicable general employee and public safety employee retirement plans, except that the person need only be a member of a covered retirement plan at the time of the commencement of the disability, that the person must have allowable service credit for the applicable retirement plan with the longest service credit eligibility requirement for the receipt of a disability benefit, and that the minimum allowable service requirements of subdivisions 3, clauses (3) and (5), and 4, clauses (3) and (4), may be met in any combination of covered retirement plans.</p>
        </div>
        <div class="subd" id="stat.356.302.6">
          <a title="Link to Subd. 6." class="permalink" href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/statutes/2024/cite/356/full#stat.356.302.6">§</a>
          <h2 class="subd_no">Subd. 6.<span class="headnote">Combined service disability benefit computation.</span></h2>
          <p>(a) The combined service disability benefit from each covered retirement plan must be based on the allowable service in each retirement plan, except as specified in paragraphs (b) to (f).</p>
          <p>(b) The disability benefit must be governed by the law in effect for each covered retirement plan on the date of the commencement of the member's most recent qualifying disability as a member of a covered retirement plan.</p>
          <p>(c) All plans must base the disability benefit on the same average salary figure to the extent practicable.</p>
          <p>(d) If the method of the covered retirement plan used to compute a disability benefit varies based on the length of allowable service credit, the benefit accrual formula percentages used by the plan must recognize the allowable service credit in the plan as a continuation of any previous allowable service credit with other covered retirement plans.</p>
          <p>(e) If the covered retirement plan is a defined benefit or formula plan and the method used to compute a disability benefit does not vary based on the length of allowable service credit, the portion of the specified benefit amount from the plan must bear the same proportion to the total specified benefit amount as the allowable service credit in that plan bears to the total allowable service credit in all covered retirement plans. If the covered retirement plan is a defined contribution or nonformula plan, the disability benefit amount for allowable service under the plan is not affected, but the service and the covered salary under the plan must be used as applicable in calculations by other covered retirement plans.</p>
          <p>(f) A period for which a person has allowable service credit in more than one covered retirement plan must be used only once in determining the total allowable service credit for calculating the combined service disability benefit, with any period of duplicated service credit handled as provided in section <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/statutes/cite/356.30#stat.356.30.1">356.30, subdivision 1</a>, paragraphs (g) and (h).</p>
          <p>(g) If a person is entitled to a minimum benefit payable from one of the public pension plans enumerated in section <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/statutes/cite/356.30#stat.356.30.3">356.30, subdivision 3</a>, the person may receive additional credit for only those years of service in another covered pension plan that, when added to the years of service in the pension plan that is paying the minimum benefit, exceed the years of service on which the minimum benefit is based.</p>
          <p>(h) A partially employed recipient of a disability benefit must have any current reemployment income plus the total disability payments from all plans enumerated in subdivision 7 added together, and then compared to their final salary rate as a public employee. If current income plus the total disability payments exceed the final salary of the person at the time of retirement, then disability benefit payments from all the plans must be reduced on a prorated basis relative to the years of service in each fund so that earnings plus benefit payments do not exceed the final salary rate.</p>
        </div>
        <div class="subd" id="stat.356.302.7">
          <a title="Link to Subd. 7." class="permalink" href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/statutes/2024/cite/356/full#stat.356.302.7">§</a>
          <h2 class="subd_no">Subd. 7.<span class="headnote">Covered retirement plans.</span></h2>
          <p>This section applies to the following retirement plans:</p>
          <p>(1) the general state employees retirement plan of the Minnesota State Retirement System, established by chapter 352;</p>
          <p>(2) the unclassified state employees retirement program of the Minnesota State Retirement System, established by chapter 352D;</p>
          <p>(3) the general employees retirement plan of the Public Employees Retirement Association, established by chapter 353;</p>
          <p>(4) the Teachers Retirement Association, established by chapter 354;</p>
          <p>(5) the St. Paul Teachers Retirement Fund Association, established by chapter 354A;</p>
          <p>(6) the state correctional employees retirement plan of the Minnesota State Retirement System, established by chapter 352;</p>
          <p>(7) the State Patrol retirement plan, established by chapter 352B;</p>
          <p>(8) the public employees police and fire plan of the Public Employees Retirement Association, established by chapter 353;</p>
          <p>(9) the local government correctional service retirement plan of the Public Employees Retirement Association, established by chapter 353E; and</p>
          <p>(10) the judges retirement plan, established by chapter 490.</p>
        </div>
      </div>
      <div class="history" id="stat.356.302.history
			">
        <h2>History: </h2>
        <p class="first"><a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/laws/1987/0/284/#laws.8.1.0">1987 c 284 art 8 s 1</a>; <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/laws/1988/0/709/#laws.5.39.0">1988 c 709 art 5 s 39</a>,40; <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/laws/1989/0/319/#laws.5.5.0">1989 c 319 art 5 s 5</a>; <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/laws/1990/0/570/#laws.12.56.0">1990 c 570 art 12 s 56</a>,57; <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/laws/1992/0/432/#laws.2.46.0">1992 c 432 art 2 s 46</a>; <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/laws/1993/0/307/#laws.2.18.0">1993 c 307 art 2 s 18</a>; art 4 s 51; <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/laws/1995/0/141/#laws.3.20.0">1995 c 141 art 3 s 20</a>; <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/laws/1999/0/222/#laws.2.18.0">1999 c 222 art 2 s 18</a>; <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/laws/2000/0/461/#laws.3.46.0">2000 c 461 art 3 s 46</a>; <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/laws/2002/0/392/#laws.11.20.0">2002 c 392 art 11 s 20</a>; <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/laws/2004/0/267/#laws.8.33.0">2004 c 267 art 8 s 33</a>; <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/laws/2006/0/271/#laws.11.48.0">2006 c 271 art 11 s 48</a>; <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/laws/2006/0/277/#laws.3.36.0">2006 c 277 art 3 s 36</a>; <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/laws/2010/0/359/#laws.1.71.0">2010 c 359 art 1 s 71</a>-73; art 12 s 26,27; <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/laws/2014/0/296/#laws.6.37.0">2014 c 296 art 6 s 37</a>,49; <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/laws/2015/0/68/#laws.14.20.0">2015 c 68 art 14 s 20</a></p>
      </div>
      <div class="section" id="stat.356.303">
        <h1 class="shn">356.303 SURVIVOR BENEFIT WITH COMBINED SERVICE.</h1>
        <div class="subd" id="stat.356.303.1">
          <a title="Link to Subdivision 1." class="permalink" href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/statutes/2024/cite/356/full#stat.356.303.1">§</a>
          <h2 class="subd_no">Subdivision 1.<span class="headnote">Definitions.</span></h2>
          <p>(a) The terms used in this section are defined in this subdivision.</p>
          <p>(b) "Average salary" means the highest average of covered salary for the appropriate period of credited service that is required for the calculation of a survivor annuity or a survivor benefit, whichever applies, by the covered retirement plan and that is drawn from any period of credited service and covered salary in a covered retirement plan.</p>
          <p>(c) "Covered retirement plan" or "plan" means a retirement plan enumerated in subdivision 4.</p>
          <p>(d) "Deceased member" means a person who on the date of death was an active member of a covered retirement plan and who has reached the minimum age, if any, that is required by the covered retirement plan as part of qualifying for a survivor annuity or survivor benefit.</p>
          <p>(e) "Surviving child" means a child of a deceased member (1) who is unmarried; (2) who has not reached age 18, or, if a full-time student, who has not reached a higher age as specified by the applicable covered retirement plan; and (3) if specified by that plan, who was actually dependent on the deceased member for a specified proportion of support before the deceased member's death. "Surviving child" includes a natural child, an adopted child, or a child of a deceased member who is conceived during the member's lifetime and who is born after the member's death.</p>
          <p>(f) "Surviving spouse" means the legally married spouse of the deceased member who was residing with the deceased member on the date of death and who, if specified by the applicable covered retirement plan, had been married to the deceased member for a specified period of time before the death of the deceased member.</p>
          <p>(g) "Survivor annuity" means the entitlement to a future amount payable to a survivor as the remainder interest of an optional annuity form implied by law as having been chosen by a deceased member before the date of death and effective on the date of death or provided automatically.</p>
          <p>(h) "Survivor benefit" means an entitlement to a future amount payable to a survivor that is not included in the definition of a survivor annuity.</p>
        </div>
        <div class="subd" id="stat.356.303.2">
          <a title="Link to Subd. 2." class="permalink" href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/statutes/2024/cite/356/full#stat.356.303.2">§</a>
          <h2 class="subd_no">Subd. 2.<span class="headnote">Entitlement; eligibility.</span></h2>
          <p>Notwithstanding any provision of law to the contrary governing a covered retirement plan, a person who is the survivor of a deceased member of a covered retirement plan may receive a combined service survivor benefit from each covered retirement plan in which the deceased member had credit for at least one-half year of allowable service if the deceased member:</p>
          <p>(1) had credit for sufficient allowable service in any combination of covered retirement plans to meet the minimum allowable service credit requirement of the applicable covered retirement fund with the longest allowable service credit requirement for qualification for a survivor benefit or annuity;</p>
          <p>(2) had credit for at least one-half year of allowable service with the most recent covered retirement plan before the date of death and was an active member of that covered retirement plan on the date of death; and</p>
          <p>(3) was not receiving a retirement annuity from any covered retirement plan on the date of death.</p>
        </div>
        <div class="subd" id="stat.356.303.3">
          <a title="Link to Subd. 3." class="permalink" href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/statutes/2024/cite/356/full#stat.356.303.3">§</a>
          <h2 class="subd_no">Subd. 3.<span class="headnote">Combined service survivor benefit computation.</span></h2>
          <p>(a) The combined service survivor annuity or survivor benefit from each covered retirement plan must be based on the allowable service in each covered retirement plan, except as provided by paragraphs (b) to (f).</p>
          <p>(b) The survivor annuity or survivor benefit must be governed by the law in effect for each covered retirement plan on the date of the death of the deceased member.</p>
          <p>(c) All plans must base the survivor annuity or survivor benefit on the same average salary figure if the annuity or benefit is salary related.</p>
          <p>(d) If the method of the covered retirement plan used to compute a survivor benefit or annuity varies based on the length of allowable service credit, the benefit accrual formula percentages used by the plan must recognize the allowable service credit in the plan as a continuation of any previous allowable service credit with other covered retirement plans.</p>
          <p>(e) If the covered retirement plan is a defined benefit or formula plan and the method used to compute a survivor benefit or annuity does not vary based on the length of allowable service credit, the portion of the specified benefit or annuity amount from the covered retirement plan must bear the same proportion to the total specified benefit or annuity amount as the allowable service credit in that plan bears to the total allowable service credit in all covered retirement plans. If the covered retirement plan is a defined contribution or nonformula plan, the survivor benefit amount for allowable service under the plan is not affected, but the service and covered salary under the plan must be used in calculations by other covered retirement plans.</p>
          <p>(f) A period for which a deceased member had allowable service credit in more than one covered retirement plan must be used only once in determining the total allowable service credit for calculating the combined service survivor annuity or survivor benefit. A period of duplicated service credit must be handled as provided in section <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/statutes/cite/356.30#stat.356.30.1">356.30, subdivision 1</a>, paragraphs (g) and (h).</p>
          <p>(g) If a person is entitled to a minimum benefit payable from a public pension plan named in section <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/statutes/cite/356.30#stat.356.30.3">356.30, subdivision 3</a>, the person may receive additional credit for only those years of service in another covered pension plan that, when added to the years of service in the pension plan that is paying the minimum benefit, exceed the years of service on which the minimum benefit is based.</p>
        </div>
        <div class="subd" id="stat.356.303.4">
          <a title="Link to Subd. 4." class="permalink" href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/statutes/2024/cite/356/full#stat.356.303.4">§</a>
          <h2 class="subd_no">Subd. 4.<span class="headnote">Covered retirement plans.</span></h2>
          <p>This section applies to the following retirement plans:</p>
          <p>(1) the legislators retirement plan, established by chapter 3A;</p>
          <p>(2) the general state employees retirement plan of the Minnesota State Retirement System, established by chapter 352;</p>
          <p>(3) the correctional state employees retirement plan of the Minnesota State Retirement System, established by chapter 352;</p>
          <p>(4) the State Patrol retirement plan, established by chapter 352B;</p>
          <p>(5) the elective state officers retirement plan, established by chapter 352C;</p>
          <p>(6) the unclassified state employees retirement program, established by chapter 352D;</p>
          <p>(7) the general employees retirement plan of the Public Employees Retirement Association, established by chapter 353;</p>
          <p>(8) the public employees police and fire plan of the Public Employees Retirement Association, established by chapter 353;</p>
          <p>(9) the local government correctional service retirement plan of the Public Employees Retirement Association, established by chapter 353E;</p>
          <p>(10) the Teachers Retirement Association, established by chapter 354;</p>
          <p>(11) the St. Paul Teachers Retirement Fund Association, established by chapter 354A; and</p>
          <p>(12) the judges retirement fund, established by chapter 490.</p>
        </div>
      </div>
      <div class="history" id="stat.356.303.history
			">
        <h2>History: </h2>
        <p class="first"><a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/laws/1987/0/284/#laws.8.2.0">1987 c 284 art 8 s 2</a>; <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/laws/1989/0/319/#laws.5.6.0">1989 c 319 art 5 s 6</a>; <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/laws/1992/0/432/#laws.2.47.0">1992 c 432 art 2 s 47</a>; <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/laws/1995/0/141/#laws.3.20.0">1995 c 141 art 3 s 20</a>; <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/laws/1999/0/222/#laws.2.19.0">1999 c 222 art 2 s 19</a>; <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/laws/2000/0/461/#laws.3.46.0">2000 c 461 art 3 s 46</a>; <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/laws/2002/0/392/#laws.11.21.0">2002 c 392 art 11 s 21</a>; <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/laws/2006/0/271/#laws.11.48.0">2006 c 271 art 11 s 48</a>; <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/laws/2006/0/277/#laws.3.37.0">2006 c 277 art 3 s 37</a>; <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/laws/2010/0/359/#laws.1.74.0">2010 c 359 art 1 s 74</a>; art 12 s 28; <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/laws/2014/0/296/#laws.6.38.0">2014 c 296 art 6 s 38</a>,49; <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/laws/2015/0/68/#laws.14.21.0">2015 c 68 art 14 s 21</a>; <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/laws/2024/0/101/#laws.3.2.0">2024 c 101 art 3 s 2</a></p>
      </div>
      <div class="sr" id="stat.356.305"><b>356.305</b> [Repealed, <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/laws/2002/0/392/#laws.11.53.0">2002 c 392 art 11 s 53</a>]</div>
      <div class="sr" id="stat.356.306"><b>356.306</b> [Repealed, <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/laws/2002/0/392/#laws.11.53.0">2002 c 392 art 11 s 53</a>]</div>
      <div class="sr" id="stat.356.31"><b>356.31</b> [Repealed, <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/laws/2002/0/392/#laws.11.53.0">2002 c 392 art 11 s 53</a>]</div>
      <div class="section" id="stat.356.311">
        <h1 class="shn">356.311 COVERAGE BY MORE THAN ONE PLAN.</h1>
        <p>(a) Any person who has been a member of two or more of the retirement plans listed in paragraph (b) is entitled, when qualified, to an annuity from each fund if:</p>
        <p>(1) the person's combined service in any two or more retirement plans equals or exceeds the vesting requirement of the fund with the longest vesting requirement; and</p>
        <p>(2) the person has not taken a refund from any of the retirement plans.</p>
        <p>(b) This section applies to any defined benefit plan administered by the Minnesota State Retirement System, including the State Patrol Retirement Plan; the Public Employees Retirement Association, including the public employees police and fire plan; the Teachers Retirement Association; and the St. Paul Teachers Retirement Fund Association, except as noted in paragraph (c).</p>
        <p>(c) This section does not apply to plans providing benefits for police officers or firefighters under sections <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/statutes/cite/424A.091">424A.091</a> to <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/statutes/cite/424A.096">424A.096</a> or the Bloomington Fire Department Relief Association.</p>
        <p>(d) No portion of the service upon which the retirement annuity from one retirement plan is based shall be again used in the computation of a retirement annuity from another plan. The annuity from each plan must be determined under the laws applicable to that plan except that the requirement that a person meet the vesting requirement in any particular plan shall not apply, provided the combined service in any two or more plans equals or exceeds the vesting requirement of the plan with the longest vesting requirement.</p>
        <p>(e) Any deferred annuity payable under this section shall be subject to augmentation under the laws applicable to the deferred annuity.</p>
        <p>(f) Any person to whom an annuity is not payable under this section because the person took a refund from one of the funds shall be entitled to repay the refund in accordance with the laws governing the refund. Upon repayment, the person is entitled to annuities under this section, if the person would otherwise be entitled.</p>
      </div>
      <div class="history" id="stat.356.311.history
			">
        <h2>History: </h2>
        <p class="first">
          <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/laws/2018/0/211/#laws.5.5.0">2018 c 211 art 5 s 5</a>
        </p>
      </div>

          <h2 class="subd_no">Subd. 9.<span class="headnote">Future benefit accrual rate increases.</span></h2>
          <p>After January 2, 1998, benefit accrual rate increases under section <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/statutes/cite/352.115#stat.352.115.3">352.115, subdivision 3</a>; <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/statutes/cite/352.87">352.87, subdivision </a>3; <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/statutes/cite/352.93#stat.352.93.3">352.93, subdivision 3</a>; <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/statutes/cite/352.95#stat.352.95.1">352.95, subdivision 1</a>; <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/statutes/cite/352B.08#stat.352B.08.2">352B.08, subdivision 2</a>; <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/statutes/cite/352B.10">352B.10</a>, subdivision 1; <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/statutes/cite/353.29#stat.353.29.3">353.29, subdivision 3</a>; <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/statutes/cite/353.651#stat.353.651.3">353.651, subdivision 3</a>; <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/statutes/cite/353.656">353.656, subdivision </a>1, 1a, or 3a; <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/statutes/cite/353E.04#stat.353E.04.3">353E.04, subdivision 3</a>; <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/statutes/cite/353E.06#stat.353E.06.1">353E.06, subdivision 1</a>; <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/statutes/cite/354.44#stat.354.44.6">354.44, subdivision 6</a>; <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/statutes/cite/354A.31#stat.354A.31.4">354A.31, subdivision 4</a> or 4a; <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/statutes/cite/356.30#stat.356.30.1">356.30, subdivision 1</a>; <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/statutes/cite/490.121#stat.490.121.22">490.121, subdivision 22</a>; or <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/statutes/cite/490.124">490.124</a>, subdivision 1, must apply only to allowable service or formula service rendered after the effective date of the benefit accrual rate increase.</p>
        </div>

      <div class="section" id="stat.356.32">
        <h1 class="shn">356.32 PROPORTIONATE ANNUITY AT AGE 65.</h1>
        <div class="subd" id="stat.356.32.1">
          <a title="Link to Subdivision 1." class="permalink" href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/statutes/2024/cite/356/full#stat.356.32.1">§</a>
          <h2 class="subd_no">Subdivision 1.<span class="headnote">Proportionate retirement annuity.</span></h2>
          <p>(a) Notwithstanding any provision to the contrary of the laws governing any of the retirement funds enumerated in subdivision 2, any person who is an active member of any applicable fund, who has credit for at least one year of allowable service in one or more of the covered plans, and who terminates active service under a mandatory retirement law or policy or at the normal retirement age but not less than age 65, is entitled upon making written application on the form prescribed by the chief administrative officer of the plan to a proportionate retirement annuity from each covered plan in which the person has at least six months of allowable service credit.</p>
          <p>(b) The proportionate annuity must be calculated under the applicable laws governing annuities based upon allowable service credit at the time of retirement and the person's average salary for the highest five successive years of allowable service or the average salary for the entire period of allowable service if less than five years.</p>
          <p>(c) Nothing in this section prevents the imposition of the appropriate early retirement reduction of an annuity which commences before the normal retirement age.</p>
        </div>
        <div class="subd" id="stat.356.32.2">
          <a title="Link to Subd. 2." class="permalink" href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/statutes/2024/cite/356/full#stat.356.32.2">§</a>
          <h2 class="subd_no">Subd. 2.<span class="headnote">Covered retirement plans.</span></h2>
          <p>The provisions of this section apply to the following retirement plans:</p>
          <p>(1) the general state employees retirement plan of the Minnesota State Retirement System, established under chapter <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/statutes/cite/352">352</a>;</p>
          <p>(2) the correctional state employees retirement plan of the Minnesota State Retirement System, established under chapter <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/statutes/cite/352">352</a>;</p>
          <p>(3) the State Patrol retirement plan, established under chapter <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/statutes/cite/352B">352B</a>;</p>
          <p>(4) the general employees retirement plan of the Public Employees Retirement Association, established under chapter <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/statutes/cite/353">353</a>;</p>
          <p>(5) the public employees police and fire plan of the Public Employees Retirement Association, established under chapter <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/statutes/cite/353">353</a>;</p>
          <p>(6) the local government correctional service retirement plan of the Public Employees Retirement Association, established under chapter <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/statutes/cite/353E">353E</a>;</p>
          <p>(7) the Teachers Retirement Association, established under chapter <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/statutes/cite/354">354</a>; and</p>
          <p>(8) the St. Paul Teachers Retirement Fund Association, established under chapter <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/statutes/cite/354A">354A</a>.</p>
        </div>
      </div>
      <div class="history" id="stat.356.32.history
			">
        <h2>History: </h2>
        <p class="first"><a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/laws/1975/0/183/#laws.0.2.0">1975 c 183 s 2</a>; <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/laws/1976/0/130/#laws.0.1.0">1976 c 130 s 1</a>; <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/laws/1978/0/649/#laws.0.3.0">1978 c 649 s 3</a>; <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/laws/1978/0/796/#laws.0.44.0">1978 c 796 s 44</a>; <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/laws/1979/0/40/#laws.0.10.0">1979 c 40 s 10</a>; <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/laws/1979/0/217/#laws.0.27.0">1979 c 217 s 27</a>; <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/laws/1980/0/342/#laws.0.15.0">1980 c 342 s 15</a>; <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/laws/1981/0/37/#laws.0.2.0">1981 c 37 s 2</a>; <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/laws/1981/0/224/#laws.0.174.0">1981 c 224 s 174</a>; <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/laws/1981/0/298/#laws.0.11.0">1981 c 298 s 11</a>; <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/laws/1987/0/372/#laws.1.21.0">1987 c 372 art 1 s 21</a>; <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/laws/1989/0/319/#laws.13.93.0">1989 c 319 art 13 s 93</a>; <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/laws/1995/0/141/#laws.3.20.0">1995 c 141 art 3 s 20</a>; <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/laws/1997/0/233/#laws.1.63.0">1997 c 233 art 1 s 63</a>; <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/laws/2002/0/392/#laws.11.23.0">2002 c 392 art 11 s 23</a>; <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/laws/2009/0/169/#laws.11.2.0">2009 c 169 art 11 s 2</a>; <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/laws/2010/0/359/#laws.12.29.0">2010 c 359 art 12 s 29</a>; <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/laws/2014/0/296/#laws.6.39.0">2014 c 296 art 6 s 39</a>,49; <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/laws/2015/0/68/#laws.12.38.0">2015 c 68 art 12 s 38</a>; art 14 s 22; <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/laws/2018/0/211/#laws.13.1.0">2018 c 211 art 13 s 1</a></p>
      </div>
      <div class="sr" id="stat.356.325"><b>356.325</b> [Repealed, <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/laws/2002/0/392/#laws.11.53.0">2002 c 392 art 11 s 53</a>]</div>
      <div class="sr" id="stat.356.34"><b>356.34</b> [Repealed, <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/laws/1978/0/781/#laws.0.13.0">1978 c 781 s 13</a>]</div>
      <div class="sr" id="stat.356.35"><b>356.35</b> [Repealed, <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/laws/2002/0/392/#laws.11.53.0">2002 c 392 art 11 s 53</a>]</div>

      <div class="sr" id="stat.356.37"><b>356.37</b> [Repealed, <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/laws/2002/0/392/#laws.11.53.0">2002 c 392 art 11 s 53</a>]</div>
      <div class="sr" id="stat.356.371"><b>356.371</b> [Repealed, <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/laws/2002/0/392/#laws.11.53.0">2002 c 392 art 11 s 53</a>]</div>
      <div class="sr" id="stat.356.372"><b>356.372</b> [Repealed, <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/laws/2002/0/392/#laws.11.53.0">2002 c 392 art 11 s 53</a>]</div>
      <div class="sr" id="stat.356.38"><b>356.38</b> [Repealed, <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/laws/2002/0/392/#laws.11.53.0">2002 c 392 art 11 s 53</a>]</div>
      <div class="sr" id="stat.356.39"><b>356.39</b> [Repealed, <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/laws/2002/0/392/#laws.11.53.0">2002 c 392 art 11 s 53</a>]</div>
      <div class="section" id="stat.356.40">
        <h1 class="shn">356.40 DATE FOR PAYMENT OF ANNUITIES AND BENEFITS.</h1>
        <p>(a) Notwithstanding any law to the contrary, all annuities and benefits payable by a covered retirement fund, as defined in section <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/statutes/cite/356.30#stat.356.30.3">356.30, subdivision 3</a>, must be paid in advance for each month during the first week of that month.</p>
        <p>(b) In no event, however, may this section authorize the payment of both a retirement annuity and a surviving spouse's benefit in one month where the law governing the applicable retirement fund provides for the payment of the retired member's retirement annuity to the surviving spouse for the month in which the retired member dies.</p>
      </div>
      <div class="history" id="stat.356.40.history
			">
        <h2>History: </h2>
        <p class="first"><a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/laws/1977/0/388/#laws.0.3.0">1977 c 388 s 3</a>; <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/laws/2002/0/392/#laws.11.24.0">2002 c 392 art 11 s 24</a>; <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/laws/2015/0/68/#laws.13.47.0">2015 c 68 art 13 s 47</a></p>
      </div>
      <div class="section" id="stat.356.401">
        <h1 class="shn">356.401 EXEMPTION FROM PROCESS.</h1>
        <div class="subd" id="stat.356.401.1">
          <a title="Link to Subdivision 1." class="permalink" href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/statutes/2024/cite/356/full#stat.356.401.1">§</a>
          <h2 class="subd_no">Subdivision 1.<span class="headnote">Exemption; exceptions.</span></h2>
          <p>None of the money, annuities, or other benefits provided for in the governing law of a covered retirement plan is assignable either in law or in equity or subject to state estate tax, or to execution, levy, attachment, garnishment, or other legal process, except as provided in subdivision 2 or section <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/statutes/cite/518.58">518.58</a>, <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/statutes/cite/518.581">518.581</a>, or <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/statutes/cite/518A.53">518A.53</a>.</p>
        </div>
        <div class="subd" id="stat.356.401.2">
          <a title="Link to Subd. 2." class="permalink" href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/statutes/2024/cite/356/full#stat.356.401.2">§</a>
          <h2 class="subd_no">Subd. 2.<span class="headnote">Automatic deposits.</span></h2>
          <p>(a) The chief administrative officer of a covered retirement plan may remit, through an automatic deposit system, annuity, benefit, or refund payments only to a financial institution associated with the National Automated Clearinghouse Association or a comparable successor organization that is trustee for a person who is eligible to receive the annuity, benefit, or refund.</p>
          <p>(b) Upon the request of a retiree, disabilitant, survivor, or former member, the chief administrative officer of a covered retirement plan may remit the annuity, benefit, or refund payment to the applicable financial institution for deposit in the person's individual account or the person's joint account. If an overpayment of benefits is paid after the death of the annuitant or benefit recipient, the chief administrative officer of the pension plan is authorized to issue an administrative subpoena consistent with the requirements of section <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/statutes/cite/13A.02">13A.02</a>, requiring the applicable financial institution to disclose the names of all joint and co-owners of the account and a description of all deposits to, and withdrawals from, the account which take place on or after the death of the annuitant or benefit recipient. An overpayment to a joint account after the death of the annuitant or benefit recipient must be repaid to the fund of the applicable covered retirement plan by the joint tenant if the overpayment is not repaid to that fund by the financial institution associated with the National Automated Clearinghouse Association or its successor. The governing board of the covered retirement plan may prescribe the conditions under which these payments may be made.</p>
        </div>
        <div class="subd" id="stat.356.401.3">
          <a title="Link to Subd. 3." class="permalink" href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/statutes/2024/cite/356/full#stat.356.401.3">§</a>
          <h2 class="subd_no">Subd. 3.<span class="headnote">Covered retirement plans.</span></h2>
          <p>The provisions of this section apply to the following retirement plans:</p>
          <p>(1) the legislators retirement plan, established by chapter 3A, including constitutional officers as specified in that chapter;</p>
          <p>(2) the general state employees retirement plan of the Minnesota State Retirement System, established by chapter 352;</p>
          <p>(3) the correctional state employees retirement plan of the Minnesota State Retirement System, established by chapter 352;</p>
          <p>(4) the State Patrol retirement plan, established by chapter 352B;</p>
          <p>(5) the unclassified state employees retirement program, established by chapter 352D;</p>
          <p>(6) the general employees retirement plan of the Public Employees Retirement Association, established by chapter 353;</p>
          <p>(7) the public employees police and fire plan of the Public Employees Retirement Association, established by chapter 353;</p>
          <p>(8) the public employees defined contribution plan, established by chapter 353D;</p>
          <p>(9) the local government correctional service retirement plan of the Public Employees Retirement Association, established by chapter 353E;</p>
          <p>(10) the statewide lump-sum volunteer firefighter plan, established by chapter 353G;</p>
          <p>(11) the Teachers Retirement Association, established by chapter 354;</p>
          <p>(12) the St. Paul Teachers Retirement Fund Association, established by chapter 354A;</p>
          <p>(13) the individual retirement account plan, established by chapter 354B;</p>
          <p>(14) the higher education supplemental retirement plan, established by chapter 354C; and</p>
          <p>(15) the judges retirement fund, established by chapter 490.</p>
        </div>
      </div>
      <div class="history" id="stat.356.401.history
			">
        <h2>History: </h2>
        <p class="first"><a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/laws/2005/0/164/#laws.0.29.0">2005 c 164 s 29</a>; <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/laws/2005/1/7/#laws.0.28.0">1Sp2005 c 7 s 28</a>; <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/laws/2005/1/8/#laws.10.64.0">1Sp2005 c 8 art 10 s 64</a>; <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/laws/2006/0/271/#laws.11.48.0">2006 c 271 art 11 s 48</a>; <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/laws/2009/0/169/#laws.4.40.0">2009 c 169 art 4 s 40</a>; art 9 s 27; <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/laws/2010/0/359/#laws.12.30.0">2010 c 359 art 12 s 30</a>; <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/laws/2011/1/8/#laws.6.19.0">1Sp2011 c 8 art 6 s 19; art 7 s 19; art 8 s 8,14</a>; <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/laws/2013/0/111/#laws.2.29.0">2013 c 111 art 2 s 29</a>; <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/laws/2014/0/296/#laws.6.40.0">2014 c 296 art 6 s 40</a>,49; <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/laws/2015/0/68/#laws.14.23.0">2015 c 68 art 14 s 23</a>; <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/laws/2020/0/108/#laws.7.9.0">2020 c 108 art 7 s 9</a></p>
      </div>
      <div class="section" id="stat.356.403">
        <h1 class="shn">356.403 NORMAL RETIREMENT AGE; SAVINGS CLAUSE.</h1>
        <p>The intent of the legislature in sections <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/statutes/cite/352.01#stat.352.01.25">352.01, subdivision 25</a>; <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/statutes/cite/353.01#stat.353.01.37">353.01, subdivision 37</a>; <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/statutes/cite/354.05#stat.354.05.38">354.05, subdivision 38</a>; and <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/statutes/cite/354A.011#stat.354A.011.15a">354A.011, subdivision 15a</a>, is to create a normal retirement age for persons first covered by those sections after May 16, 1989, that is the same as the retirement age in the federal Social Security law, including future amendments to that law. If a court determines that the legislature may not incorporate by reference the future changes in federal Social Security law, the legislature reserves the right to amend the appropriate sections to make the normal retirement age conform to the retirement age in the federal Social Security law. No person first covered by any of those sections after May 16, 1989, has a right to a normal retirement age that is less than the retirement age in the federal Social Security law.</p>
      </div>
      <div class="history" id="stat.356.403.history
			">
        <h2>History: </h2>
        <p class="first">
          <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/laws/2002/0/392/#laws.11.25.0">2002 c 392 art 11 s 25</a>
        </p>
      </div>
      <div class="sr" id="stat.356.405"><b>356.405</b> [Repealed, <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/laws/2015/0/68/#laws.13.65.0">2015 c 68 art 13 s 65</a>]</div>
      <h1 class="fgh">SURVIVOR BENEFITS</h1>
      <div class="section" id="stat.356.406">
        <h1 class="shn">356.406 LOSS OF ENTITLEMENT TO BENEFITS FOR SURVIVOR CAUSING DEATH OF PENSION PLAN MEMBER.</h1>
        <div class="subd" id="stat.356.406.1">
          <a title="Link to Subdivision 1." class="permalink" href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/statutes/2024/cite/356/full#stat.356.406.1">§</a>
          <h2 class="subd_no">Subdivision 1.<span class="headnote">Definitions.</span></h2>
          <p>(a) Each of the words or terms defined in this subdivision has the meaning indicated.</p>
          <p>(b) "Public pension plan" means any retirement plan or fund enumerated in section <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/statutes/cite/356.20#stat.356.20.2">356.20, subdivision 2</a>, or <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/statutes/cite/356.30#stat.356.30.3">356.30, subdivision 3</a>, the Bloomington Fire Department Relief Association, any relief association governed by sections <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/statutes/cite/424A.091">424A.091</a> to <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/statutes/cite/424A.095">424A.095</a>, any retirement plan governed by chapter 354B or 354C, the Hennepin County supplemental retirement plan governed by sections <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/statutes/cite/383B.46">383B.46</a> to <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/statutes/cite/383B.52">383B.52</a>, or any housing and redevelopment authority retirement plan.</p>
          <p>(c) "Public pension plan member" means a person who is a participant covered by a public pension plan; a former participant of a public pension plan who has sufficient service to be entitled to receive a future retirement annuity or service pension; a recipient of a retirement annuity, service pension, or disability benefit from a public pension plan; or a former participant of a public pension plan who has member or employee contributions to the person's credit in the public pension plan.</p>
          <p>(d) "Survivor" means the surviving spouse, a former spouse, a surviving child, a joint annuitant, a designated recipient of a second or remainder portion of an optional annuity form, a beneficiary, or the estate of a deceased public pension plan member, as those terms are commonly understood or defined in the benefit plan document of the public pension plan.</p>
          <p>(e) "Survivor benefit" means a surviving spouse benefit, surviving child benefit, second or remainder portion of an optional annuity form, a death benefit, a funeral benefit, or a refund of member or employee contributions payable on account of the death of a public pension plan member as provided for in the benefit plan document of the public pension plan.</p>
        </div>
        <div class="subd" id="stat.356.406.2">
          <a title="Link to Subd. 2." class="permalink" href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/statutes/2024/cite/356/full#stat.356.406.2">§</a>
          <h2 class="subd_no">Subd. 2.<span class="headnote">Suspension of survivor benefits upon felony charge.</span></h2>
          <p>During the pendency of a charge of a survivor of a felony that caused the death of a public pension plan member, of criminal liability for a death by wrongful act felony, or of conspiracy to commit a death by wrongful act felony, the entitlement of that survivor to receive a survivor benefit is suspended.</p>
        </div>
        <div class="subd" id="stat.356.406.3">
          <a title="Link to Subd. 3." class="permalink" href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/statutes/2024/cite/356/full#stat.356.406.3">§</a>
          <h2 class="subd_no">Subd. 3.<span class="headnote">Forfeiture of survivor benefits upon felony conviction.</span></h2>
          <p>On final conviction of a survivor of a felony that caused the death of a public pension plan member, of criminal liability for a death by wrongful act felony, or of conspiracy to commit a death by wrongful act felony, the entitlement of that survivor to receive a survivor benefit is forfeited, including entitlement for any previously suspended survivor benefits under subdivision 2.</p>
        </div>
        <div class="subd" id="stat.356.406.4">
          <a title="Link to Subd. 4." class="permalink" href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/statutes/2024/cite/356/full#stat.356.406.4">§</a>
          <h2 class="subd_no">Subd. 4.<span class="headnote">Suspension or forfeiture actions separate.</span></h2>
          <p>The charge of one survivor under subdivision 2 or the conviction of one survivor under subdivision 3 does not affect the entitlement of another survivor to a survivor benefit.</p>
        </div>
        <div class="subd" id="stat.356.406.5">
          <a title="Link to Subd. 5." class="permalink" href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/statutes/2024/cite/356/full#stat.356.406.5">§</a>
          <h2 class="subd_no">Subd. 5.<span class="headnote">Recovery of certain benefits.</span></h2>
          <p>If monthly benefits or a refund of the balance of a participant or former participant's account have already been paid to an individual who is later charged or convicted as described under this section, the executive director or chief administrative officer of the public pension plan shall attempt to recover the amounts paid. Payment may be made to the next beneficiary or survivor only in an amount equal to the amount recovered and in the amount of any future payments that would legally accrue to another survivor under the applicable laws of the retirement plan.</p>
        </div>
        <div class="subd" id="stat.356.406.6">
          <a title="Link to Subd. 6." class="permalink" href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/statutes/2024/cite/356/full#stat.356.406.6">§</a>
          <h2 class="subd_no">Subd. 6.<span class="headnote">Disposition of forfeited survivor benefits.</span></h2>
          <p>If the benefit plan document governing the public pension plan does not provide for the disposition of forfeited benefits, survivor benefits forfeited under this section must be deposited in the general fund of the state.</p>
        </div>
      </div>
      <div class="history" id="stat.356.406.history
			">
        <h2>History: </h2>
        <p class="first"><a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/laws/2002/0/392/#laws.11.27.0">2002 c 392 art 11 s 27</a>; <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/laws/2013/0/111/#laws.5.66.0">2013 c 111 art 5 s 66</a>,80</p>
      </div>
      <div class="section" id="stat.356.407">
        <h1 class="shn">356.407 RESTORATION OF SURVIVOR BENEFITS.</h1>
        <div class="subd" id="stat.356.407.1">
          <a title="Link to Subdivision 1." class="permalink" href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/statutes/2024/cite/356/full#stat.356.407.1">§</a>
          <h2 class="subd_no">Subdivision 1.<span class="headnote">Restoration upon termination of remarriage.</span></h2>
          <p>Notwithstanding any provision to the contrary of the laws governing any of the retirement plans enumerated in subdivision 2, any person who was receiving a surviving spouse's benefit from any of those plans and whose benefit terminated solely because of remarriage is, if the remarriage terminates for any reason, again entitled upon reapplication to a surviving spouse's benefit; provided, however, that the person is not entitled to retroactive payments for the period of remarriage. The benefit resumes at the level which the person would have been receiving if there had been no remarriage.</p>
        </div>
        <div class="subd" id="stat.356.407.2">
          <a title="Link to Subd. 2." class="permalink" href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/statutes/2024/cite/356/full#stat.356.407.2">§</a>
          <h2 class="subd_no">Subd. 2.<span class="headnote">Covered funds.</span></h2>
          <p>The provisions of this section apply to the following retirement funds:</p>
          <p>(1) the general employees retirement plan of the Public Employees Retirement Association established under chapter 353;</p>
          <p>(2) the public employees police and fire plan of the Public Employees Retirement Association established under chapter 353;</p>
          <p>(3) the State Patrol retirement plan established under chapter 352B;</p>
          <p>(4) the legislators retirement plan established under chapter 3A;</p>
          <p>(5) the elective state officers retirement plan established under chapter 352C; and</p>
          <p>(6) the Teachers Retirement Association established under chapter 354.</p>
        </div>
      </div>
      <div class="history" id="stat.356.407.history
			">
        <h2>History: </h2>
        <p class="first"><a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/laws/2002/0/392/#laws.11.28.0">2002 c 392 art 11 s 28</a>; <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/laws/2010/0/359/#laws.12.31.0">2010 c 359 art 12 s 31</a>; <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/laws/2015/0/68/#laws.13.48.0">2015 c 68 art 13 s 48</a>; art 14 s 24</p>
      </div>
      <div class="section" id="stat.356.408">
        <h1 class="shn">356.408 TERMINATION OF SURVIVOR DESIGNATION.</h1>
        <div class="subd" id="stat.356.408.1">
          <a title="Link to Subdivision 1." class="permalink" href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/statutes/2024/cite/356/full#stat.356.408.1">§</a>
          <h2 class="subd_no">Subdivision 1.<span class="headnote">Authorization to terminate optional annuity form.</span></h2>
          <p>A public pension plan retired member receiving a joint and survivor retirement annuity or a person receiving a joint and survivor disability benefit from a plan listed in section <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/statutes/cite/356.30">356.30</a>, subdivision 3, and the designated survivor of that person may mutually agree to terminate the survivor designation by filing a termination statement on a form and in the manner specified by the chief administrative officer of the applicable public pension plan. Upon filing a valid termination statement accepted by the chief administrative officer, the rights of the designated survivor to receive a benefit upon death of the plan retired or disabled annuitant are terminated, and the retired or disabled annuitant must receive a normal single-life annuity.</p>
        </div>
        <div class="subd" id="stat.356.408.2">
          <a title="Link to Subd. 2." class="permalink" href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/statutes/2024/cite/356/full#stat.356.408.2">§</a>
          <h2 class="subd_no">Subd. 2.<span class="headnote">Revised annuity form.</span></h2>
          <p>The replacement single life annuity must be actuarially equivalent to the joint and survivor annuity as of the first day of the month following acceptance of the valid termination statement by the chief administrative officer and payment of this revised prospective annuity begins on that same date.</p>
        </div>
        <div class="subd" id="stat.356.408.3">
          <a title="Link to Subd. 3." class="permalink" href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/statutes/2024/cite/356/full#stat.356.408.3">§</a>
          <h2 class="subd_no">Subd. 3.<span class="headnote">Application.</span></h2>
          <p>This section does not apply if the designated survivor is the spouse or former spouse of the plan member.</p>
        </div>
        <div class="subd" id="stat.356.408.4">
          <a title="Link to Subd. 4." class="permalink" href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/statutes/2024/cite/356/full#stat.356.408.4">§</a>
          <h2 class="subd_no">Subd. 4.<span class="headnote">Termination statement form requirements.</span></h2>
          <p>The annuity form termination statements must be in written form and must be notarized. Before accepting any signed form or forms, the chief administrative officer of the applicable pension plan must offer counseling to the retired or disabled annuitant and the designated survivor regarding the implications of the annuity form waiver. The forms must indicate that this counseling has been offered and either has been completed or has been waived by the retired or disabled annuitant and the designated survivor.</p>
        </div>
        <div class="subd" id="stat.356.408.5">
          <a title="Link to Subd. 5." class="permalink" href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/statutes/2024/cite/356/full#stat.356.408.5">§</a>
          <h2 class="subd_no">Subd. 5.<span class="headnote">Prohibition against further annuity form revisions.</span></h2>
          <p>No retired or disabled annuitant who waives the annuity form under this section may further revise the annuity form at any later date.</p>
        </div>
      </div>
      <div class="history" id="stat.356.408.history
			">
        <h2>History: </h2>
        <p class="first">
          <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/laws/2013/0/111/#laws.7.7.0">2013 c 111 art 7 s 7</a>
        </p>
      </div>
      <h1 class="fgh">POSTRETIREMENT INCREASES</h1>
      <div class="sr" id="stat.356.41"><b>356.41</b> [Repealed, <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/laws/2009/0/169/#laws.1.77.0">2009 c 169 art 1 s 77</a>]</div>
      <div class="section" id="stat.356.415">
        <h1 class="shn">356.415 POSTRETIREMENT ADJUSTMENTS; STATEWIDE RETIREMENT PLANS.</h1>
        <div class="subd" id="stat.356.415.1">
          <a title="Link to Subdivision 1." class="permalink" href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/statutes/2024/cite/356/full#stat.356.415.1">§</a>
          <h2 class="subd_no">Subdivision 1.<span class="headnote">Annual postretirement adjustments; Minnesota State Retirement System general state employees retirement plan, legislators retirement plan, and unclassified state employees retirement program.</span></h2>
          <p>(a) Recipients of a retirement annuity, disability benefit, or survivor benefit from the general state employees retirement plan, the legislators retirement plan, or the unclassified state employees retirement program are entitled to an annual postretirement adjustment, effective as of each January 1, as follows:</p>
          <p>(1) effective January 1, 2019, through December 31, 2023, a postretirement increase of one percent must be applied each year to the amount of the monthly annuity or benefit of each annuitant or benefit recipient who has been receiving an annuity or a benefit for at least 12 full months as of the June 30 of the calendar year immediately before the adjustment;</p>
          <p>(2) effective January 1, 2019, through December 31, 2023, for each annuitant or benefit recipient who has been receiving an annuity or a benefit for at least one full month, but less than 12 full months as of the June 30 of the calendar year immediately before the adjustment, a postretirement increase of 1/12 of one percent for each month that the person has been receiving an annuity or benefit must be applied to the amount of the monthly annuity or benefit of the annuitant or benefit recipient;</p>
          <p>(3) effective January 1, 2024, and thereafter, a postretirement increase of 1.5 percent must be applied each year to the amount of the monthly annuity or benefit of each annuitant or benefit recipient who has been receiving an annuity or a benefit for at least 12 full months as of the June 30 of the calendar year immediately before the adjustment; and</p>
          <p>(4) effective January 1, 2024, and thereafter, for each annuitant or benefit recipient who has been receiving an annuity or a benefit for at least one full month, but less than 12 full months as of the June 30 of the calendar year immediately before the adjustment, an annual postretirement increase of 1/12 of 1.5 percent for each month that the person has been receiving an annuity or benefit must be applied to the amount of the monthly annuity or benefit of the annuitant or benefit recipient.</p>
          <p>(b) An increase in annuity or benefit payments under this subdivision must be made automatically unless written notice is filed by the annuitant or benefit recipient with the executive director of the covered retirement plan requesting that the increase not be made.</p>
        </div>
        <div class="subd" id="stat.356.415.1a">
          <a title="Link to Subd. 1a." class="permalink" href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/statutes/2024/cite/356/full#stat.356.415.1a">§</a>
          <h2 class="subd_no">Subd. 1a.<span class="headnote">Annual postretirement adjustments; Minnesota State Retirement System correctional state employees retirement plan.</span></h2>
          <p>(a) Retirement annuity, disability benefit, or survivor benefit recipients of the correctional state employees retirement plan are entitled to an annual postretirement adjustment, effective as of each January 1, as follows:</p>
          <p>(1) a postretirement increase of 1.5 percent must be applied each year to the monthly annuity or benefit of each annuitant or benefit recipient who has been receiving an annuity or a benefit for at least 12 full months as of the June 30 of the calendar year immediately before the adjustment; and</p>
          <p>(2) for each annuitant or benefit recipient who has been receiving an annuity or a benefit for at least one full month, but less than 12 full months as of the June 30 of the calendar year immediately before the adjustment, an annual postretirement increase of 1/12 of 1.5 percent for each month that the person has been receiving an annuity or benefit must be applied to the amount of the monthly annuity or benefit of each annuitant or benefit recipient.</p>
          <p>(b) An increase in annuity or benefit payments under this subdivision must be made automatically unless written notice is filed by the annuitant or benefit recipient with the executive director of the applicable covered retirement plan requesting that the increase not be made.</p>
        </div>
        <div class="subd" id="stat.356.415.1b">
          <a title="Link to Subd. 1b." class="permalink" href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/statutes/2024/cite/356/full#stat.356.415.1b">§</a>
          <h2 class="subd_no">Subd. 1b.<span class="headnote">Annual postretirement adjustments; PERA; general employees retirement plan.</span></h2>
          <p>(a) Annuities, disability benefits, and survivor benefits being paid from the general employees retirement plan of the Public Employees Retirement Association shall be increased effective each January 1 by the percentage of increase determined under this subdivision. The increase to the annuity or benefit shall be determined by multiplying the monthly amount of the annuity or benefit by the percentage of increase specified in paragraph (b), after taking into account any reduction to the percentage of increase required under paragraph (c).</p>
          <p>(b) The percentage of increase shall be one percent unless the federal Social Security Administration has announced a cost-of-living adjustment pursuant to United States Code, title 42, section 415(i), in the last quarter of the preceding calendar year that is greater than two percent. If the cost-of-living adjustment announced by the federal Social Security Administration is greater than two percent, the percentage of increase shall be 50 percent of the cost-of-living adjustment announced by the federal Social Security Administration, but in no event may the percentage of increase exceed 1.5 percent.</p>
          <p>(c)(1) If the recipient of an annuity, disability benefit, or survivor's benefit has been receiving the annuity or benefit for at least 12 full months as of the June 30 of the calendar year immediately before the effective date of the increase, there is no reduction in the percentage of increase.</p>
          <p>(2) If the recipient of an annuity, disability benefit, or survivor's benefit has been receiving the annuity or benefit for at least one month, but less than 12 full months, as of the June 30 of the calendar year immediately preceding the effective date of the increase, the percentage of increase is multiplied by a fraction, the numerator of which is the number of months the annuity or benefit was received as of June 30 of the preceding calendar year and the denominator of which is 12.</p>
          <p>(d) An increase in annuity or benefit payments under this section must be made automatically unless written notice is filed by the recipient with the executive director of the Public Employees Retirement Association requesting that the increase not be made.</p>
        </div>
        <div class="subd" id="stat.356.415.1c">
          <a title="Link to Subd. 1c." class="permalink" href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/statutes/2024/cite/356/full#stat.356.415.1c">§</a>
          <h2 class="subd_no">Subd. 1c.<span class="headnote">Annual postretirement adjustments; PERA-police and fire.</span></h2>
          <p>(a) Retirement annuity, disability benefit, or survivor benefit recipients of the public employees police and fire retirement plan are entitled to an annual postretirement adjustment, effective as of each January 1, as follows:</p>
          <p>(1) for each annuitant or benefit recipient who will have been receiving an annuity or benefit for at least 36 full months as of the immediate preceding June 30, a postretirement increase of one percent must be applied each year to the amount of the monthly annuity or benefit of the annuitant or benefit recipient; or</p>
          <p>(2) for each annuitant or benefit recipient who has been receiving the annuity or benefit for at least 25 full months, but less than 36 months as of the immediate preceding June 30, a postretirement increase of 1/12 of one percent for each full month that the person has been receiving an annuity or benefit during the fiscal year in which the annuity or benefit was effective must be applied each year to the amount of the monthly annuity or benefit of the annuitant or benefit recipient.</p>
          <p>(b) An increase in annuity or benefit payments under this section must be made automatically unless written notice is filed by the annuitant or benefit recipient with the executive director of the Public Employees Retirement Association requesting that the increase not be made.</p>
        </div>
        <div class="subd" id="stat.356.415.1d">
          <a title="Link to Subd. 1d." class="permalink" href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/statutes/2024/cite/356/full#stat.356.415.1d">§</a>
          <h2 class="subd_no">Subd. 1d.<span class="headnote">Teachers Retirement Association annual postretirement adjustments.</span></h2>
          <p>(a) Except as set forth in paragraph (d), recipients of a retirement annuity, disability benefit, or survivor benefit from the Teachers Retirement Association are entitled to an annual postretirement adjustment, effective as of each January 1, as follows:</p>
          <p>(1) effective January 1, 2019, through December 31, 2023, a postretirement increase of one percent must be applied each year to the amount of the monthly annuity or benefit of each annuitant or benefit recipient who has been receiving an annuity or a benefit for at least 12 full months as of the June 30 of the calendar year immediately before the adjustment;</p>
          <p>(2) effective January 1, 2019, through December 31, 2023, for each annuitant or benefit recipient who has been receiving an annuity or a benefit for at least one full month, but less than 12 full months as of the June 30 of the calendar year immediately before the adjustment, a postretirement increase of 1/12 of one percent for each month the person has been receiving an annuity or benefit must be applied to the amount of the monthly annuity or benefit of the annuitant or benefit recipient;</p>
          <p>(3) effective January 1, 2024, and thereafter, a postretirement increase must be applied each year to the amount of the monthly annuity or benefit of each annuitant or benefit recipient who has been receiving an annuity or a benefit for at least 12 full months as of the June 30 of the calendar year immediately before the adjustment, at the following rates:</p>
          <table>
            <col style="width:5.494505494505495%">
            <col style="width:60.4395604395604%">
            <col style="width:34.0659340659341%">
            <tr>
              <td></td>
              <td>from January 1, 2024, through December 31, 2024</td>
              <td>1.1 percent</td>
            </tr>
            <tr>
              <td></td>
              <td>from January 1, 2025, through December 31, 2025</td>
              <td>1.2 percent</td>
            </tr>
            <tr>
              <td></td>
              <td>from January 1, 2026, through December 31, 2026</td>
              <td>1.3 percent</td>
            </tr>
            <tr>
              <td></td>
              <td>from January 1, 2027, through December 31, 2027</td>
              <td>1.4 percent</td>
            </tr>
            <tr>
              <td></td>
              <td>from January 1, 2028, and thereafter</td>
              <td>1.5 percent</td>
            </tr>
          </table>
          <p>(4) effective January 1, 2024, and thereafter, for each annuitant or benefit recipient who has been receiving an annuity or a benefit for at least one full month, but less than 12 full months, as of the June 30 of the calendar year immediately before the adjustment, an annual postretirement increase of 1/12 of the applicable percentage for each month that the person has been receiving an annuity or benefit must be applied to the amount of the monthly annuity or benefit of the annuitant or benefit recipient. The applicable percentages are the following:</p>
          <table>
            <col style="width:5.494505494505495%">
            <col style="width:60.4395604395604%">
            <col style="width:34.0659340659341%">
            <tr>
              <td></td>
              <td>from January 1, 2024, through December 31, 2024</td>
              <td>1.1 percent</td>
            </tr>
            <tr>
              <td></td>
              <td>from January 1, 2025, through December 31, 2025</td>
              <td>1.2 percent</td>
            </tr>
            <tr>
              <td></td>
              <td>from January 1, 2026, through December 31, 2026</td>
              <td>1.3 percent</td>
            </tr>
            <tr>
              <td></td>
              <td>from January 1, 2027, through December 31, 2027</td>
              <td>1.4 percent</td>
            </tr>
            <tr>
              <td></td>
              <td>from January 1, 2028, and thereafter</td>
              <td>1.5 percent</td>
            </tr>
          </table>
          <p>(b) An increase in annuity or benefit payments under this section must be made automatically unless written notice is filed by the annuitant or benefit recipient with the executive director of the Teachers Retirement Association requesting that the increase not be made.</p>
          <p>(c) The retirement annuity payable to a person who retires before becoming eligible for Social Security benefits and who has elected the optional payment as provided in section <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/statutes/cite/354.35">354.35</a> must be treated as the sum of a period-certain retirement annuity and a life retirement annuity for the purposes of any postretirement adjustment. The period-certain retirement annuity plus the life retirement annuity must be the annuity amount payable until age 62, 65, or normal retirement age, as selected by the member at retirement, for an annuity amount payable under section <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/statutes/cite/354.35">354.35</a>. A postretirement adjustment granted on the period-certain retirement annuity must terminate when the period-certain retirement annuity terminates.</p>
          <p>(d) Members who retire on or after July 1, 2024, are entitled to an annual postretirement adjustment of the member's retirement annuity, effective as of each January 1, beginning with the year following the year in which the member attains normal retirement age, as follows:</p>
          <p>(1) if a member has been receiving an annuity for at least 12 full months as of the June 30 of the calendar year immediately before the date of the adjustment, a postretirement increase equal to the percentage specified in paragraph (a), clause (3), must be applied, effective on January 1, to the amount of the member's monthly annuity;</p>
          <p>(2) if a member has been receiving an annuity for at least one full month, but less than 12 full months as of the June 30 of the calendar year immediately before the date of adjustment, a postretirement increase of 1/12 of the applicable percentage specified in paragraph (a), clause (4), for each month that the member has been receiving an annuity must be applied, effective on January 1, to the amount of the member's monthly annuity; or</p>
          <p>(3) if a member has been receiving an annuity for fewer than seven months as of the January 1 of the year following the year in which the member attains normal retirement age, a postretirement adjustment shall be applied effective as of the next January 1. The amount of the adjustment shall be determined under clause (2).</p>
          <p>(e) Paragraph (d) does not apply to members who retire under section <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/statutes/cite/354.44#stat.354.44.6">354.44, subdivision 6</a>, paragraph (c), clause (3), or who retire when the member is at least age 62 and has at least 30 years of service under section <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/statutes/cite/354.44#stat.354.44.6">354.44, subdivision 6</a>, paragraph (c), (d), (e), or (f), as applicable.</p>
        </div>
        <div class="subd" id="stat.356.415.1e">
          <a title="Link to Subd. 1e." class="permalink" href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/statutes/2024/cite/356/full#stat.356.415.1e">§</a>
          <h2 class="subd_no">Subd. 1e.<span class="headnote">Annual postretirement adjustments; State Patrol retirement plan.</span></h2>
          <p>(a) Retirement annuity, disability benefit, or survivor benefit recipients of the State Patrol retirement plan are entitled to an annual postretirement adjustment, effective as of each January 1, as follows:</p>
          <p>(1) a postretirement increase of one percent must be applied each year to the monthly annuity or benefit of each annuitant or benefit recipient who has been receiving an annuity or a benefit for at least 12 full months as of the June 30 of the calendar year immediately before the adjustment; and</p>
          <p>(2) for each annuitant or benefit recipient who has been receiving an annuity or a benefit for at least one full month, but less than 12 full months as of the June 30 of the calendar year immediately before the adjustment, an annual postretirement increase of 1/12 of one percent for each month that the person has been receiving an annuity or benefit must be applied to the amount of the monthly annuity or benefit of each annuitant or benefit recipient.</p>
          <p>(b) An increase in annuity or benefit payments under this subdivision must be made automatically unless written notice is filed by the annuitant or benefit recipient with the executive director of the applicable covered retirement plan requesting that the increase not be made.</p>
        </div>
        <div class="subd" id="stat.356.415.1f">
          <a title="Link to Subd. 1f." class="permalink" href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/statutes/2024/cite/356/full#stat.356.415.1f">§</a>
          <h2 class="subd_no">Subd. 1f.<span class="headnote">Annual postretirement adjustments; Minnesota State Retirement System judges retirement plan.</span></h2>
          <p>(a) Recipients of a retirement annuity, disability benefit, or survivor benefit from the judges retirement plan are entitled to an annual postretirement adjustment, effective as of each January 1, as follows:</p>
          <p>(1) through December 31, 2021, a postretirement increase of 1.75 percent must be applied each year to the monthly annuity or benefit of each annuitant or benefit recipient who has been receiving an annuity or a benefit for at least 12 full months as of the June 30 of the calendar year immediately before the adjustment;</p>
          <p>(2) through December 31, 2021, for each annuitant or benefit recipient who has been receiving an annuity or a benefit for at least one full month, but less than 12 full months as of the June 30 of the calendar year immediately before the adjustment, an annual postretirement increase of 1/12 of 1.75 percent for each month that the person has been receiving an annuity or benefit must be applied to the amount of the monthly annuity or benefit;</p>
          <p>(3) effective January 1, 2022, and thereafter, a postretirement increase of 1.5 percent must be applied each year to the monthly annuity or benefit of each annuitant or benefit recipient who has been receiving an annuity or a benefit for at least 12 full months as of the June 30 of the calendar year immediately before the adjustment; and</p>
          <p>(4) effective January 1, 2022, and thereafter, for each annuitant or benefit recipient who has been receiving an annuity or a benefit for at least one full month, but less than 12 full months as of the June 30 of the calendar year immediately before the adjustment, an annual postretirement increase of 1/12 of 1.5 percent for each month that the person has been receiving an annuity or benefit must be applied to the amount of the monthly annuity or benefit.</p>
          <p>(b) An increase in annuity or benefit payments under this subdivision must be made automatically unless written notice is filed by the annuitant or benefit recipient with the executive director of the applicable covered retirement plan requesting that the increase not be made.</p>
        </div>
        <div class="subd" id="stat.356.415.1g">
          <a title="Link to Subd. 1g." class="permalink" href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/statutes/2024/cite/356/full#stat.356.415.1g">§</a>
          <h2 class="subd_no">Subd. 1g.<span class="headnote">Annual postretirement adjustments; PERA local government correctional retirement plan.</span></h2>
