          <p>(a) Annuities, disability benefits, and survivor benefits being paid from the local government correctional retirement plan of the Public Employees Retirement Association shall be increased effective each January 1 by the percentage of increase determined under this subdivision. The increase to the annuity or benefit shall be determined by multiplying the monthly amount of the annuity or benefit by the percentage of increase specified in paragraph (b), after taking into account any reduction to the percentage of increase required under paragraph (d).</p>
          <p>(b) As of each January 1, the percentage of increase must be one percent unless the federal Social Security Administration has announced a cost-of-living adjustment pursuant to United States Code, title 42, section 415(i), in the last quarter of the preceding calendar year that is greater than one percent. If the cost-of-living adjustment announced by the federal Social Security Administration is greater than one percent, the percentage of increase must be the same as the cost-of-living adjustment announced by the federal Social Security Administration, but in no event may the percentage of increase exceed the applicable maximum percentage in effect on January 1 under paragraph (c).</p>
          <p>(c) The applicable maximum percentage in effect on January 1 is 2.5 percent, unless either of the following is true, in which case the applicable maximum percentage is 1.5 percent:</p>
          <p>(1) the market value of assets equals or is less than 85 percent of the actuarial accrued liabilities as reported by the plan's actuary in the most recent two consecutive annual actuarial valuations; or</p>
          <p>(2) the market value of assets equals or is less than 80 percent of the actuarial accrued liabilities as reported by the plan's actuary in the most recent annual actuarial valuation. If, on January 1 after a year during which the applicable maximum percentage was 1.5 percent, neither clause (1) or (2) is true, then the applicable maximum percentage is 2.5 percent.</p>
          <p>(d) (1) If the recipient of an annuity, disability benefit, or survivor's benefit has been receiving the annuity or benefit for at least 12 full months as of the June 30 of the calendar year immediately before the effective date of the increase, there is no reduction in the percentage of increase.</p>
          <p>(2) If the recipient of an annuity, disability benefit, or survivor's benefit has been receiving the annuity or benefit for at least one month, but less than 12 full months, as of the June 30 of the calendar year immediately preceding the effective date of the increase, the percentage of increase is multiplied by a fraction, the numerator of which is the number of months the annuity or benefit was received as of June 30 of the preceding calendar year and the denominator of which is 12.</p>
          <p>(e) An increase in annuity or benefit payments under this section must be made automatically unless written notice is filed by the recipient with the executive director of the Public Employees Retirement Association requesting that the increase not be made.</p>
        </div>
        <div class="subd" id="stat.356.415.2">
          <a title="Link to Subd. 2." class="permalink" href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/statutes/2024/cite/356/full#stat.356.415.2">§</a>
          <h2 class="subd_no">Subd. 2.<span class="headnote">Covered retirement plans.</span></h2>
          <p>The provisions of this section apply to the following retirement plans:</p>
          <p>(1) the legislators retirement plan established under chapter 3A, including constitutional officers as specified in that chapter;</p>
          <p>(2) the correctional state employees retirement plan of the Minnesota State Retirement System established under chapter 352;</p>
          <p>(3) the general state employees retirement plan of the Minnesota State Retirement System established under chapter 352;</p>
          <p>(4) the State Patrol retirement plan established under chapter 352B;</p>
          <p>(5) the general employees retirement plan of the Public Employees Retirement Association established under chapter 353;</p>
          <p>(6) the public employees police and fire retirement plan of the Public Employees Retirement Association established under chapter 353;</p>
          <p>(7) the local government correctional employees retirement plan of the Public Employees Retirement Association established under chapter 353E;</p>
          <p>(8) the teachers retirement plan established under chapter 354; and</p>
          <p>(9) the judges retirement plan established under chapter 490.</p>
        </div>
        <div class="subd" id="stat.356.415.3">
          <a title="Link to Subd. 3." class="permalink" href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/statutes/2024/cite/356/full#stat.356.415.3">§</a>
          <h2 class="subd_no">Subd. 3.</h2>
          <p>[Repealed, <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/laws/2014/0/296/#laws.10.3.0">2014 c 296 art 10 s 3</a>]</p>
        </div>
      </div>
      <div class="history" id="stat.356.415.history
			">
        <h2>History: </h2>
        <p class="first"><a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/laws/2009/0/169/#laws.1.73.0">2009 c 169 art 1 s 73</a>; <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/laws/2010/0/359/#laws.1.76.0">2010 c 359 art 1 s 76</a>-82; art 12 s 32; <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/laws/2012/0/286/#laws.8.8.0">2012 c 286 art 8 s 8</a>; <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/laws/2013/0/111/#laws.2.30.0">2013 c 111 art 2 s 30</a>,31; art 3 s 28,29; art 9 s 10; art 11 s 13,14; art 14 s 2,3; <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/laws/2014/0/296/#laws.11.3.0">2014 c 296 art 11 s 3</a>-6; art 13 s 19; <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/laws/2015/0/68/#laws.4.4.0">2015 c 68 art 4 s 4</a>-10; art 12 s 39; art 13 s 49-53; art 14 s 25; <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/laws/2018/0/211/#laws.5.6.0">2018 c 211 art 5 s 6</a>-13; <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/laws/2021/0/22/#laws.1.2.0">2021 c 22 art 1 s 2</a>; <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/laws/2023/0/45/#laws.2.1.0">2023 c 45 art 2 s 1</a>-3</p>
      </div>
      <div class="section" id="stat.356.42">
        <h1 class="shn">356.42 POSTRETIREMENT ADJUSTMENT; LUMP-SUM PAYMENTS.</h1>
        <div class="subd" id="stat.356.42.1">
          <a title="Link to Subdivision 1." class="permalink" href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/statutes/2024/cite/356/full#stat.356.42.1">§</a>
          <h2 class="subd_no">Subdivision 1.<span class="headnote">Entitlement.</span></h2>
          <p>A person who is receiving a retirement annuity, a disability benefit, or a surviving spouse's annuity or benefit from a retirement fund specified in subdivision 3, clauses (1) to (8), is entitled to receive a postretirement adjustment from the applicable retirement fund in the amount specified in subdivision 2, if the annuity or benefit was computed under:</p>
          <p>(1) the laws in effect before June 1, 1973, if the person is receiving an annuity or benefit from the retirement fund specified in subdivision 3, clause (4);</p>
          <p>(2) the laws in effect before July 1, 1973, if the person is receiving an annuity or benefit from a retirement fund specified in subdivision 3, clause (1), (2), (3), or (5);</p>
          <p>(3) the Metropolitan Transit Commission transit operating division employees retirement fund plan document in effect on or before December 31, 1977, if the person is receiving a retirement annuity, a disability benefit, or a surviving spouse's annuity or benefit from the retirement fund specified in subdivision 3, clause (5);</p>
          <p>(4) the laws in effect before May 1, 1974, and before any adjustment under Laws 1987, chapter 372, article 3, if the person is receiving an annuity or benefit from the retirement fund specified in subdivision 3, clause (6);</p>
          <p>(5) the laws in effect before January 1, 1970, if the person is receiving an annuity or benefit from the retirement fund specified in subdivision 3, clause (7); or</p>
          <p>(6) the laws in effect before June 30, 1971, if the person is receiving an annuity or benefit from the retirement fund specified in subdivision 3, clause (8).</p>
        </div>
        <div class="subd" id="stat.356.42.2">
          <a title="Link to Subd. 2." class="permalink" href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/statutes/2024/cite/356/full#stat.356.42.2">§</a>
          <h2 class="subd_no">Subd. 2.<span class="headnote">Amount of postretirement adjustment; payment.</span></h2>
          <p>(a) For any person receiving an annuity or benefit on November 30, 1989, and entitled to receive a postretirement adjustment under subdivision 1, the postretirement adjustment is a lump-sum payment calculated under paragraph (b) or (c).</p>
          <p>(b) For coordinated plan annuity or benefit recipients, the postretirement adjustment in 1989 is $25 for each full year of allowable service credited to the person by the respective retirement fund. In 1990 and each following year, the postretirement adjustment is the amount payable in the preceding year increased by the same percentage applied to regular annuities paid from the postretirement fund or, for the retirement funds specified in subdivision 3, clauses (6), (7), and (8), by the same percentage applied under the articles of incorporation and bylaws of these funds.</p>
          <p>(c) For basic plan annuity or benefit recipients, the postretirement adjustment in 1989 is the greater of:</p>
          <p>(1) $25 for each full year of allowable service credited to the person by the respective retirement fund; or</p>
          <p>(2) the difference between:</p>
          <p>(i) the product of $400 times the number of full years of allowable service credited to the person by the respective retirement fund; and</p>
          <p>(ii) the sum of the benefits payable to the person from any Minnesota public employee pension plan, and cash benefits payable to the person from the Social Security Administration.</p>
          <p>In 1990 and each following year, each eligible basic plan annuity or benefit recipient shall receive the amount received in the preceding year increased by the same percentage applied to regular annuities paid from the postretirement fund or, for the retirement funds specified in subdivision 3, clauses (6), (7), and (8), by the same percentage applied under the articles of incorporation and bylaws of these funds.</p>
          <p>(d) The postretirement adjustment provided for in this section must be paid on December 1 to those persons receiving an annuity or benefit on the preceding November 30. This section does not authorize the payment of a postretirement adjustment to an estate if the annuity or benefit recipient dies before the November 30 eligibility date. The postretirement adjustment provided for in this section must be paid automatically unless the intended recipient files a written notice with the retirement fund requesting that the postretirement adjustment not be paid or returns the amount of adjustment to the retirement fund. Written notice of the waiver of the postretirement adjustment is irrevocable for the year during which it was made.</p>
        </div>
        <div class="subd" id="stat.356.42.3">
          <a title="Link to Subd. 3." class="permalink" href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/statutes/2024/cite/356/full#stat.356.42.3">§</a>
          <h2 class="subd_no">Subd. 3.<span class="headnote">Covered retirement plans.</span></h2>
          <p>The postretirement adjustment provided in this section applies to the following retirement funds:</p>
          <p>(1) the general employees retirement plans of the Public Employees Retirement Association;</p>
          <p>(2) the public employees police and fire plan of the Public Employees Retirement Association;</p>
          <p>(3) the teachers retirement association;</p>
          <p>(4) the State Patrol retirement plan;</p>
          <p>(5) the state employees retirement plan of the Minnesota State Retirement System; and</p>
          <p>(6) the St. Paul Teachers Retirement Fund Association established under chapter 354A.</p>
        </div>
      </div>
      <div class="history" id="stat.356.42.history
			">
        <h2>History: </h2>
        <p class="first"><a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/laws/2002/0/392/#laws.11.30.0">2002 c 392 art 11 s 30</a>; <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/laws/2006/0/277/#laws.3.40.0">2006 c 277 art 3 s 40</a>; <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/laws/2014/0/296/#laws.6.41.0">2014 c 296 art 6 s 41</a>,49</p>
      </div>
      <div class="sr" id="stat.356.43"><b>356.43</b> [Repealed, <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/laws/2010/0/359/#laws.11.27.0">2010 c 359 art 11 s 27</a>]</div>
      <div class="section" id="stat.356.431">
        <h1 class="shn">356.431 CONVERSION OF LUMP-SUM POSTRETIREMENT AND SUPPLEMENTAL PAYMENT TO AN INCREASED MONTHLY ANNUITY.</h1>
        <div class="subd" id="stat.356.431.1">
          <a title="Link to Subdivision 1." class="permalink" href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/statutes/2024/cite/356/full#stat.356.431.1">§</a>
          <h2 class="subd_no">Subdivision 1.<span class="headnote">Lump-sum postretirement payment conversion.</span></h2>
          <p>For benefits paid to eligible persons under Minnesota Statutes 2014, section <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/statutes/cite/356.42">356.42</a>, the amount of the most recent lump-sum benefit payable to an eligible recipient under Minnesota Statutes 2014, section <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/statutes/cite/356.42">356.42</a> must be divided by 12. The result must be added to the monthly annuity or benefit otherwise payable to an eligible recipient, must become a permanent part of the benefit recipient's pension, and must be included in any pension benefit subject to future postretirement adjustments.</p>
        </div>
        <div class="subd" id="stat.356.431.2">
          <a title="Link to Subd. 2." class="permalink" href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/statutes/2024/cite/356/full#stat.356.431.2">§</a>
          <h2 class="subd_no">Subd. 2.</h2>
          <p>[Repealed, <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/laws/2009/0/169/#laws.1.77.0">2009 c 169 art 1 s 77</a>]</p>
        </div>
      </div>
      <div class="history" id="stat.356.431.history
			">
        <h2>History: </h2>
        <p class="first"><a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/laws/2002/0/392/#laws.11.32.0">2002 c 392 art 11 s 32</a>; <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/laws/2005/0/10/#laws.5.3.0">2005 c 10 art 5 s 3</a>; <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/laws/2010/0/359/#laws.12.33.0">2010 c 359 art 12 s 33</a>; <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/laws/2015/0/68/#laws.13.54.0">2015 c 68 art 13 s 54</a></p>
      </div>
      <h1 class="fgh">REFUNDS</h1>
      <div class="sr" id="stat.356.44"><b>356.44</b> MS 2018 [Repealed, <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/laws/2020/0/108/#laws.5.18.0">2020 c 108 art 5 s 18</a>]</div>
      <div class="section" id="stat.356.441">
        <h1 class="shn">356.441 PAYMENT ACCEPTANCE ALLOWED.</h1>
        <div class="subd" id="stat.356.441.1">
          <a title="Link to Subdivision 1." class="permalink" href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/statutes/2024/cite/356/full#stat.356.441.1">§</a>
          <h2 class="subd_no">Subdivision 1.<span class="headnote">Payment authorization.</span></h2>
          <p>The repayment of a refund and interest on that refund or the payment of equivalent contributions and interest for an eligible leave of absence, as permitted under laws governing any public pension plan in Minnesota, may be made:</p>
          <p>(1) with funds distributed or transferred from a plan qualified under the federal Internal Revenue Code of 1986, section 401, subsection (a) or (k); 403; 408; or 457, subsection (b), as amended from time to time; or</p>
          <p>(2) with funds distributed from an individual retirement account or individual retirement annuity, if done solely in a manner that is eligible for treatment as a nontaxable rollover or transfer under the applicable federal law.</p>
        </div>
        <div class="subd" id="stat.356.441.2">
          <a title="Link to Subd. 2." class="permalink" href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/statutes/2024/cite/356/full#stat.356.441.2">§</a>
          <h2 class="subd_no">Subd. 2.<span class="headnote">Separate accounting requirement.</span></h2>
          <p>Nontaxable rollovers or transfer amounts under subdivision 1 received by a public pension fund must be separately accounted for as member contributions not previously taxed. Before accepting any rollovers or transfers to which this section applies, the executive director shall require the member to provide written documentation to demonstrate that the amounts to be rolled over or transferred are eligible for a tax-free rollover or transfer and qualify for that treatment under the federal Internal Revenue Code of 1986, as amended.</p>
        </div>
      </div>
      <div class="history" id="stat.356.441.history
			">
        <h2>History: </h2>
        <p class="first"><a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/laws/2002/0/392/#laws.11.34.0">2002 c 392 art 11 s 34</a>; <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/laws/2004/0/267/#laws.9.22.0">2004 c 267 art 9 s 22</a></p>
      </div>
      <div class="sr" id="stat.356.45"><b>356.45</b> [Repealed, <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/laws/2002/0/392/#laws.11.53.0">2002 c 392 art 11 s 53</a>]</div>
      <div class="sr" id="stat.356.451"><b>356.451</b> [Repealed, <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/laws/2002/0/392/#laws.11.53.0">2002 c 392 art 11 s 53</a>]</div>
      <div class="sr" id="stat.356.452"><b>356.452</b> [Repealed, <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/laws/2002/0/392/#laws.11.53.0">2002 c 392 art 11 s 53</a>]</div>
      <div class="sr" id="stat.356.453"><b>356.453</b> [Repealed, <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/laws/2002/0/392/#laws.11.53.0">2002 c 392 art 11 s 53</a>]</div>
      <div class="sr" id="stat.356.454"><b>356.454</b> [Repealed, <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/laws/2002/0/392/#laws.11.53.0">2002 c 392 art 11 s 53</a>]</div>
      <div class="sr" id="stat.356.455"><b>356.455</b> [Repealed, <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/laws/2002/0/392/#laws.11.53.0">2002 c 392 art 11 s 53</a>]</div>
      <h1 class="fgh">OPTIONAL ANNUITY FORMS</h1>
      <div class="section" id="stat.356.46">
        <h1 class="shn">356.46 APPLICATION FOR RETIREMENT ANNUITY; PROCEDURE FOR ELECTING ANNUITY FORM; MANDATORY JOINT AND SURVIVOR OPTIONAL ANNUITY FORM.</h1>
        <div class="subd" id="stat.356.46.1">
          <a title="Link to Subdivision 1." class="permalink" href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/statutes/2024/cite/356/full#stat.356.46.1">§</a>
          <h2 class="subd_no">Subdivision 1.<span class="headnote">Definitions.</span></h2>
          <p class="in">As used in this section, each of the following terms shall have the meaning given.</p>
          <p class="in">(a) "Annuity form" means the payment procedure and duration of a retirement annuity or disability benefit available to a member of a public pension plan, based on the period over which a retirement annuity or disability benefit is payable, determined by the number of persons to whom the retirement annuity or disability benefit is payable, and the amount of the retirement annuity or disability benefit which is payable to each person.</p>
          <p class="in">(b) "Joint and survivor optional annuity" means an optional annuity form which provides a retirement annuity or disability benefit to a retired member or disabilitant and the spouse of the member or disabilitant on a joint basis during the lifetime of the retired member or disabilitant and all or a portion of the original retirement annuity or disability benefit amount to the surviving spouse in the event of the death of the retired member or disabilitant.</p>
          <p class="in">(c) "Optional annuity form" means an annuity form which is elected by a member and is not provided automatically as the standard annuity form of the public pension plan.</p>
          <p class="in">(d) "Public pension plan" means a public pension plan as defined under section <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/statutes/cite/356.63">356.63, paragraph (b)</a>.</p>
          <p class="in">(e) "Retirement annuity" means a series of monthly payments to which a former or retired member of a public pension plan is entitled due to attaining a specified age and acquiring credit for a specified period of service, which includes a retirement annuity, retirement allowance, or service pension.</p>
          <p class="in">(f) "Disability benefit" means a series of monthly payments to which a former or disabled member of a public pension plan is entitled due to a physical or mental inability to engage in specified employment.</p>
        </div>
        <div class="subd" id="stat.356.46.2">
          <a title="Link to Subd. 2." class="permalink" href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/statutes/2024/cite/356/full#stat.356.46.2">§</a>
          <h2 class="subd_no">Subd. 2.<span class="headnote">Provision of information on annuity forms.</span></h2>
          <p class="in">(a) Every public pension plan which provides for an annuity form other than a single life annuity as an option which can be elected by an active, disabled, or retiring member shall provide as a part of, or accompanying the annuity application form, a written statement summarizing the annuity forms which are available, a general indication of the consequences of selecting one annuity form over another, a calculation of the actuarial reduction in the amount of the retirement annuity which would be required for each optional annuity form, and the procedure to be followed to obtain more information from the public pension plan administration concerning all annuity forms provided by the plan. If the public pension plan offers joint and survivor optional annuity forms, the annuity application and accompanying information must include a statement informing the member and the member's spouse that, notwithstanding any law to the contrary, unless the spouse waives any rights to an optional annuity by a notarized statement on the annuity application or other form provided by the pension plan administration, the public pension plan administration shall assume that the member selected the 50 percent joint and survivor optional annuity form.</p>
          <p class="in">(b) In lieu of the notarized statement requirement referred to in paragraph (a), the pension plan administration may accept a statement which has been verified, including electronic verification, by administrators of the pension plan.</p>
        </div>
        <div class="subd" id="stat.356.46.3">
          <a title="Link to Subd. 3." class="permalink" href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/statutes/2024/cite/356/full#stat.356.46.3">§</a>
          <h2 class="subd_no">Subd. 3.<span class="headnote">Requirement of notice to member's spouse.</span></h2>
          <p class="in">(a) Every public pension plan administration that provides for a joint and survivor optional retirement or disability annuity potentially applicable to the surviving spouse of a member shall send a copy of the written statement required by subdivision 2 to the spouse of the member before the member's selection of the form of retirement or disability benefit.</p>
          <p class="in">(b) Following the selection of a retirement or disability annuity by the member, a copy of the completed annuity application and annuity beneficiary form, if applicable, must be sent by the executive director of the public pension plan to the spouse of the retiring or disabled member. A signed acknowledgment must be required from the spouse confirming receipt of a copy of the completed annuity application and annuity beneficiary form, unless the spouse's signature acknowledging the annuity form selected is on the annuity application or other form as designated by the plan. If the public pension plan administration has not received from the spouse within 30 days a signed acknowledgment, because the annuity application or other form as designated by the public pension plan administration did not include the spouse's signature, the executive director of the public pension plan must notify the member and the member's spouse that the 50 percent joint and survivor annuity form, or a higher joint and survivor form if selected, shall be paid if the spouse does not acknowledge the annuity form selected by the member by responding to the second notice sent to the spouse within 30 days. The second notice must be sent by certified mail with restricted delivery.</p>
          <p class="in">(c) If a public pension plan administration receives notice that the provisions of this section have not been complied with, or if a member selects a benefit form without the valid consent of the member's spouse, the executive director of the public pension plan shall suspend the payment of monthly benefits and shall take all actions necessary to comply with this subdivision.</p>
          <p class="in">(d) For the Teachers Retirement Association, the statement to the spouse that is required under paragraph (a) must be sent before or upon the member's election of an annuity.</p>
        </div>
        <div class="subd" id="stat.356.46.4">
          <a title="Link to Subd. 4." class="permalink" href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/statutes/2024/cite/356/full#stat.356.46.4">§</a>
          <h2 class="subd_no">Subd. 4.<span class="headnote">Plan exclusions.</span></h2>
          <p class="in">This section does not apply to:</p>
          <p class="in">(1) any firefighters relief association to which sections <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/statutes/cite/424A.091">424A.091</a> to <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/statutes/cite/424A.096">424A.096</a> apply; and</p>
          <p class="in">(2) any plan under which the applicable surviving spouse would receive automatic surviving spouse coverage if a joint and survivor annuity were not elected.</p>
        </div>
        <div class="subd" id="stat.356.46.5">
          <a title="Link to Subd. 5." class="permalink" href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/statutes/2024/cite/356/full#stat.356.46.5">§</a>
          <h2 class="subd_no">Subd. 5.<span class="headnote">Disabilitant survivor treatment.</span></h2>
          <p class="in">This section should not be interpreted as prohibiting payment of a survivor annuity to the spouse of a deceased disabilitant, in lieu of any other annuity, if laws specific to the plan provide for a higher surviving spouse annuity.</p>
        </div>
        <div class="subd" id="stat.356.46.6">
          <a title="Link to Subd. 6." class="permalink" href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/statutes/2024/cite/356/full#stat.356.46.6">§</a>
          <h2 class="subd_no">Subd. 6.<span class="headnote">Limitations due to marriage dissolution.</span></h2>
          <p class="in">The requirement to pay a 50 percent joint and survivor annuity is void if there is a court order to the contrary.</p>
        </div>
        <div class="subd" id="stat.356.46.7">
          <a title="Link to Subd. 7." class="permalink" href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/statutes/2024/cite/356/full#stat.356.46.7">§</a>
          <h2 class="subd_no">Subd. 7.<span class="headnote">Liability waiver.</span></h2>
          <p class="in">The pension fund and plan, its employees, and any agent working on behalf of the plan administration are not liable for harm caused by any act of fraud committed by the retiring member or current or previous spouse, or any information withheld from, or incorrect information supplied to the plan administration.</p>
        </div>
      </div>
      <div class="history" id="stat.356.46.history
			">
        <h2>History: </h2>
        <p class="first"><a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/laws/2002/0/392/#laws.11.35.0">2002 c 392 art 11 s 35</a>; <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/laws/2003/0/2/#laws.1.41.0">2003 c 2 art 1 s 41</a>; <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/laws/2007/0/134/#laws.2.44.0">2007 c 134 art 2 s 44</a>; <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/laws/2008/0/349/#laws.4.7.0">2008 c 349 art 4 s 7</a>; <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/laws/2013/0/111/#laws.5.80.0">2013 c 111 art 5 s 80</a>; <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/laws/2024/0/102/#laws.2.31.0">2024 c 102 art 2 s 31</a></p>
      </div>
      <div class="section" id="stat.356.461">
        <h1 class="shn">356.461 VARIOUS RETIREMENT SYSTEMS; JOINT AND SURVIVOR ANNUITY COMPUTATION.</h1>
        <div class="subd" id="stat.356.461.1">
          <a title="Link to Subdivision 1." class="permalink" href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/statutes/2024/cite/356/full#stat.356.461.1">§</a>
          <h2 class="subd_no">Subdivision 1.<span class="headnote">Joint and survivor annuity computation.</span></h2>
          <p>Notwithstanding any provision of section <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/statutes/cite/356.215#stat.356.215.8">356.215, subdivision 8</a>, or <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/statutes/cite/356.415">356.415</a> to the contrary, for purposes of computing joint and survivor annuities, the applicable investment return assumption is 6.5 percent.</p>
        </div>
        <div class="subd" id="stat.356.461.2">
          <a title="Link to Subd. 2." class="permalink" href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/statutes/2024/cite/356/full#stat.356.461.2">§</a>
          <h2 class="subd_no">Subd. 2.<span class="headnote">Covered plans.</span></h2>
          <p>This section applies to the following retirement plans:</p>
          <p>(1) the legislators retirement plan, established under chapter 3A, including constitutional officers as specified in that chapter;</p>
          <p>(2) the correctional state employees retirement plan of the Minnesota State Retirement System, established under chapter 352;</p>
          <p>(3) the general state employees retirement plan of the Minnesota State Retirement System, established under chapter 352;</p>
          <p>(4) the State Patrol retirement plan, established under chapter 352B;</p>
          <p>(5) the unclassified state employees retirement program of the Minnesota State Retirement System, established under chapter 352D;</p>
          <p>(6) the judges retirement plan, established under chapter 490;</p>
          <p>(7) the general employees retirement plan of the Public Employees Retirement Association, established under chapter 353;</p>
          <p>(8) the public employees police and fire retirement plan of the Public Employees Retirement Association, established under chapter 353;</p>
          <p>(9) the local government correctional service retirement plan of the Public Employees Retirement Association, established under chapter 353E; and</p>
          <p>(10) the Teachers Retirement Association, established under chapter 354.</p>
        </div>
      </div>
      <div class="history" id="stat.356.461.history
			">
        <h2>History: </h2>
        <p class="first"><a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/laws/2014/0/296/#laws.4.8.0">2014 c 296 art 4 s 8</a>; <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/laws/2015/0/68/#laws.14.26.0">2015 c 68 art 14 s 26</a>; <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/laws/2019/1/8/#laws.8.23.0">1Sp2019 c 8 art 8 s 23</a></p>
      </div>
      <div class="section" id="stat.356.465">
        <h1 class="shn">356.465 SUPPLEMENTAL NEEDS TRUST AS OPTIONAL ANNUITY FORM RECIPIENT.</h1>
        <div class="subd" id="stat.356.465.1">
          <a title="Link to Subdivision 1." class="permalink" href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/statutes/2024/cite/356/full#stat.356.465.1">§</a>
          <h2 class="subd_no">Subdivision 1.<span class="headnote">Inclusion as recipient.</span></h2>
          <p> A retiring member may designate a qualified supplemental needs trust under subdivision 2 as the remainder recipient on an optional retirement annuity form for a period not to exceed the lifetime of the beneficiary of the supplemental needs trust.</p>
        </div>
        <div class="subd" id="stat.356.465.2">
          <a title="Link to Subd. 2." class="permalink" href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/statutes/2024/cite/356/full#stat.356.465.2">§</a>
          <h2 class="subd_no">Subd. 2.<span class="headnote">Definition of qualified supplemental needs trust.</span></h2>
          <p>A qualified supplemental needs trust is a trust that:</p>
          <p>(1) was established on or after July 1, 1992;</p>
          <p>(2) was established solely for the benefit of one person who has a disability under federal Social Security Administration Supplemental Security Income or retirement, survivors, and disability insurance disability determination standards and who was determined as such before the creation of the trust;</p>
          <p>(3) is funded, in whole or in part, by the primary recipient of the optional annuity form and, unless the trust is a Zebley trust, is not funded by the beneficiary, the beneficiary's spouse, or a person who is required to pay a sum to or for the trust beneficiary under the terms of litigation or a litigation settlement;</p>
          <p>(4) is established to cover reasonable living expenses and other basic needs of the disabilitant, in whole or in part, in instances when public assistance does not provide sufficiently for these needs;</p>
          <p>(5) is not permitted to make disbursement to replace or reduce public assistance otherwise available;</p>
          <p>(6) is irrevocable;</p>
          <p>(7) terminates upon the death of the disabled person for whose benefit it was established; and</p>
          <p>(8) is determined by the executive director to be a trust that contains excluded assets for purposes of the qualification for public entitlement benefits under the applicable federal and state laws and regulations.</p>
        </div>
        <div class="subd" id="stat.356.465.3">
          <a title="Link to Subd. 3." class="permalink" href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/statutes/2024/cite/356/full#stat.356.465.3">§</a>
          <h2 class="subd_no">Subd. 3.<span class="headnote">Covered retirement plans.</span></h2>
          <p>The provisions of this section apply to the following retirement plans:</p>
          <p>(1) the general state employees retirement plan of the Minnesota State Retirement System established under chapter 352;</p>
          <p>(2) the correctional state employees retirement plan of the Minnesota State Retirement System established under chapter 352;</p>
          <p>(3) the State Patrol retirement plan established under chapter 352B;</p>
          <p>(4) the legislators retirement plan established under chapter 3A;</p>
          <p>(5) the judges retirement plan established under chapter 490;</p>
          <p>(6) the general employees retirement plan of the Public Employees Retirement Association established under chapter 353;</p>
          <p>(7) the public employees police and fire plan of the Public Employees Retirement Association established under chapter 353;</p>
          <p>(8) the teachers retirement plan established under chapter 354;</p>
          <p>(9) the St. Paul Teachers Retirement Fund Association established under chapter 354A; and</p>
          <p>(10) the local government correctional service retirement plan of the Public Employees Retirement Association established under chapter 353E.</p>
        </div>
        <div class="subd" id="stat.356.465.4">
          <a title="Link to Subd. 4." class="permalink" href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/statutes/2024/cite/356/full#stat.356.465.4">§</a>
          <h2 class="subd_no">Subd. 4.<span class="headnote">Expanded eligibility.</span></h2>
          <p>(a) Notwithstanding subdivision 1, for a retirement plan specified in paragraph (b), a designation under subdivision 1 may be made by an active, disabled, deferred, or retiring member.</p>
          <p>(b) The applicable plan is the Teachers Retirement Association established under chapter 354.</p>
        </div>
      </div>
      <div class="history" id="stat.356.465.history
			">
        <h2>History: </h2>
        <p class="first"><a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/laws/2002/0/392/#laws.11.36.0">2002 c 392 art 11 s 36</a>; <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/laws/2006/0/277/#laws.3.41.0">2006 c 277 art 3 s 41</a>; <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/laws/2009/0/169/#laws.4.41.0">2009 c 169 art 4 s 41</a>,42; <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/laws/2010/0/359/#laws.12.34.0">2010 c 359 art 12 s 34</a>; <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/laws/2011/1/8/#laws.6.19.0">1Sp2011 c 8 art 6 s 19; art 7 s 19; art 8 s 9</a>,14; <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/laws/2014/0/296/#laws.6.42.0">2014 c 296 art 6 s 42</a>,49; <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/laws/2015/0/68/#laws.14.27.0">2015 c 68 art 14 s 27</a></p>
      </div>
      <h1 class="fgh">REEMPLOYED ANNUITANT EARNINGS DISPOSITION</h1>
      <div class="section" id="stat.356.47">
        <h1 class="shn">356.47 DISPOSITION OF AMOUNT IN EXCESS OF REEMPLOYED ANNUITANT EARNINGS LIMITATIONS.</h1>
        <div class="subd" id="stat.356.47.1">
          <a title="Link to Subdivision 1." class="permalink" href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/statutes/2024/cite/356/full#stat.356.47.1">§</a>
          <h2 class="subd_no">Subdivision 1.<span class="headnote">Application.</span></h2>
          <p>(a) This section applies to the balance of annual retirement annuities on the amount of retirement annuity reductions after reemployed annuitant earnings limitations for retirement plans governed by section <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/statutes/cite/352.115#stat.352.115.10">352.115, subdivision 10</a>; <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/statutes/cite/353.37">353.37</a>; or <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/statutes/cite/354.44#stat.354.44.5">354.44, subdivision 5</a>.</p>
          <p>(b) This section also applies to the balance of annual retirement annuities on the amount of retirement annuity reductions under section <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/statutes/cite/354A.31#stat.354A.31.3">354A.31, subdivision 3</a>, for members of the St. Paul Teachers Retirement Fund Association whose effective date of retirement is before July 1, 2013.</p>
        </div>
        <div class="subd" id="stat.356.47.2">
          <a title="Link to Subd. 2." class="permalink" href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/statutes/2024/cite/356/full#stat.356.47.2">§</a>
          <h2 class="subd_no">Subd. 2.<span class="headnote">Record keeping; reporting.</span></h2>
          <p>The chief administrative officer of each retirement plan shall keep records for each reemployed annuitant of the amount of the annuity reduction. This amount must be reported to each member at least once each year.</p>
        </div>
        <div class="subd" id="stat.356.47.3">
          <a title="Link to Subd. 3." class="permalink" href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/statutes/2024/cite/356/full#stat.356.47.3">§</a>
          <h2 class="subd_no">Subd. 3.<span class="headnote">Payment.</span></h2>
          <p class="in">(a) Beginning one year after the reemployment withholding period ends relating to the reemployment that gave rise to the limitation, and the filing of a written application, the retired member is entitled to the payment, in a lump sum, of the value of the person's amount under subdivision 2, plus annual compound interest. For the general state employees retirement plan, the correctional state employees retirement plan, the general employees retirement plan of the Public Employees Retirement Association, the public employees police and fire retirement plan, the local government correctional employees retirement plan, and the teachers retirement plan, the annual interest rate is six percent from the date on which the amount was deducted from the retirement annuity to the date of payment or until January 1, 2011, whichever is earlier, and no interest after January 1, 2011. For the St. Paul Teachers Retirement Fund Association, the annual interest is the rate of six percent from the date that the amount was deducted from the retirement annuity to the date of payment or June 30, 2011, whichever is earlier, and with no interest accrual after June 30, 2011.</p>
          <p class="in">(b) The written application must be on a form prescribed by the chief administrative officer of the applicable retirement plan.</p>
          <p class="in">(c) If the retired member dies before the payment provided for in paragraph (a) is made, the amount is payable, upon written application, to the deceased person's surviving spouse, or if none, to the deceased person's designated beneficiary, or if none, to the deceased person's estate.</p>
          <p class="in">(d) If the amount under subdivision 2 is an eligible rollover distribution as defined in section <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/statutes/cite/356.633#stat.356.633.1">356.633, subdivision 1</a>, paragraph (d), the applicable retirement plan shall provide notice and an election:</p>
          <p class="in">(1) to the member regarding the member's right to elect a direct rollover under section <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/statutes/cite/356.633">356.633</a>, subdivisions 1 and 2, in lieu of a direct payment; or</p>
          <p>(2) if paragraph (c) applies and the amount is to be paid to a person who is a distributee as defined in section <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/statutes/cite/356.633#stat.356.633.1">356.633, subdivision 1</a>, paragraph (b), to the distributee regarding the distributee's right to elect a direct rollover under section <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/statutes/cite/356.633">356.633</a>, subdivisions 1 and 2, in lieu of a direct payment.</p>
        </div>
      </div>
      <div class="history" id="stat.356.47.history
			">
        <h2>History: </h2>
        <p class="first"><a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/laws/2002/0/392/#laws.11.37.0">2002 c 392 art 11 s 37</a>; <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/laws/2005/1/8/#laws.3.7.0">1Sp2005 c 8 art 3 s 7</a>; <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/laws/2008/0/349/#laws.5.31.0">2008 c 349 art 5 s 31</a>; <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/laws/2010/0/359/#laws.1.83.0">2010 c 359 art 1 s 83</a>; <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/laws/2011/1/8/#laws.2.20.0">1Sp2011 c 8 art 2 s 20</a>; <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/laws/2013/0/111/#laws.13.18.0">2013 c 111 art 13 s 18</a>; <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/laws/2014/0/275/#laws.2.14.0">2014 c 275 art 2 s 14</a>; <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/laws/2014/0/296/#laws.6.43.0">2014 c 296 art 6 s 43</a>,49; <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/laws/2018/0/211/#laws.13.2.0">2018 c 211 art 13 s 2</a>; art 19 s 8; <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/laws/2024/0/102/#laws.8.15.0">2024 c 102 art 8 s 15</a></p>
      </div>
      <h1 class="fgh">MARRIAGE DISSOLUTION RETIREMENT<br>COVERAGE INFORMATION</h1>
      <div class="section" id="stat.356.48">
        <h1 class="shn">356.48 REVOCATION OF OPTIONAL ANNUITY DUE TO MARRIAGE DISSOLUTION OR ANNULMENT.</h1>
        <div class="subd" id="stat.356.48.1">
          <a title="Link to Subdivision 1." class="permalink" href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/statutes/2024/cite/356/full#stat.356.48.1">§</a>
          <h2 class="subd_no">Subdivision 1.<span class="headnote">Covered plans.</span></h2>
          <p>This section applies to the following retirement plans:</p>
          <p>(1) the general state employees retirement plan of the Minnesota State Retirement System established under chapter 352;</p>
          <p>(2) the correctional state employees retirement plan of the Minnesota State Retirement System established under chapter 352;</p>
          <p>(3) the State Patrol retirement plan established under chapter 352B;</p>
          <p>(4) the unclassified state employees retirement program of the Minnesota State Retirement System established under chapter 352D;</p>
          <p>(5) the general employee retirement plan of the Public Employees Retirement Association established under chapter 353;</p>
          <p>(6) the public employees police and fire retirement plan established under chapter 353;</p>
          <p>(7) the local government correctional employees retirement plan of the Public Employees Retirement Association established under chapter 353E;</p>
          <p>(8) the Teachers Retirement Association established under chapter 354;</p>
          <p>(9) the St. Paul Teachers Retirement Fund Association established under chapter 354A; and</p>
          <p>(10) the uniform judicial retirement plan established under chapter 490.</p>
        </div>
        <div class="subd" id="stat.356.48.2">
          <a title="Link to Subd. 2." class="permalink" href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/statutes/2024/cite/356/full#stat.356.48.2">§</a>
          <h2 class="subd_no">Subd. 2.<span class="headnote">Treatment.</span></h2>
          <p>(a) The treatment specified in this section applies if, after the accrual date of an annuity or benefit from an applicable plan or plans, a marriage dissolution decree or annulment decree is rendered that specifies that the designation of an optional annuity must be revoked and if the other requirements specified in this section are satisfied.</p>
          <p>(b) Notwithstanding any law to the contrary, if the applicable pension plan or plans have provisions of law that revise the monthly benefit amount payable to the primary annuitant upon the death of the individual named as the optional joint annuitant, the monthly benefit amount must be recomputed as though the individual that had been named as the optional joint annuitant died on the date a certified copy of the marriage dissolution or annulment decree is received by the chief administrative officer. Payment of any benefit adjustment under this section is prospective only.</p>
        </div>
        <div class="subd" id="stat.356.48.3">
          <a title="Link to Subd. 3." class="permalink" href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/statutes/2024/cite/356/full#stat.356.48.3">§</a>
          <h2 class="subd_no">Subd. 3.<span class="headnote">Restrictions.</span></h2>
          <p>(a) This section does not apply if the marriage dissolution decree or annulment decree is not consistent with the requirements under section <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/statutes/cite/518.58">518.58</a>.</p>
          <p>(b) The pension plan benefit recipient must not designate, and the court may not require that the member designate, a subsequent optional annuity beneficiary.</p>
          <p>(c) This section does not apply if more than one surviving individual was named as an optional joint annuitant.</p>
        </div>
        <div class="subd" id="stat.356.48.4">
          <a title="Link to Subd. 4." class="permalink" href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/statutes/2024/cite/356/full#stat.356.48.4">§</a>
          <h2 class="subd_no">Subd. 4.<span class="headnote">Submission of documentation.</span></h2>
          <p>To receive the treatment provided in this section, an eligible retiree or disabilitant must provide, to the chief administrative officer of the applicable pension plan, a certified copy of the marriage dissolution or annulment decree. The retiree or disabilitant and the joint annuitant must also submit a form, prescribed by the chief administrative officer of the applicable pension plan and signed by both individuals, requesting the annuity bounce back as provided in subdivision 2. The individuals must also provide any other documentation the chief administrative officer may request.</p>
        </div>
      </div>
      <div class="history" id="stat.356.48.history
			">
        <h2>History: </h2>
        <p class="first"><a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/laws/2010/0/359/#laws.10.1.0">2010 c 359 art 10 s 1</a>; <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/laws/2013/0/111/#laws.7.8.0">2013 c 111 art 7 s 8</a></p>
      </div>
      <div class="section" id="stat.356.49">
        <h1 class="shn">356.49 PROVISION OF INFORMATION IN EVENT OF MARRIAGE DISSOLUTION.</h1>
        <div class="subd" id="stat.356.49.1">
          <a title="Link to Subdivision 1." class="permalink" href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/statutes/2024/cite/356/full#stat.356.49.1">§</a>
          <h2 class="subd_no">Subdivision 1.<span class="headnote">Information for pending marriage dissolution.</span></h2>
          <p>(a) Upon receipt of a written request by a person with access to the data under subdivision 3 who cites this statute, a public or private pension plan administrator must provide the court and the parties to a marriage dissolution action involving a plan member or former plan member with information regarding pension benefits or rights of the plan member or former plan member. The pension plan shall provide this information upon the request of the court or a party to the action without requiring a signed authorization from the plan member or former plan member.</p>
          <p>(b) The information must include the pension benefits or rights of the plan member or former plan member as of the first day of the month following the date of the request, or as of the end of the previous fiscal year for the plan, and as of the date of valuation of marital assets under section <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/statutes/cite/518.58">518.58</a>, if the person requesting the information specifies that date. The information must include the accrued service credit of the person, the credited salary of the person for the most current five-year period, a summary of the benefit plan, and any other information relevant to the calculation of the present value of the benefits or rights.</p>
        </div>
        <div class="subd" id="stat.356.49.2">
          <a title="Link to Subd. 2." class="permalink" href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/statutes/2024/cite/356/full#stat.356.49.2">§</a>
          <h2 class="subd_no">Subd. 2.</h2>
          <p>[Repealed, <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/laws/2015/0/68/#laws.13.65.0">2015 c 68 art 13 s 65</a>]</p>
        </div>
        <div class="subd" id="stat.356.49.3">
          <a title="Link to Subd. 3." class="permalink" href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/statutes/2024/cite/356/full#stat.356.49.3">§</a>
          <h2 class="subd_no">Subd. 3.<span class="headnote">Access to data.</span></h2>
          <p>Notwithstanding any provision of chapter 13 to the contrary, an administrator may release private or confidential data on individuals to the court, the parties to a marriage dissolution, their attorneys, and an actuary appointed under section <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/statutes/cite/518.582">518.582</a>, to the extent necessary to comply with this section, but only if the administrator has received a copy of the legal petition showing that an action for marriage dissolution has commenced and a copy of the affidavit of service showing that the petition has been served on the responding party to the action.</p>
        </div>
      </div>
      <div class="history" id="stat.356.49.history
			">
        <h2>History: </h2>
        <p class="first">
          <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/laws/2002/0/392/#laws.11.38.0">2002 c 392 art 11 s 38</a>
        </p>
      </div>
      <h1 class="fgh">SERVICE AND SALARY CREDIT</h1>
      <div class="section" id="stat.356.50">
        <h1 class="shn">356.50 SERVICE AND SALARY CREDIT FROM BACK PAY AWARDS IN THE EVENT OF WRONGFUL DISCHARGE; ANNUITY AND DISABILITY TREATMENT.</h1>
        <div class="subd" id="stat.356.50.1">
          <a title="Link to Subdivision 1." class="permalink" href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/statutes/2024/cite/356/full#stat.356.50.1">§</a>
          <h2 class="subd_no">Subdivision 1.<span class="headnote">Application.</span></h2>
          <p>(a) A person who is wrongfully discharged from public employment that gave rise to coverage by a public employee pension plan enumerated in section <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/statutes/cite/356.30#stat.356.30.3">356.30, subdivision 3</a>, is entitled to obtain allowable service credit from the applicable public employee pension plan for the applicable period caused by the wrongful discharge.</p>
          <p>(b) A person is wrongfully discharged for purposes of this section if:</p>
          <p>(1) the person has been determined by a court of competent jurisdiction, by an arbitrator in binding arbitration, by the commissioner of veterans affairs, or by a board, commission, or panel acting under section <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/statutes/cite/197.46">197.46</a>, whichever applies, to have been wrongfully discharged from public employment;</p>
          <p>(2) the person received an award of back pay with respect to that discharge; and</p>
          <p>(3) the award does not include any amount for any lost or interrupted public pension plan coverage.</p>
        </div>
        <div class="subd" id="stat.356.50.2">
          <a title="Link to Subd. 2." class="permalink" href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/statutes/2024/cite/356/full#stat.356.50.2">§</a>
          <h2 class="subd_no">Subd. 2.<span class="headnote">Service credit procedure.</span></h2>
          <p> (a) To obtain the public pension plan allowable service credit, the eligible person under subdivision 1 shall pay the required member contribution amount. The required member contribution amount is the member contribution rate or rates in effect for the pension plan during the period of service covered by the back pay award, applied to the unpaid gross salary amounts of the back pay award including unemployment insurance, workers' compensation, or wages from other sources which reduced the back award. No contributions may be made under this clause for compensation covered by a public pension plan listed in section <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/statutes/cite/356.30#stat.356.30.3">356.30, subdivision 3</a>, for employment during the removal period. The person shall pay the required member contribution amount within 60 days of the date of receipt of the back pay award or within 60 days of a billing from the retirement fund, whichever is later.</p>
          <p>(b) The public employer who wrongfully discharged the public employee must pay an employer contribution on the back pay award. The employer contribution must be based on the employer contribution rate or rates in effect for the pension plan during the period of service covered by the back pay award, applied to the salary amount on which the member contribution amount was determined under paragraph (a). The employer must pay compound interest on both the required member and employer contribution amounts at the applicable monthly rate or rates specified in section <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/statutes/cite/356.59">356.59</a>, subdivision 2, 3, 4, or 5, whichever applies, between the date the contribution amount would have been paid to the date of actual payment. The employer payment must be made within 30 days of the payment under paragraph (a).</p>
        </div>
        <div class="subd" id="stat.356.50.3">
          <a title="Link to Subd. 3." class="permalink" href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/statutes/2024/cite/356/full#stat.356.50.3">§</a>
          <h2 class="subd_no">Subd. 3.<span class="headnote">Employer reporting.</span></h2>
          <p>The employer must report to the executive director of the applicable pension plan that a person has been determined to be wrongfully discharged and the employer must provide a copy of the written order or decision.</p>
        </div>
        <div class="subd" id="stat.356.50.4">
          <a title="Link to Subd. 4." class="permalink" href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/statutes/2024/cite/356/full#stat.356.50.4">§</a>
          <h2 class="subd_no">Subd. 4.<span class="headnote">Annuity repayment.</span></h2>
          <p>Notwithstanding subdivisions 1 and 2, if after being discharged, the person commences receipt of an annuity from the applicable plan, and it is later determined that the person was wrongfully discharged, the person shall repay the annuity received in a lump sum within 60 days of receipt of the back pay award.</p>
        </div>
        <div class="subd" id="stat.356.50.5">
          <a title="Link to Subd. 5." class="permalink" href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/statutes/2024/cite/356/full#stat.356.50.5">§</a>
          <h2 class="subd_no">Subd. 5.<span class="headnote">Disability treatment.</span></h2>
          <p>If a person is wrongfully discharged and before reinstatement takes a refund of employee contributions under the applicable plan's refund provision and fails to repay that refund, then not withstanding other law to the contrary, if the person applies for a disability benefit and is approved for that benefit, the disability benefit amount must be computed solely on the years of covered service provided after reinstatement, on the individual's salary for benefit computation purposes, and on the applicable plan accrual rates, rather than receiving a minimum disability benefit amount, if applicable, specified in plan law.</p>
        </div>
      </div>
      <div class="history" id="stat.356.50.history
			">
        <h2>History: </h2>
        <p class="first"><a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/laws/1992/0/443/#laws.0.1.0">1992 c 443 s 1</a>; <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/laws/1994/0/488/#laws.0.8.0">1994 c 488 s 8</a>; <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/laws/2002/0/392/#laws.11.39.0">2002 c 392 art 11 s 39</a>; <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/laws/2004/0/206/#laws.0.52.0">2004 c 206 s 52</a>; <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/laws/2006/0/271/#laws.3.41.0">2006 c 271 art 3 s 41</a>; <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/laws/2010/0/359/#laws.2.16.0">2010 c 359 art 2 s 16</a>; <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/laws/2015/0/68/#laws.3.14.0">2015 c 68 art 3 s 14</a>; <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/laws/2018/0/211/#laws.6.37.0">2018 c 211 art 6 s 37</a></p>
      </div>
      <div class="sr" id="stat.356.55"><b>356.55</b> MS 2002 [Repealed, <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/laws/1998/0/390/#laws.4.1.0">1998 c 390 art 4 s 1</a>; <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/laws/2001/1/10/#laws.6.16.0">1Sp2001 c 10 art 6 s 16</a>; 2002 c 392 art 11 s 40; <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/laws/2003/1/12/#laws.6.1.0">1Sp2003 c 12 art 6 s 1</a>]</div>
      <div class="section" id="stat.356.551">
        <h1 class="shn">356.551 POST-JULY 1, 2004, PRIOR SERVICE CREDIT PURCHASE PAYMENT AMOUNT DETERMINATION PROCEDURE.</h1>
        <div class="subd" id="stat.356.551.1">
          <a title="Link to Subdivision 1." class="permalink" href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/statutes/2024/cite/356/full#stat.356.551.1">§</a>
          <h2 class="subd_no">Subdivision 1.<span class="headnote">Application.</span></h2>
          <p>(a) Unless the prior service credit purchase authorization special law or general statute provision explicitly specifies a different purchase payment amount determination procedure, this section governs the determination of the prior service credit purchase payment amount of any prior service credit purchase.</p>
          <p>(b) The purchase payment amount determination procedure must recognize any service credit accrued to the purchaser in a pension plan enumerated in section <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/statutes/cite/356.30#stat.356.30.3">356.30, subdivision 3</a>.</p>
          <p>(c) Any service credit in a Minnesota defined benefit public employee pension plan available to be reinstated by the purchaser through the repayment of a refund of member or employee contributions previously received must be repaid in full before any purchase of prior service credit payment is made under this section.</p>
        </div>
        <div class="subd" id="stat.356.551.2">
          <a title="Link to Subd. 2." class="permalink" href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/statutes/2024/cite/356/full#stat.356.551.2">§</a>
          <h2 class="subd_no">Subd. 2.<span class="headnote">Determination.</span></h2>
          <p class="in">(a) Unless the minimum purchase amount set forth in paragraph (c) applies, the prior service credit purchase amount is an amount equal to the actuarial present value, on the date of payment, as calculated by the chief administrative officer of the pension plan and reviewed by the actuary retained under section <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/statutes/cite/356.214">356.214</a>, of the amount of the additional retirement annuity obtained by the acquisition of the additional service credit in this section.</p>
          <p class="in">(b) Calculation of this amount must be made using the investment return assumption applicable to the public pension plan specified in section <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/statutes/cite/356.215#stat.356.215.8">356.215, subdivision 8</a>, and the mortality table adopted for the public pension plan.</p>
          <p class="in">(1) Unless clause (2) applies, the calculation must assume continuous future service in the public pension plan until, and retirement at, the age at which the minimum requirements of the fund for normal retirement or retirement with an annuity unreduced for retirement at an early age, including section <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/statutes/cite/356.30">356.30</a>, are met with the additional service credit purchased. The calculation must also assume a full-time equivalent salary, or actual salary, whichever is greater, and a future salary history that includes annual salary increases at the applicable salary increase rate for the plan specified in section <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/statutes/cite/356.215#stat.356.215.8">356.215, subdivision 8</a>.</p>
          <p class="in">(2) This clause applies when the calculation is being done for purposes of section <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/statutes/cite/352.272">352.272</a>; <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/statutes/cite/352B.087">352B.087</a>; <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/statutes/cite/353.0141#stat.353.0141.3">353.0141, subdivision 3</a>; <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/statutes/cite/354.544">354.544</a>; <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/statutes/cite/354A.0961">354A.0961</a>; or <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/statutes/cite/490.1211#stat.490.1211.2">490.1211, subdivision 2</a>. The calculation must include continuous future service in the public pension plan until, and retirement at, any age at or after which the minimum requirements of the fund for early retirement or retirement with an annuity unreduced for retirement at an early age, including section <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/statutes/cite/356.30">356.30</a>, are met with the additional service credit purchased. The calculation must be determined using the retirement age that provides the most valuable benefit to the member. The calculation must also assume a full-time equivalent salary, or actual salary, whichever is greater, and a future salary history that includes annual salary increases at the applicable salary increase rate for the plan specified in section <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/statutes/cite/356.215#stat.356.215.8">356.215, subdivision 8</a>.</p>
          <p class="in">(c) The prior service credit purchase amount may not be less than the amount determined by applying, for each year or fraction of a year being purchased, the sum of the employee contribution rate, the employer contribution rate, and the additional employer contribution rate, if any, applicable during that period, to the person's annual salary during that period, or fractional portion of a year's salary, if applicable, plus interest at the applicable annual rate or rates specified in section <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/statutes/cite/356.59">356.59</a>, subdivision 2, 3, 4, or 5, whichever applies, compounded annually, from the end of the year in which contributions would otherwise have been made to the date on which the payment is received.</p>
          <p class="in">(d) Unless otherwise provided by statutes governing a specific plan, payment must be made in one lump sum within one year of the prior service credit authorization or prior to the member's effective date of retirement, whichever is earlier. Payment of the amount calculated under this section must be made by the applicable eligible person.</p>
          <p class="in">(e) However, the current employer or the prior employer may, at its discretion, pay all or any portion of the payment amount that exceeds an amount equal to the employee contribution rates in effect during the period or periods of prior service applied to the actual salary rates in effect during the period or periods of prior service, plus interest at the applicable annual rate or rates specified in section <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/statutes/cite/356.59">356.59</a>, subdivision 2, 3, 4, or 5, whichever applies, compounded annually, from the date on which the contributions would otherwise have been made to the date on which the payment is made. If the employer agrees to payments under this subdivision, the purchaser must make the employee payments required under this subdivision within 90 days of the prior service credit authorization. If that employee payment is made, the employer payment under this subdivision must be remitted to the chief administrative officer of the public pension plan within 60 days of receipt by the chief administrative officer of the employee payments specified under this subdivision.</p>
        </div>
        <div class="subd" id="stat.356.551.3">
          <a title="Link to Subd. 3." class="permalink" href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/statutes/2024/cite/356/full#stat.356.551.3">§</a>
          <h2 class="subd_no">Subd. 3.<span class="headnote">Documentation.</span></h2>
          <p>The prospective prior service credit purchaser must provide any relevant documentation required by the chief administrative officer of the applicable public pension plan to determine eligibility for the prior service credit under this section.</p>
        </div>
        <div class="subd" id="stat.356.551.4">
          <a title="Link to Subd. 4." class="permalink" href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/statutes/2024/cite/356/full#stat.356.551.4">§</a>
          <h2 class="subd_no">Subd. 4.<span class="headnote">Payment precondition for credit grant.</span></h2>
          <p>Service credit for the purchase period must be granted by the public pension plan to the purchaser upon receipt of the full purchase payment amount specified in subdivision 2.</p>
        </div>
      </div>
      <div class="history" id="stat.356.551.history
			">
        <h2>History: </h2>
        <p class="first"><a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/laws/1998/0/390/#laws.4.2.0">1998 c 390 art 4 s 2</a>; <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/laws/2002/0/392/#laws.11.41.0">2002 c 392 art 11 s 41</a>; <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/laws/2005/1/8/#laws.10.65.0">1Sp2005 c 8 art 10 s 65</a>; <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/laws/2008/0/349/#laws.5.32.0">2008 c 349 art 5 s 32</a>; <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/laws/2015/0/68/#laws.3.15.0">2015 c 68 art 3 s 15</a>; <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/laws/2018/0/211/#laws.6.38.0">2018 c 211 art 6 s 38</a>; <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/laws/2019/1/8/#laws.6.2.0">1Sp2019 c 8 art 6 s 2</a>; <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/laws/2022/0/65/#laws.1.6.0">2022 c 65 art 1 s 6</a>; art 8 s 3; <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/laws/2023/0/47/#laws.1.2.0">2023 c 47 art 1 s 2</a></p>
      </div>
      <div class="sr" id="stat.356.555"><b>356.555</b> MS 2002 [Expired]</div>
      <div class="sr" id="stat.356.58"><b>356.58</b> [Repealed, <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/laws/2003/0/2/#laws.1.45.0">2003 c 2 art 1 s 45</a>]</div>
      <div class="section" id="stat.356.59">
        <h1 class="shn">356.59 INTEREST RATES.</h1>
        <div class="subd" id="stat.356.59.1">
          <a title="Link to Subdivision 1." class="permalink" href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/statutes/2024/cite/356/full#stat.356.59.1">§</a>
          <h2 class="subd_no">Subdivision 1.<span class="headnote">Applicable interest rates.</span></h2>
          <p>Whenever the payment of interest is required with respect to any payment, including refunds, remittances, shortages, contributions, or repayments, the rate of interest is the rate or rates specified in subdivisions 2 to 5 for each public retirement plan.</p>
        </div>
        <div class="subd" id="stat.356.59.2">
          <a title="Link to Subd. 2." class="permalink" href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/statutes/2024/cite/356/full#stat.356.59.2">§</a>

        <div class="subd" id="stat.356.59.4">
          <a title="Link to Subd. 4." class="permalink" href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/statutes/2024/cite/356/full#stat.356.59.4">§</a>
          <h2 class="subd_no">Subd. 4.<span class="headnote">Teachers Retirement Association.</span></h2>
          <p>The interest rates for the retirement plan administered by the Teachers Retirement Association are as follows:</p>
          <table>
