      <div class="history" id="stat.356.59.history
			">
        <h2>History: </h2>
        <p class="first"><a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/laws/2018/0/211/#laws.6.39.0">2018 c 211 art 6 s 39</a>; <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/laws/2023/0/45/#laws.1.2.0">2023 c 45 art 1 s 2</a></p>
      </div>
      <div class="sr" id="stat.356.60"><b>356.60</b> [Repealed, <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/laws/1982/0/578/#laws.1.19.0">1982 c 578 art 1 s 19</a>]</div>
      <div class="sr" id="stat.356.61"><b>356.61</b> [Repealed, <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/laws/2000/0/461/#laws.14.1.0">2000 c 461 art 14 s 1</a>]</div>
      <h1 class="fgh">INTERNAL REVENUE CODE COMPLIANCE</h1>
      <div class="section" id="stat.356.611">
        <h1 class="shn">356.611 LIMITATION ON PUBLIC EMPLOYEE SALARIES FOR PENSION PURPOSES.</h1>
        <div class="subd" id="stat.356.611.1">
          <a title="Link to Subdivision 1." class="permalink" href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/statutes/2024/cite/356/full#stat.356.611.1">§</a>
          <h2 class="subd_no">Subdivision 1.</h2>
          <p>[Repealed, <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/laws/2005/0/169/#laws.0.2.0">2005 c 169 s 2</a>]</p>
        </div>
        <div class="subd" id="stat.356.611.2">
          <a title="Link to Subd. 2." class="permalink" href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/statutes/2024/cite/356/full#stat.356.611.2">§</a>
          <h2 class="subd_no">Subd. 2.<span class="headnote">Federal compensation limits.</span></h2>
          <p class="in">(a) For members or participants of a covered retirement plan listed in subdivision 6, compensation in excess of the limitation specified in section 401(a)(17) of the Internal Revenue Code, as adjusted, for changes in the cost of living under section 401(a)(17)(B) of the Internal Revenue Code, must not be included for contribution and benefit computation purposes.</p>
          <p class="in">(b) Notwithstanding paragraph (a), for members or participants specified in paragraph (a) who first contributed to a plan specified in that paragraph before July 1, 1995, the annual compensation limit specified in section 401(a)(17) of the Internal Revenue Code on June 30, 1993, applies if that provides a greater allowable annual compensation.</p>
          <p>(c) To the extent required by sections 3401(h) and 414(u)(12) of the Internal Revenue Code, an individual receiving a differential wage payment as defined in section 3401(h)(2) of the Internal Revenue Code from an employer shall be treated as employed by that employer, and the differential wage payment will be treated as compensation for purposes of applying the limits on annual additions under section 415(c) of the Internal Revenue Code.</p>
        </div>
        <div class="subd" id="stat.356.611.3">
          <a title="Link to Subd. 3." class="permalink" href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/statutes/2024/cite/356/full#stat.356.611.3">§</a>
          <h2 class="subd_no">Subd. 3.</h2>
          <p>MS 2016 [Repealed, <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/laws/2018/0/211/#laws.13.21.0">2018 c 211 art 13 s 21</a>]</p>
        </div>
        <div class="subd" id="stat.356.611.3a">
          <a title="Link to Subd. 3a." class="permalink" href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/statutes/2024/cite/356/full#stat.356.611.3a">§</a>
          <h2 class="subd_no">Subd. 3a.</h2>
          <p>MS 2016 [Repealed, <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/laws/2018/0/211/#laws.13.21.0">2018 c 211 art 13 s 21</a>]</p>
        </div>
        <div class="subd" id="stat.356.611.4">
          <a title="Link to Subd. 4." class="permalink" href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/statutes/2024/cite/356/full#stat.356.611.4">§</a>
          <h2 class="subd_no">Subd. 4.</h2>
          <p>MS 2016 [Repealed, <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/laws/2018/0/211/#laws.13.21.0">2018 c 211 art 13 s 21</a>]</p>
        </div>
        <div class="subd" id="stat.356.611.5">
          <a title="Link to Subd. 5." class="permalink" href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/statutes/2024/cite/356/full#stat.356.611.5">§</a>
          <h2 class="subd_no">Subd. 5.</h2>
          <p>MS 2016 [Repealed, <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/laws/2018/0/211/#laws.13.21.0">2018 c 211 art 13 s 21</a>]</p>
        </div>
        <div class="subd" id="stat.356.611.6">
          <a title="Link to Subd. 6." class="permalink" href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/statutes/2024/cite/356/full#stat.356.611.6">§</a>
          <h2 class="subd_no">Subd. 6.<span class="headnote">Covered retirement plan.</span></h2>
          <p>As used in this section, "covered retirement plan" means any of the following plans:</p>
          <p>(1) the legislator's retirement plan, established by chapter 3A, including constitutional officers as specified in that chapter;</p>
          <p>(2) the general state employees retirement plan of the Minnesota State Retirement System, established by chapter 352;</p>
          <p>(3) the correctional state employees retirement plan of the Minnesota State Retirement System, established by chapter 352;</p>
          <p>(4) the State Patrol retirement plan, established by chapter 352B;</p>
          <p>(5) the unclassified state employees retirement plan, established by chapter 352D;</p>
          <p>(6) the general employees retirement plan of the Public Employees Retirement Association, established by chapter 353;</p>
          <p>(7) the public employees police and fire retirement plan of the Public Employees Retirement Association, established by chapter 353;</p>
          <p>(8) the public employees defined contribution plan, established by chapter 353D;</p>
          <p>(9) the local government correctional service retirement plan of the Public Employees Retirement Association, established by chapter 353E;</p>
          <p>(10) the statewide volunteer firefighter retirement plan, established by chapter 353G;</p>
          <p>(11) the Teachers Retirement Association, established by chapter 354;</p>
          <p>(12) the St. Paul Teachers Retirement Fund Association, established by chapter 354A;</p>
          <p>(13) the higher education individual retirement account plan, established by chapter 354B;</p>
          <p>(14) the higher education supplemental retirement plan, established by chapter 354C;</p>
          <p>(15) a retirement plan of a volunteer firefighter retirement association subject to chapter 424A;</p>
          <p>(16) the judges retirement plan, established by chapter 490; or</p>
          <p>(17) the Bloomington Fire Department Relief Association governed by Laws 2013, chapter 111, article 5, sections 31 to 42; Minnesota Statutes 2000, chapter 424; and Laws 1965, chapter 446, as amended.</p>
        </div>
      </div>
      <div class="history" id="stat.356.611.history
			">
        <h2>History: </h2>
        <p class="first"><a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/laws/1994/0/528/#laws.4.11.0">1994 c 528 art 4 s 11</a>; <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/laws/1995/0/262/#laws.1.15.0">1995 c 262 art 1 s 15</a>; <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/laws/2002/0/392/#laws.11.43.0">2002 c 392 art 11 s 43</a>; <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/laws/2004/0/267/#laws.2.6.0">2004 c 267 art 2 s 6</a>-8; art 10 s 1; <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/laws/2005/1/8/#laws.1.23.0">1Sp2005 c 8 art 1 s 23</a>; <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/laws/2007/0/13/#laws.2.17.0">2007 c 13 art 2 s 17</a>; <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/laws/2008/0/349/#laws.5.33.0">2008 c 349 art 5 s 33</a>,34,37; <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/laws/2009/0/169/#laws.4.43.0">2009 c 169 art 4 s 43</a>,44; <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/laws/2012/0/286/#laws.9.1.0">2012 c 286 art 9 s 1</a>-5; <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/laws/2024/0/102/#laws.8.2.0">2024 c 102 art 8 s 2</a>,3</p>
      </div>
      <div class="section" id="stat.356.612">
        <h1 class="shn">356.612 LIMITATION ON BENEFITS AND CONTRIBUTIONS.</h1>
        <div class="subd" id="stat.356.612.1">
          <a title="Link to Subdivision 1." class="permalink" href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/statutes/2024/cite/356/full#stat.356.612.1">§</a>
          <h2 class="subd_no">Subdivision 1.<span class="headnote">Definitions.</span></h2>
          <p>(a) For purposes of this section, the following terms have the meanings given.</p>
          <p>(b) "Annual addition" means the sum for the limitation year of all pretax and after-tax contributions made by the member or the member's employer and credited to an account in the name of the member in any defined contribution plan maintained by the employer.</p>
          <p>(c) "Annuity starting date" means the first day of the first period for which an amount is payable as an annuity or, in the case of a benefit not payable in the form of an annuity, the first day on which all events have occurred which entitle the member to the benefit.</p>
          <p>(d) "Compensation" means the compensation actually paid or made available to a member or participant for any limitation year, including all items of remuneration described in Code of Federal Regulations, title 26, section <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/statutes/cite/1.415">1.415 </a>(c)-2(b), and excluding all items of remuneration described in Code of Federal Regulations, title 26, section <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/statutes/cite/1.415">1.415 </a>(c)-2(c). Compensation for pension plan purposes for any limitation year shall not exceed the applicable federal compensation limit described in section <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/statutes/cite/356.611#stat.356.611.2">356.611, subdivision 2</a>.</p>
          <p>(e) "Limitation year" means the calendar year or fiscal year, whichever is applicable to the particular pension plan.</p>
          <p>(f) "Maximum permissible benefit" means an annual benefit of $160,000, automatically adjusted under section 415(d) of the Internal Revenue Code for each limitation year ending after December 31, 2001, payable in the form of a single life annuity. The new limitation shall apply to limitation years ending with or within the calendar year of the date of the adjustment, but a member's benefits shall not reflect the adjusted limit prior to January 1 of that calendar year. The maximum permissible benefit amount shall be further adjusted as follows:</p>
          <p>(1) if the member has less than ten years of participation, the maximum permissible benefit shall be multiplied by a fraction, the numerator of which is the number of years, or part thereof, but not less than one year, of participation in the plan, and the denominator of which is ten;</p>
          <p>(2) for a member who is not a qualified participant, if the annual benefit begins before the member has attained 62 years of age, the determination as to whether the maximum permissible benefit limit has been satisfied shall be made, in accordance with regulations prescribed by the United States secretary of the treasury, by reducing the limit so that the limit, as so reduced, equals an annual benefit, beginning when the annual benefit actually begins, which is equivalent to a $160,000, as adjusted, annual benefit beginning at 62 years of age; and</p>
          <p>(3) if the annual benefit begins after the member has attained 65 years of age, the determination as to whether the maximum permissible benefit limit has been satisfied shall be made, in accordance with regulations prescribed by the United States secretary of the treasury, by increasing the limit so that the limit, as so increased, equals an annual benefit, beginning when the annual benefit actually begins, which is equivalent to a $160,000, as adjusted, annual benefit beginning at 65 years of age.</p>
          <p>(g) "Qualified participant" means a member of a defined benefit plan listed in section <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/statutes/cite/356.611#stat.356.611.6">356.611, subdivision 6</a>, with respect to whom the period of service taken into account in determining the amount of the benefit under such defined benefit plan includes at least 15 years of service of the member:</p>
          <p>(1) as a full-time employee of any police department or fire department which is organized and operated by the state, Indian Tribal government, or any political subdivision maintaining such defined benefit plan to provide police protection, firefighting services, or medical services for any area within the jurisdiction of the state, Indian Tribal government, or political subdivision; or</p>
          <p>(2) as a member of the Armed Forces of the United States.</p>
        </div>
        <div class="subd" id="stat.356.612.2">
          <a title="Link to Subd. 2." class="permalink" href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/statutes/2024/cite/356/full#stat.356.612.2">§</a>
          <h2 class="subd_no">Subd. 2.<span class="headnote">Annual benefit limitations; defined benefit plans.</span></h2>
          <p>(a) For a defined benefit plan listed in section <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/statutes/cite/356.611#stat.356.611.6">356.611, subdivision 6</a>, the annual benefit payable to a member shall not exceed the maximum permissible benefit. If the benefit the member would otherwise receive for a limitation year would result in the payment of an annual benefit in excess of the maximum permissible benefit, the benefit shall be reduced to the extent necessary so the benefit does not exceed the maximum permissible benefit.</p>
          <p>(b) For purposes of applying the limitation in paragraph (a), an annual benefit that is payable in any form other than a single life annuity shall be adjusted to an actuarially equivalent single life annuity that equals, if the annuity starting date is in a plan year beginning after 2005, the annual amount of the single life annuity commencing at the same annuity starting date that has the same actuarial present value as the member's form of benefit, using whichever of the following produces the greatest annual amount:</p>
          <p>(1) the interest rate and the mortality table or other tabular factor specified in the plan for adjusting benefits in the same form;</p>
          <p>(2) a 5.5 percent interest rate assumption and the applicable mortality table; or</p>
          <p>(3) the applicable interest rate under section 417(e)(3) of the Internal Revenue Code and the applicable mortality table, divided by <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/statutes/cite/1.05">1.05</a>.</p>
          <p>(c) If a member participated in more than one pension plan in which the employer participates, the benefits under each plan must be reduced proportionately to satisfy the limitation in paragraph (a).</p>
        </div>
        <div class="subd" id="stat.356.612.3">
          <a title="Link to Subd. 3." class="permalink" href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/statutes/2024/cite/356/full#stat.356.612.3">§</a>
          <h2 class="subd_no">Subd. 3.<span class="headnote">Annual addition limitation; defined contribution plans.</span></h2>
          <p>For any limitation year, the annual additions by or on behalf of a member to a defined contribution plan listed in section <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/statutes/cite/356.611#stat.356.611.6">356.611, subdivision 6</a>, shall not exceed the lesser of:</p>
          <p>(1) 100 percent of the member's compensation for the limitation year; or</p>
          <p>(2) the dollar limit in effect for the limitation year under section 415(c)(1)(A) of the Internal Revenue Code, as adjusted by the United States secretary of the treasury under section 415(d)(1)(C) of the Internal Revenue Code.</p>
        </div>
        <div class="subd" id="stat.356.612.4">
          <a title="Link to Subd. 4." class="permalink" href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/statutes/2024/cite/356/full#stat.356.612.4">§</a>
          <h2 class="subd_no">Subd. 4.<span class="headnote">Incorporation by reference.</span></h2>
          <p>Any requirements of sections 415(b) and (c) of the Internal Revenue Code and related regulations and agency guidance not addressed by this section shall be considered incorporated by reference, including provisions applicable to a qualified participant and to survivor and disability benefits. This section shall be interpreted in a manner that is consistent with the requirements of sections 415(b) and (c) of the Internal Revenue Code and the related regulations.</p>
        </div>
      </div>
      <div class="history" id="stat.356.612.history
			">
        <h2>History: </h2>
        <p class="first">
          <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/laws/2024/0/102/#laws.8.4.0">2024 c 102 art 8 s 4</a>
        </p>
      </div>
      <div class="section" id="stat.356.614">
        <h1 class="shn">356.614 LIMITATION ON USE OF FORFEITURES.</h1>
        <p>This section applies to any defined benefit plan listed in section <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/statutes/cite/356.611">356.611, subdivision </a>6. Unless otherwise permitted by section 401(a)(8) of the Internal Revenue Code, forfeitures must not be applied to increase the benefits any participant would otherwise receive under the plan at any time prior to the termination of the plan or the complete discontinuance of employer contributions.</p>
      </div>
      <div class="history" id="stat.356.614.history
			">
        <h2>History: </h2>
        <p class="first">
          <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/laws/2024/0/102/#laws.8.5.0">2024 c 102 art 8 s 5</a>
        </p>
      </div>
      <div class="sr" id="stat.356.615"><b>356.615</b> [Repealed, <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/laws/2002/0/392/#laws.11.53.0">2002 c 392 art 11 s 53</a>]</div>
      <div class="section" id="stat.356.62">
        <h1 class="shn">356.62 PAYMENT OF EMPLOYEE CONTRIBUTION.</h1>
        <div class="subd" id="stat.356.62.1">
          <a title="Link to Subdivision 1." class="permalink" href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/statutes/2024/cite/356/full#stat.356.62.1">§</a>
          <h2 class="subd_no">Subdivision 1.<span class="headnote">Definitions.</span></h2>
          <p>(a) For purposes of this section, the following terms have the meanings given.</p>
          <p>(b) "Employee" means any person covered by a public pension or retirement plan.</p>
          <p>(c) "Employee contributions" means any sums deducted from the employee's salary or wages or otherwise paid in lieu thereof, regardless of whether they are denominated contributions by the public pension or retirement plan.</p>
          <p>(d) "Public pension or retirement plan" means a covered retirement plan listed in section <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/statutes/cite/356.611#stat.356.611.6">356.611, subdivision 6</a>, or any other public retirement plan to which section 414(h)(2) of the Internal Revenue Code applies.</p>
        </div>
        <div class="subd" id="stat.356.62.2">
          <a title="Link to Subd. 2." class="permalink" href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/statutes/2024/cite/356/full#stat.356.62.2">§</a>
          <h2 class="subd_no">Subd. 2.<span class="headnote">Pick up of employee contributions.</span></h2>
          <p>(a) For purposes of any public pension or retirement plan, each employer shall pick up the employee contributions required under law or under the pension plan document for all salaries. The employer shall pick up these employee contributions by a reduction in the cash salary of the employee.</p>
          <p>(b) Employee contributions that are picked up must be treated for all purposes of the public pension or retirement plan in the same manner and to the same extent as employee contributions that were made before the date on which the employee contributions pick up began. The amount of the employee contributions that are picked up must be included in the salary upon which retirement coverage is credited and upon which retirement and survivor's benefits are determined.</p>
          <p>(c) The employing unit shall supply each employee and the commissioner of revenue with an information return indicating the amount of the employer's picked-up contributions for the calendar year that were not subject to withholding. This return must be provided to the employee not later than January 31 of the succeeding calendar year. The commissioner of revenue shall prescribe the form of the return and the provisions of section <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/statutes/cite/289A.12">289A.12</a> must apply to the extent not inconsistent with the provisions of this section.</p>
        </div>
      </div>
      <div class="history" id="stat.356.62.history
			">
        <h2>History: </h2>
        <p class="first"><a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/laws/1982/3/1/#laws.2.7.0">3Sp1982 c 1 art 2 s 7</a>; <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/laws/1983/0/148/#laws.0.6.0">1983 c 148 s 6</a>; <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/laws/1983/0/216/#laws.1.85.0">1983 c 216 art 1 s 85</a>; <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/laws/1990/0/480/#laws.1.46.0">1990 c 480 art 1 s 46</a>; <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/laws/1993/0/375/#laws.8.14.0">1993 c 375 art 8 s 14</a>; <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/laws/2001/0/7/#laws.0.66.0">2001 c 7 s 66</a>; <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/laws/2002/0/379/#laws.1.78.0">2002 c 379 art 1 s 78</a>; <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/laws/2002/0/392/#laws.11.44.0">2002 c 392 art 11 s 44</a>; <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/laws/2003/0/2/#laws.1.42.0">2003 c 2 art 1 s 42</a>; <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/laws/2015/0/68/#laws.13.55.0">2015 c 68 art 13 s 55</a>; <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/laws/2024/0/102/#laws.8.6.0">2024 c 102 art 8 s 6</a></p>
      </div>
      <div class="section" id="stat.356.63">
        <h1 class="shn">356.63 LIMITATION ON USE OF PUBLIC PENSION PLAN ASSETS.</h1>
        <p>(a) Money held by or credited to a public pension plan as assets, including employer and employee contributions, state aid, appropriations from the state or a governmental subdivision, and accrued earnings on investments, constitutes a dedicated fund. The dedicated fund may be used exclusively to pay retirement annuities, service pensions, disability benefits, survivor benefits, refunds of contributions, or other benefits provided under the benefit plan document or documents governing the public pension plan, and to pay reasonable administrative expenses approved by the governing board of the public pension plan or by another appropriate authority. No assets of a public pension plan may be loaned or transferred to the state or a governmental subdivision or be used to amortize an unfunded actuarial accrued liability in another public pension plan or fund, whether or not the plan providing the assets consolidates or has consolidated with the plan receiving the assets. Nothing in this section prohibits a public pension plan or the State Board of Investment from investing the assets of a plan as authorized by law, including the investment of the assets of public pension plans by the State Board of Investment in a commingled investment fund.</p>
        <p>(b) A public pension plan for purposes of this section means a pension plan or fund specified in section <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/statutes/cite/356.20#stat.356.20.2">356.20, subdivision 2</a>, or <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/statutes/cite/356.30#stat.356.30.3">356.30, subdivision 3</a>, or a retirement or pension plan or fund, including a supplemental retirement plan or fund, established, maintained, or supported by a governmental subdivision or public body whose revenues are derived from taxation, fees, assessments, or other public sources.</p>
      </div>
      <div class="history" id="stat.356.63.history
			">
        <h2>History: </h2>
        <p class="first">
          <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/laws/2002/0/392/#laws.11.45.0">2002 c 392 art 11 s 45</a>
        </p>
      </div>
      <div class="sr" id="stat.356.631"><b>356.631</b> [Renumbered <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/statutes/cite/356.648">356.648</a>]</div>
      <div class="section" id="stat.356.633">
        <h1 class="shn">356.633 DIRECT ROLLOVERS.</h1>
        <div class="subd" id="stat.356.633.1">
          <a title="Link to Subdivision 1." class="permalink" href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/statutes/2024/cite/356/full#stat.356.633.1">§</a>
          <h2 class="subd_no">Subdivision 1.<span class="headnote">Definitions.</span></h2>
          <p>(a) For purposes of this section, the following terms have the meanings given.</p>
          <p>(b) "Distributee" means:</p>
          <p>(1) a participant in a covered retirement plan listed in section <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/statutes/cite/356.611#stat.356.611.6">356.611, subdivision 6</a>;</p>
          <p>(2) the surviving spouse of a participant;</p>
          <p>(3) the former spouse of the participant who is the alternate payee under a qualified domestic relations order as defined in section 414(p) of the Internal Revenue Code, or who is a recipient of a court-ordered equitable distribution of marital property, as provided in section <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/statutes/cite/518.58">518.58</a>; or</p>
          <p>(4) a nonspousal beneficiary of a participant who qualifies for a distribution under the plan and is a designated beneficiary as defined in section 401(a)(9)(E) of the Internal Revenue Code.</p>
          <p>(c) "Eligible retirement plan" means:</p>
          <p>(1) an individual retirement account under section 408(a) or 408A of the Internal Revenue Code;</p>
          <p>(2) an individual retirement annuity plan under section 408(b) of the Internal Revenue Code;</p>
          <p>(3) an annuity plan under section 403(a) of the Internal Revenue Code;</p>
          <p>(4) a qualified trust plan under section 401(a) of the Internal Revenue Code that accepts the distributee's eligible rollover distribution;</p>
          <p>(5) an annuity contract under section 403(b) of the Internal Revenue Code;</p>
          <p>(6) an eligible deferred compensation plan under section 457(b) of the Internal Revenue Code, which is maintained by a state or local government and which agrees to separately account for the amounts transferred into the plan;</p>
          <p>(7) in the case of an eligible rollover distribution to a nonspousal beneficiary, an individual account or annuity treated as an inherited individual retirement account under section 402(c)(11) of the Internal Revenue Code; or</p>
          <p>(8) a savings incentive match plan for employees of small employers (SIMPLE) individual retirement account under section 408(p) of the Internal Revenue Code, provided that the rollover distribution is made after the two-year period beginning on the date the distributee first participated in any qualified salary reduction arrangement maintained by the distributee's employer under section 408(p)(2) of the Internal Revenue Code, as described in section 72(t)(6) of the Internal Revenue Code.</p>
          <p>(d) "Eligible rollover distribution" means any distribution of all or any portion of the balance to the credit of the distributee. An eligible rollover distribution does not include:</p>
          <p>(1) a distribution that is one of a series of substantially equal periodic payments, receivable annually or more frequently, that is made for the life or life expectancy of the distributee, the joint lives or joint life expectancies of the distributee and the distributee's designated beneficiary, or for a specified period of ten years or more;</p>
          <p>(2) a distribution that is required under section 401(a)(9) of the Internal Revenue Code; or</p>
          <p>(3) any other exception required by law or the Internal Revenue Code.</p>
        </div>
        <div class="subd" id="stat.356.633.2">
          <a title="Link to Subd. 2." class="permalink" href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/statutes/2024/cite/356/full#stat.356.633.2">§</a>
          <h2 class="subd_no">Subd. 2.<span class="headnote">Right to elect direct rollover.</span></h2>
          <p>Except as provided in subdivision 3 for after-tax contributions, a distributee may elect, at the time and in the manner prescribed by the plan administrator, to have all or any portion of an eligible rollover distribution paid directly to an eligible retirement plan as specified by the distributee.</p>
        </div>
        <div class="subd" id="stat.356.633.3">
          <a title="Link to Subd. 3." class="permalink" href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/statutes/2024/cite/356/full#stat.356.633.3">§</a>
          <h2 class="subd_no">Subd. 3.<span class="headnote">Distributions of after-tax contributions.</span></h2>
          <p>For distributions of after-tax contributions which are not includable in gross income, the after-tax portion may be transferred only to an individual retirement account or annuity described in section 408(a) or (b) of the Internal Revenue Code, to a Roth individual retirement account described in section 408A of the Internal Revenue Code, to a qualified plan described in either section 401(a) of the Internal Revenue Code, or to an annuity contract described in section 403(b) of the Internal Revenue Code, that agrees to separately account for the amounts transferred, including separately accounting for the portion of the distribution which is includable in gross income and the portion of the distribution which is not includable.</p>
        </div>
      </div>
      <div class="history" id="stat.356.633.history
			">
        <h2>History: </h2>
        <p class="first">
          <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/laws/2024/0/102/#laws.8.7.0">2024 c 102 art 8 s 7</a>
        </p>
      </div>
      <div class="section" id="stat.356.635">
        <h1 class="shn">356.635 INTERNAL REVENUE CODE COMPLIANCE.</h1>
        <div class="subd" id="stat.356.635.1">
          <a title="Link to Subdivision 1." class="permalink" href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/statutes/2024/cite/356/full#stat.356.635.1">§</a>
          <h2 class="subd_no">Subdivision 1.<span class="headnote">Definitions.</span></h2>
          <p>(a) For purposes of this section, the following terms have the meanings given.</p>
          <p>(b) "Beneficiary" means the person designated as the beneficiary under the terms of the applicable covered retirement plan.</p>
          <p>(c) "Covered retirement plan" means a pension or retirement plan listed in section <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/statutes/cite/356.611#stat.356.611.6">356.611, subdivision 6</a>.</p>
          <p>(d) "Designated beneficiary" means an individual beneficiary within the meaning of section 401(a)(9)(E)(i) of the Internal Revenue Code.</p>
          <p>(e) "Distribution calendar year" means a calendar year for which a minimum distribution is required. For distributions beginning before the participant's death, the first distribution calendar year is the calendar year immediately preceding the calendar year which contains the participant's required beginning date. For distributions beginning after the participant's death, the first distribution calendar year is the calendar year in which distributions are required to begin under subdivision 2a, paragraph (b). The required minimum distribution for the participant's first distribution calendar year shall be made on or before the participant's required beginning date.</p>
          <p>(f) "Eligible designated beneficiary" means a designated beneficiary who meets the additional criteria under section 401(a)(9)(E)(ii) of the Internal Revenue Code.</p>
          <p>(g) "Participant's account balance" means the account balance as of the last valuation date in the valuation calendar year increased by the amount of any contributions made and allocated to the account balance as of dates in the valuation calendar year after the valuation date and decreased by distributions made in the valuation calendar year after the valuation date. The account balance for the valuation calendar year includes any amounts rolled over or transferred to the plan either in the valuation calendar year or in the distribution calendar year if distributed or transferred in the valuation calendar year.</p>
          <p>(h) "Required beginning date" means April 1 of the calendar year following the later of:</p>
          <p>(1) the calendar year in which the member or the participant attains the age specified in section 401(a)(9)(C)(i)(I) of the Internal Revenue Code; or</p>
          <p>(2) the calendar year in which the member or participant terminates employment.</p>
          <p>(i) "Valuation calendar year" means the calendar year immediately preceding the distribution calendar year.</p>
        </div>
        <div class="subd" id="stat.356.635.1a">
          <a title="Link to Subd. 1a." class="permalink" href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/statutes/2024/cite/356/full#stat.356.635.1a">§</a>
          <h2 class="subd_no">Subd. 1a.<span class="headnote">Required beginning date.</span></h2>
          <p>(a) Notwithstanding any state law to the contrary, the retirement benefit of a member or participant must begin to be distributed or, if a lump sum, be distributed no later than the member's or participant's required beginning date.</p>
          <p>(b) A pension or retirement plan is not required to obtain the consent of a member or participant to a distribution if the distribution is required to satisfy the requirements of paragraph (a). If the plan is unable to obtain the consent of a member or participant to a distribution that is required to satisfy the requirements of paragraph (a), the plan must make the required distribution to the member or participant. If the plan is a defined benefit plan that permits the distribution to be in the form of an annuity, the required distribution must be:</p>
          <p>(1) in the form of a single life annuity if the plan administrator's records do not indicate that the member is married; or</p>
          <p>(2) in the form of a 50 percent joint and survivor annuity naming the member's spouse as survivor if the plan administrator's records indicate that the member is married.</p>
        </div>
        <div class="subd" id="stat.356.635.2">
          <a title="Link to Subd. 2." class="permalink" href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/statutes/2024/cite/356/full#stat.356.635.2">§</a>
          <h2 class="subd_no">Subd. 2.<span class="headnote">Required minimum distributions.</span></h2>
          <p>Notwithstanding any state law to the contrary:</p>
          <p>(1) distributions from a covered retirement plan must be determined and made in accordance with a reasonable, good faith interpretation of the requirements of section 401(a)(9) of the Internal Revenue Code as applicable to governmental plans, as defined under section 414(d) of the Internal Revenue Code, and the treasury regulations adopted under section 401(a)(9), including, but not limited to, the incidental death benefit provisions of section 401(a)(9)(G) of the Internal Revenue Code; and</p>
          <p>(2) the entire interest of a member or participant under a covered retirement plan must begin to be distributed or, if a lump sum, be distributed no later than the member's or participant's required beginning date.</p>
        </div>
        <div class="subd" id="stat.356.635.2a">
          <a title="Link to Subd. 2a." class="permalink" href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/statutes/2024/cite/356/full#stat.356.635.2a">§</a>
          <h2 class="subd_no">Subd. 2a.<span class="headnote">Required distributions from defined contribution plans.</span></h2>
          <p>(a) This section applies to any covered retirement plan that is a defined contribution plan, including but not limited to the following:</p>
          <p>(1) the unclassified state employees retirement plan, established by chapter 352D;</p>
          <p>(2) the public employees defined contribution plan, established by chapter 353D;</p>
          <p>(3) the defined contribution plan that is part of the statewide volunteer firefighter retirement plan, established by chapter 353G;</p>
          <p>(4) the higher education individuals retirement account plan, established by chapter 354B;</p>
          <p>(5) the higher education supplemental retirement plan, established by chapter 354C; and</p>
          <p>(6) a defined contribution relief association, as defined under section <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/statutes/cite/424A.001">424A.001</a>, subdivision 1c.</p>
          <p>(b) If the participant dies before the required minimum distribution begins, the participant's account must be distributed in a lump sum no later than as follows:</p>
          <p>(1) if the participant's account balance is payable to an eligible designated beneficiary, the distribution must be made by December 31 of the calendar year immediately following the calendar year in which the participant died. If the eligible designated beneficiary is the surviving spouse, the surviving spouse may elect to delay payment until December 31 of the calendar year in which the participant would have attained the participant's required beginning date. Effective for calendar years beginning after December 31, 2023, a surviving spouse who is the member's sole designated beneficiary may elect to be treated as if the surviving spouse were the member as provided under section 401(a)(9)(B)(iv) of the Internal Revenue Code;</p>
          <p>(2) if the participant's account balance is payable to a beneficiary that is not a designated beneficiary, the participant's account must be distributed by December 31 of the calendar year containing the fifth anniversary of the participant's death; or</p>
          <p>(3) if the participant's account balance is payable to a designated beneficiary who is not an eligible designated beneficiary, the participant's account must be distributed by December 31 of the calendar year containing the tenth anniversary of the participant's death.</p>
          <p>(c) Upon the death of the participant after distribution of the participant's account balance begins, any remaining portion of the participant's account balance shall continue to be distributed at least as rapidly as under the method of distribution in effect at the time of the participant's death, provided that the portion of the participant's account balance payable to a designated beneficiary who is not an eligible designated beneficiary must be distributed in its entirety by December 31 of the calendar year containing the tenth anniversary of the participant's death.</p>
          <p>(d) Upon the death of an eligible designated beneficiary, or the attainment of the age of majority of an eligible designated beneficiary who is a minor child of the participant, before distribution of the participant's entire account balance under paragraph (b) or (c), the remainder of the participant's account balance shall be distributed by December 31 of the calendar year containing the tenth anniversary of the eligible designated beneficiary's death, or by December 31 of the calendar year in which the child attains the age of majority plus ten years, as applicable.</p>
          <p>(e) Notwithstanding any other provisions of this subdivision, a participant or beneficiary, who would have been required to receive required minimum distributions in 2020 (or paid in 2021 for the 2020 calendar year for a participant with a required beginning date of April 1, 2021) but for the enactment of section 401(a)(9)(I) of the Internal Revenue Code, and who would have satisfied that requirement by receiving a distribution that satisfies the required minimum distribution for 2020, will receive that distribution unless the participant or beneficiary chooses not to receive the distribution. Solely for purposes of applying the direct rollover provisions of section <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/statutes/cite/356.633">356.633</a>, such distributions will be treated as eligible rollover distributions in 2020.</p>
        </div>
        <div class="subd" id="stat.356.635.3">
          <a title="Link to Subd. 3." class="permalink" href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/statutes/2024/cite/356/full#stat.356.635.3">§</a>
          <h2 class="subd_no">Subd. 3.</h2>
          <p>MS 2022 [Repealed, <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/laws/2024/0/102/#laws.8.16.0">2024 c 102 art 8 s 16</a>]</p>
        </div>
        <div class="subd" id="stat.356.635.4">
          <a title="Link to Subd. 4." class="permalink" href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/statutes/2024/cite/356/full#stat.356.635.4">§</a>
          <h2 class="subd_no">Subd. 4.</h2>
          <p>MS 2022 [Repealed, <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/laws/2024/0/102/#laws.8.16.0">2024 c 102 art 8 s 16</a>]</p>
        </div>
        <div class="subd" id="stat.356.635.5">
          <a title="Link to Subd. 5." class="permalink" href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/statutes/2024/cite/356/full#stat.356.635.5">§</a>
          <h2 class="subd_no">Subd. 5.</h2>
          <p>MS 2022 [Repealed, <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/laws/2024/0/102/#laws.8.16.0">2024 c 102 art 8 s 16</a>]</p>
        </div>
        <div class="subd" id="stat.356.635.6">
          <a title="Link to Subd. 6." class="permalink" href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/statutes/2024/cite/356/full#stat.356.635.6">§</a>
          <h2 class="subd_no">Subd. 6.</h2>
          <p>MS 2022 [Repealed, <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/laws/2024/0/102/#laws.8.16.0">2024 c 102 art 8 s 16</a>]</p>
        </div>
        <div class="subd" id="stat.356.635.7">
          <a title="Link to Subd. 7." class="permalink" href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/statutes/2024/cite/356/full#stat.356.635.7">§</a>
          <h2 class="subd_no">Subd. 7.</h2>
          <p>MS 2022 [Repealed, <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/laws/2024/0/102/#laws.8.16.0">2024 c 102 art 8 s 16</a>]</p>
        </div>
        <div class="subd" id="stat.356.635.8">
          <a title="Link to Subd. 8." class="permalink" href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/statutes/2024/cite/356/full#stat.356.635.8">§</a>
          <h2 class="subd_no">Subd. 8.</h2>
          <p>MS 2022 [Repealed, <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/laws/2024/0/102/#laws.8.16.0">2024 c 102 art 8 s 16</a>]</p>
        </div>
        <div class="subd" id="stat.356.635.9">
          <a title="Link to Subd. 9." class="permalink" href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/statutes/2024/cite/356/full#stat.356.635.9">§</a>
          <h2 class="subd_no">Subd. 9.<span class="headnote">Military service.</span></h2>
          <p>Contributions, benefits, including death and disability benefits under section 401(a)(37) of the federal Internal Revenue Code, and service credit with respect to qualified military service must be provided according to section 414(u) of the federal Internal Revenue Code. For deaths occurring on or after January 1, 2007, while a member is performing qualified military service as defined in United States Code, title 38, chapter 43, to the extent required by section 401(a)(37) of the Internal Revenue Code, survivors of a member in the system are entitled to any additional benefits that the system would have provided if the member had resumed employment and then died, including but not limited to accelerated vesting or survivor benefits that are contingent on the member's death while employed. In any event, a deceased member's period of qualified military service must be counted for vesting purposes.</p>
        </div>
        <div class="subd" id="stat.356.635.9a">
          <a title="Link to Subd. 9a." class="permalink" href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/statutes/2024/cite/356/full#stat.356.635.9a">§</a>
          <h2 class="subd_no">Subd. 9a.</h2>
          <p>MS 2022 [Repealed, <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/laws/2024/0/102/#laws.8.16.0">2024 c 102 art 8 s 16</a>]</p>
        </div>
        <div class="subd" id="stat.356.635.10">
          <a title="Link to Subd. 10." class="permalink" href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/statutes/2024/cite/356/full#stat.356.635.10">§</a>
          <h2 class="subd_no">Subd. 10.</h2>
          <p>MS 2022 [Repealed, <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/laws/2024/0/102/#laws.8.16.0">2024 c 102 art 8 s 16</a>]</p>
        </div>
        <div class="subd" id="stat.356.635.11">
          <a title="Link to Subd. 11." class="permalink" href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/statutes/2024/cite/356/full#stat.356.635.11">§</a>
          <h2 class="subd_no">Subd. 11.</h2>
          <p>MS 2022 [Repealed, <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/laws/2024/0/102/#laws.8.16.0">2024 c 102 art 8 s 16</a>]</p>
        </div>
        <div class="subd" id="stat.356.635.12">
          <a title="Link to Subd. 12." class="permalink" href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/statutes/2024/cite/356/full#stat.356.635.12">§</a>
          <h2 class="subd_no">Subd. 12.</h2>
          <p>MS 2022 [Repealed, <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/laws/2024/0/102/#laws.8.16.0">2024 c 102 art 8 s 16</a>]</p>
        </div>
        <div class="subd" id="stat.356.635.13">
          <a title="Link to Subd. 13." class="permalink" href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/statutes/2024/cite/356/full#stat.356.635.13">§</a>
          <h2 class="subd_no">Subd. 13.</h2>
          <p>MS 2022 [Repealed, <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/laws/2024/0/102/#laws.8.16.0">2024 c 102 art 8 s 16</a>]</p>
